# Pinzo Pricing Strategy — Competitive Analysis & Recommendations

## Context
Pre-launch strategic review of Pinzo's pricing tiers. The goal is to ensure each feature is placed in the right plan tier based on competitor analysis, SaaS best practices, and merchant psychology. This is about strategic positioning, not code changes.

---

## Current Pinzo Plans

| | Free ($0) | Starter ($4.99/mo) | Pro ($9.99/mo) | Ultimate ($19.99/mo) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ZIP codes | 20 | 500 | Unlimited | Unlimited |
| Blocked ZIPs | - | - | Yes | Yes |
| Delivery rules | - | 3 | Unlimited | Unlimited |
| Waitlist | - | 25 | Unlimited | Unlimited |
| Widget customization | - | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| ETA / COD / Return | - | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Zone organization | - | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| CSV import | - | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| CSV export | - | - | Yes | Yes |
| Cart blocking | - | - | Yes | Yes |
| Custom CSS | - | - | - | Yes |
| API access | - | - | - | Yes |

---

## Competitive Landscape Summary

**Direct competitors** (ZIP/pincode checkers): SB Zipcode ($1.99 flat), Zipprover (free+paid), Zippy (free+paid), Smart Zipcode (free). Most are very basic — single-feature, single-tier, $0-$2.

**Adjacent competitors** (delivery zones/rates): Zapiet ($14-$30+ range), Postcode Shipping, MapIt. These are more feature-rich and charge $10-$40/mo.

**Key insight**: Most ZIP checker apps are commoditized at $0-$2. Pinzo differentiates by combining ZIP checking + delivery rules + waitlist + cart blocking into one app. This "all-in-one" positioning justifies higher pricing than pure checkers, but the tiers need to reflect real merchant workflows.

---

## Strategic Issues Found

### ISSUE 1: Blocked ZIP codes locked behind Pro ($9.99) — should be Starter

**Problem**: Blocking is the flip side of allowing. A pizza shop that delivers to 50 ZIPs also needs to block AK/HI/APO addresses. Forcing them to pay $9.99/mo for basic "we don't ship here" is a friction point.

**Competitor evidence**: Every competitor that offers blocking includes it at their lowest paid tier.

**Recommendation**: Move `allowBlocked: true` to Starter tier.

**Impact**: Reduces a major upgrade objection. Merchants who only need allow + block + 500 ZIPs won't feel forced into Pro.

---

### ISSUE 2: Free plan widget is too basic to convert users

**Problem**: Free plan has zero widget customization — no color changes, no text changes, no ETA. The widget looks generic and branded, which makes merchants uninstall rather than upgrade.

**SaaS best practice**: Free tier should be good enough to generate value. Merchants who see the widget working (even with basic colors) are far more likely to upgrade for advanced features.

**Recommendation**: Give Free plan basic text customization (heading, button text, messages) but keep colors/position/advanced features in Starter. This makes the free widget usable while still giving Starter a clear upgrade hook (colors, ETA, COD, zones).

**Implementation**: Split `widgetFullCustom` into two flags:
- `widgetBasicCustom` (Free+): heading, placeholder, buttonText, messages
- `widgetFullCustom` (Starter+): colors, position, border radius, etc.

---

### ISSUE 3: Waitlist limit of 25 on Starter is too aggressive

**Problem**: 25 entries fills up quickly if a store has even moderate traffic to non-served areas. Once full, the merchant gets upgrade pressure — but it feels punitive rather than natural.

**Recommendation**: Increase Starter waitlist to **50 entries**. Still creates natural upgrade pressure at scale, but doesn't feel artificially restrictive for small stores.

---

### ISSUE 4: Ultimate plan ($19.99) has weak value proposition

**Problem**: The jump from Pro ($9.99) to Ultimate ($19.99) doubles the price for only: Custom CSS + API access + VIP support + early access. Custom CSS is niche (most merchants never write CSS). API access is developer-only. This makes Ultimate feel like "pay more for things I'll never use."

**Recommendation**: Add tangible business-value features to Ultimate:
- **Analytics dashboard** — ZIP check volume, top requested ZIPs, conversion metrics
- **White-label** — remove "Powered by Pinzo" branding
- **Webhook notifications** — real-time alerts to Slack/Zapier when customers join waitlist
- **Multi-store support** — manage multiple Shopify stores from one dashboard
- **Priority support** is already there (24/7 VIP) — this is good

These are future roadmap items, but adding even 1-2 before launch (analytics being the easiest) would significantly strengthen the tier.

---

### ISSUE 5: Zone organization placement is correct

**Status**: Zone organization in Starter is correctly placed. Small stores (free) don't need zones. Growing stores naturally organize by area.

**No change needed.**

---

### ISSUE 6: Cart blocking correctly in Pro

**Status**: Cart & checkout blocking is a conversion-optimization feature for growing stores. It prevents orders from unserviceable areas before checkout. This is a high-value feature that justifies the Pro price.

**No change needed.**

---

### ISSUE 7: CSV export correctly in Pro

**Status**: Data export is a standard SaaS paid gate. Free/Starter users can import data; exporting (for migration/analysis) is a growth feature.

**No change needed.**

---

## Recommended New Plan Structure

| | Free ($0) | Starter ($4.99) | Pro ($9.99) | Ultimate ($19.99) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ZIP codes | 20 | 500 | Unlimited | Unlimited |
| **Blocked ZIPs** | - | **Yes** (moved from Pro) | Yes | Yes |
| Delivery rules | - | 3 | Unlimited | Unlimited |
| Waitlist | - | **50** (was 25) | Unlimited | Unlimited |
| **Basic widget text** | **Yes** (new) | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Full widget customization | - | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| ETA / COD / Return | - | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Zone organization | - | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| CSV import | - | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| CSV export | - | - | Yes | Yes |
| Cart blocking | - | - | Yes | Yes |
| Custom CSS | - | - | - | Yes |
| API access | - | - | - | Yes |
| **Analytics** | - | - | - | **Yes** (new, future) |
| **Remove branding** | - | - | - | **Yes** (new, future) |

### Changes summary:
1. **Blocked ZIPs**: Pro → Starter
2. **Waitlist**: 25 → 50 on Starter
3. **Free widget text customization**: New — let free users change heading/button/messages
4. **Ultimate value add** (future roadmap): Analytics dashboard, remove branding

---

## Implementation — Code Changes Required

### Change 1: Move blocked ZIPs to Starter
**File**: `app/plans.ts`
```
starter.allowBlocked: false → true
```

### Change 2: Increase Starter waitlist limit
**File**: `app/plans.ts`
```
starter.maxWaitlist: 25 → 50
```

### Change 3: Add basic widget text customization for Free
**File**: `app/plans.ts`
- Add new field: `widgetBasicCustom: boolean`
- Free: `widgetBasicCustom: true, widgetFullCustom: false`
- Starter+: `widgetBasicCustom: true, widgetFullCustom: true`

**File**: `app/routes/app.widget.tsx`
- Text fields (heading, placeholder, buttonText, messages) → enabled when `widgetBasicCustom`
- Color/position/border fields → enabled when `widgetFullCustom` (existing behavior)

**File**: `app/routes/api.widget-config.tsx`
- Enforce: when `!widgetFullCustom`, strip colors to defaults but keep custom text

### Change 4: Update pricing page copy
**File**: `app/routes/app.pricing.tsx`
- Update Starter card bullet: add "Allowed + Blocked zip codes"
- Update Starter waitlist: "Waitlist (50 entries)"
- Update Free card: add "Basic widget text customization"
- Update feature comparison table rows

### Change 5: Update downgrade modal
**File**: `app/routes/app.pricing.tsx`
- Remove "blocked zip codes" from Pro→Starter downgrade warning (since it's now in Starter)

---

## Verification
1. `npm run typecheck` — 0 errors
2. Check pricing page renders correct new feature bullets
3. Check free plan user can edit widget heading/button text but NOT colors/position
4. Check Starter plan user can use blocked ZIP codes
5. Check feature comparison table matches new structure

---

## Pricing Amounts — No Changes Recommended

The price points ($0 / $4.99 / $9.99 / $19.99) are well-positioned:
- **$4.99 Starter** undercuts most competitors while offering more features
- **$9.99 Pro** is competitive for "unlimited everything" in this category
- **$19.99 Ultimate** needs value-add features (above) but the price point itself is fine
- **Annual discounts** (up to 38%) are standard and well-communicated
- **7-day free trial** is appropriate for the price range
# Landing Page Production-Grade Enhancement Plan

## Context
We just rebuilt the ConvertScan landing page from an 811-line monolith into 10 modular components with framer-motion animations, dark hero, bento grid, browser mockups, etc. Two deep audits (technical + design) revealed **no broken functionality** but surfaced polish, accessibility, and missing premium elements that separate a good landing page from a brand-level one.

---

## Phase 1: Accessibility & Motion Safety (Critical)

### 1.1 — Add `prefers-reduced-motion` support
**Files:** `Hero.tsx`, `FinalCTA.tsx`, `src/index.css`
- Add CSS rule in `index.css`:
  ```css
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .animate-ping { animation: none !important; }
  }
  ```
- `FinalCTA.tsx`: Add `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` check to the inline `<style>` shimmer keyframe — disable animation or set `animation: none`
- Use framer-motion's `useReducedMotion()` hook in components with stagger/fadeUp to disable animations when user prefers reduced motion

### 1.2 — Keyboard focus states on interactive cards
**Files:** `Findings.tsx`, `Pillars.tsx`, `Testimonials.tsx`
- Add `focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary/30 focus-visible:outline-none` and `tabIndex={0}` to cards that have `whileHover` animations
- Add matching `whileFocus` to framer-motion cards

### 1.3 — Semantic HTML fixes
**Files:** `Testimonials.tsx`, `HowItWorks.tsx`, `Pillars.tsx`
- Testimonials: Wrap quotes in `<blockquote>` instead of `<p>`
- HowItWorks: Add `aria-hidden="true"` to decorative connecting line
- Pillars: Remove `cursor-default` from cards (confusing with hover state) or remove hover if non-interactive

---

## Phase 2: Mobile Polish

### 2.1 — Hero browser mockup mobile optimization
**File:** `Hero.tsx`
- On mobile (below `lg`), simplify the browser mockup content:
  - Hide the detailed findings list on small screens (`hidden sm:block`)
  - Keep ScoreRing + URL + pillar bars visible (they're the visual hook)
- Add `mt-10 sm:mt-16` instead of fixed `mt-16` for tighter mobile spacing

### 2.2 — Stats strip responsive
**File:** `Hero.tsx`
- Stats strip values (`2,400+`, `7`, `40+`) — add animated counter on scroll into view using framer-motion's `useSpring` + `useInView`

---

## Phase 3: Missing Premium Sections

### 3.1 — Add FAQ section
**New file:** `src/components/landing/FAQ.tsx`
- Position: After Testimonials, before FinalCTA
- Use shadcn Accordion component (`@/components/ui/accordion`)
- 6-8 common questions:
  1. "What exactly does ConvertScan check?"
  2. "How long does an audit take?"
  3. "Do I need to install anything?"
  4. "Is it really free?"
  5. "What makes this different from Google PageSpeed?"
  6. "Can I use this for client sites?"
  7. "How accurate is the AI analysis?"
  8. "Can I export or share reports?"
- Left-aligned section header (like Findings), scroll-triggered animation
- Light background (bg-white), clean borders

### 3.2 — Enhance LogoBar with stronger social proof
**File:** `LogoBar.tsx`
- Replace generic text pills with more specific trust indicators:
  - "2,400+ pages audited" | "500+ teams" | "94% find issues on first scan"
  - Use small stat numbers with labels in a horizontal strip
- Add subtle border/dividers between stats
- This is more compelling than category labels

### 3.3 — Add animated counters to Hero stats
**File:** `Hero.tsx`
- Animate the stats strip numbers (2,400+, 7, 40+) counting up from 0 when they scroll into view
- Use framer-motion `useSpring` + `useMotionValue` + `useTransform` for smooth counter

---

## Phase 4: Visual Polish

### 4.1 — Section heading variety
**Files:** `HowItWorks.tsx`, `Testimonials.tsx`
- HowItWorks currently has centered header — keep centered (it works for 3-step layout)
- Testimonials: Make header left-aligned to break centered monotony, OR add a subtle accent element (thin primary-colored line above the label)

### 4.2 — Pillar grid fix
**File:** `Pillars.tsx`
- Remove the empty `<div className="hidden lg:block" />` hack
- Change grid to `lg:grid-cols-3` (3+3+1) or keep `lg:grid-cols-4` but let the 7th item center itself with `col-start-2` or similar

### 4.3 — FinalCTA differentiation
**File:** `FinalCTA.tsx`
- The FinalCTA feels identical to Hero (same UrlInput, same dark background, same copy pattern)
- Differentiate: Add a small "3 free scans, no account needed" badge above the input, or add mini testimonial quote below

---

## Phase 5: Update Landing.tsx composition

**File:** `src/pages/Landing.tsx`
- Add FAQ import and place between Testimonials and FinalCTA:
```tsx
<Testimonials />
<FAQ />
<FinalCTA />
```

---

# Full UI/UX Audit — Production-Grade Fixes

## Audit Summary
Audited: Dashboard, Sidebar, JobDetailView, CandidateRow, ScoreSummaryCards, Settings (5 cards), Auth, LandingPage, BookDemo, RequestFeature, Admin (8 tabs). Found 40+ issues across security, accessibility, UX, and code quality.

## Priority Tiers

### Tier 1 — Fix Now (Security + Broken Pages)

| # | Issue | File | Fix |
|---|-------|------|-----|
| 1 | Hardcoded Supabase URL in fetch calls | `JobDetailView.tsx:224,430` | Replace with `supabase.functions.invoke()` |
| 2 | Hardcoded webhook URL | `EmailSettingsTab.tsx:24` | Build from `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` env var |
| 3 | `console.error` left in production | `Index.tsx:94,169`, `PlansTab.tsx:179,223,245` | Remove or replace with toast |
| 4 | RequestFeature page is a stub | `RequestFeature.tsx` | Build proper feature request form |

### Tier 2 — UX Polish (Most Visible)

| # | Issue | File | Fix |
|---|-------|------|-----|
| 5 | Settings cards inconsistent headers | All 5 settings cards | Standardize to same header pattern |
| 6 | Missing `aria-label` on icon buttons | `CandidateRow.tsx`, `UsersTab.tsx` | Add aria-labels |
| 7 | ConfigTab state init during render | `ConfigTab.tsx:29-38` | Move to `useEffect` |
| 8 | No input validation in admin edit user | `UsersTab.tsx:117-146` | Add NaN/negative checks |
| 9 | AuthForm missing confirm password | `AuthForm.tsx` | Add confirm password field |
| 10 | BookDemo no error state | `BookDemo.tsx` | Add error fallback |

### Tier 3 — Consistency (Polish)

| # | Issue | Files | Fix |
|---|-------|-------|-----|
| 11 | Inconsistent loading states across tables | Admin tabs | Could create shared TableSkeleton |
| 12 | `as any` type casting (25+ instances) | Admin tabs | Requires types.ts update — skip for now |
| 13 | Sidebar animation inline styles | `AdminSidebar.tsx:60` | Move to Tailwind classes |
| 14 | Hardcoded page sizes vary | Multiple admin tabs | Standardize to 25 |

---

## Implementation Plan (Tier 1 + Tier 2 only)

### 1. Fix hardcoded Supabase URLs in JobDetailView
Replace raw `fetch()` calls with `supabase.functions.invoke()` which already uses the configured URL.

### 2. Fix hardcoded webhook URL in EmailSettingsTab
```typescript
const WEBHOOK_URL = `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/email-ingest`;
```

### 3. Remove console.error statements
- `Index.tsx:94` → add `toast.error("Failed to load jobs")`
- `Index.tsx:169` → already has toast, just remove console.error
- `PlansTab.tsx:179,223,245` → remove console.error lines

### 4. Build RequestFeature page
Simple form: title input, description textarea, submit button. Store in a `feature_requests` table or send via email/webhook.

### 5. Standardize settings card headers
All 5 cards use custom flex divs with icon + title + subtitle. They're already consistent visually (per screenshots earlier). Just ensure icon sizes match (standardize to `h-4.5 w-4.5`).

### 6. Add aria-labels to icon buttons
CandidateRow action buttons: phone, email, download, archive. UsersTab: view activity, edit user.

### 7. Fix ConfigTab state initialization
Move `setForm()` from render to `useEffect`.

### 8. Add validation to admin edit user credits
Check `isNaN`, negative values before save.

### 9. Add confirm password to AuthForm signup
Add matching password field with validation.

### 10. Add BookDemo error state
Show error message if platform config fails to load.

## Verification
- `npm run build` — no errors
- Grep for `console.error` and `console.log` — none remaining
- Grep for hardcoded Supabase project ID — none remaining
- Visual check of all modified pages
# Live Preview Redesign — Competitor-Inspired, Modern Widget Preview

## Context
The current preview is flat and basic. The user shared 5 competitor screenshots showing modern delivery checker widgets. This redesign incorporates the best patterns from all of them into a premium, high-converting preview.

## Competitor Design Patterns Observed

| Pattern | Seen In | Our Implementation |
|---|---|---|
| Emoji/illustrations in results (🚚 😞 ✅) | Images 1, 3, 5 | Use emoji delivery truck, checkmark, sad face in result cards |
| Countdown timer ("Order within 3h 35m") | Images 1, 5 | Show dynamic countdown in success state |
| Specific delivery date ("Get it by Friday, Feb 16") | Images 1, 2, 5 | Show estimated delivery date (not just "2-3 days") |
| Delivery timeline stepper (Order → Ship → Delivered) | Image 2 | Add 3-step visual timeline in success state |
| Bold keyword highlighting | Images 1, 3, 5 | Bold key words in messages: dates, times, ZIP codes |
| "Change" link after checking | Image 5 | Show "Change" to re-enter ZIP after success |
| Clean rounded pill input | Images 1, 3, 4 | Pill-shaped input with generous padding |
| Dark/branded button with rounded corners | Image 4 | Rounded button with brand color, good contrast |
| Sad/happy emoji in result | Images 1, 3 | Add expressive emoji/icon in result area |
| Color-coded result backgrounds | Images 1, 3 | Green-tinted success bg, pink/red-tinted error bg |
| "Returns available" info line with icon | Image 5 | Show returns info with arrow icon |

## Target File
`/Users/yashbaldha/Desktop/Shopify App/Zip-Code/app/routes/app.widget.tsx`

## Implementation Plan

### Step 1: Redesign Widget CSS (`buildWidgetCss` + `buildSharedMetaCss`)

**Search Bar — Pill style (inspired by images 1, 3, 4):**
- Full pill radius: `border-radius: 50px`
- Soft background: `#f8f9fa` (not pure white)
- Subtle border: `1px solid #e8e8e8`
- Inner shadow: `inset 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)`
- Focus glow: `box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px ${primaryColor}15`
- Generous padding: `14px 20px` on input

**Button — Branded rounded (inspired by image 4):**
- Rounded: `border-radius: 0 50px 50px 0`
- Brand color with hover brightness
- Shadow: `0 2px 8px ${primaryColor}25`
- Hover: `translateY(-1px)` lift + deeper shadow
- Bold uppercase text: `font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.5px; font-size: 13px`

**Success Result Card (inspired by images 1, 2, 5):**
- Light green tint background: `${successColor}08`
- Full `1px solid ${successColor}18` border, `border-radius: 14px`
- Layout: icon/emoji on right side (like images 1, 3), text on left
- **Delivery date line** (bold): "Get it by **Thursday, Mar 19**"
- **Countdown line**: "Order within **5h 30m** for same-day dispatch"
- **Returns line**: "↩ Returns available for this zipcode"
- Each meta item with emoji prefix: 🚚 for ETA, 📅 for days, ⏰ for cutoff, 💰 for COD

**Error Result Card (inspired by image 3):**
- Light pink/red tint: `${errorColor}08`
- Sad emoji on right side (😞 styled as subtle illustration)
- Bold "Sorry," prefix in error message
- Waitlist form appears below with friendly copy

**Delivery Timeline (inspired by image 2):**
- 3-step horizontal stepper shown in success state when ETA is enabled
- Steps: "Order Now" → "Ready to Ship" → "Delivered"
- Connected by a line, active steps colored in primaryColor
- Each step has a small circular icon above and date below
- Light primaryColor tinted background strip

**Post-check "Delivering to" state (inspired by image 5):**
- After success: show "✅ Delivering to **99501**" with "Change" link
- Clean list of info items below, each with emoji + bold key value
- This replaces the input bar in the success view

### Step 2: Update Widget HTML Structure (`WidgetPreview`)

**Idle state (default):**
```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  📍 Check Delivery Availability         │
│                                          │
│  ┌──────────────────────┬──────────┐    │
│  │ Enter your zip code  │  CHECK   │    │
│  └──────────────────────┴──────────┘    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Success state:**
```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  📍 Delivery Options                     │
│                                          │
│  ✅ Delivering to 10001        Change   │
│                                          │
│  ┌─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ┐  │
│  │ 🚚  Get it by Thu, Mar 19        │  │
│  │ ⏰  Order within 5h 30m          │  │
│  │ 📅  Mon · Tue · Wed · Thu · Fri  │  │
│  │ 💰  Cash on Delivery Available   │  │
│  │ ↩   30-day returns accepted      │  │
│  └─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ─ ┘  │
│                                          │
│  ┌─────────┬──────────┬────────────┐    │
│  │ 🛍 Order│ 🚛 Ship  │ 📦 Deliver │    │
│  │ Mar 18  │ Mar 19   │ Mar 21     │    │
│  └─────────┴──────────┴────────────┘    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Error state:**

# Plan: Complete UI/UX Audit & Fix — Install Page

## Context
The install page at `app/routes/_index/route.tsx` uses Polaris v13.9.5 but has structural, responsive, and branding inconsistencies. The goal is a single comprehensive rewrite that makes the page fully Polaris-native, pixel-perfect, and responsive with zero bugs.

## Critical File
`app/routes/_index/route.tsx` — only file to edit
`app/routes/_index/styles.module.css` — delete (unused dead code)

---

## Complete Issue List (All to fix in one pass)

### 1. Raw `div` wrappers — replace with Polaris `Box`
- **Header**: raw `div` with inline `background` + `borderBottom` → `Box` with `background="bg-surface"`, `borderBlockEndWidth="025"`, `borderColor="border"`
- **Main content**: raw `div` with inline `flex:1 / maxWidth / padding` → `Box` using padding tokens
- **Hero wrapper**: raw `div` with `textAlign:"center"` + `paddingBottom:"3rem"` → `Box paddingBlockEnd="1600"` (or "800")
- **Subheading constraint**: raw `div maxWidth:600px` → `Box maxWidth` inline style kept minimal, or use a constrained Box

### 2. Hardcoded CSS values → Polaris space tokens
- `padding: "3rem 2rem 4rem"` → `paddingBlockStart="800" paddingBlockEnd="1200" paddingInlineStart="600" paddingInlineEnd="600"`
- `paddingBottom: "3rem"` → `paddingBlockEnd="1200"`
- `width: "32px/44px"` icon containers → keep as pixel values (icons need explicit sizing)

### 3. Responsive grid — mobile cards are cramped
- Current: `xs: 6` → 2 cards per row on small phones (too tight)
- Fix: `xs: 12, sm: 6, md: 4, lg: 4, xl: 4` → 1 per row on mobile, 2 on tablet, 3 on desktop

### 4. Form responsive — breaks on narrow screens
- Current: `InlineStack wrap={false}` → TextField + Button never wrap, causes overflow on small screens
- Fix: Wrap the form in a `BlockStack` and use `InlineStack wrap={true}` with the Button having `fullWidth` logic, OR change to `BlockStack gap="300"` on mobile (use CSS variable approach via a wrapping div with media query, or conditionally use BlockStack)
- Simplest correct fix: Remove `wrap={false}`, let it wrap naturally, and style the Button to be `size="large"` with enough min-width

### 5. Feature card equal heights
- Cards with different text lengths render at different heights in the grid row
- Fix: Wrap each `Grid.Cell` content in a flex column `div` with `height: "100%"`, and give `Card` `height="100%"` via a wrapping Box

### 6. Header max-width alignment
- Header content left-aligns to edge while main content has 1100px max-width → header logo appears disconnected
- Fix: Add a constrained inner wrapper inside header that matches the main max-width, OR just let the header span full width with consistent padding (full-width header is standard Shopify style — keep, just ensure padding matches)

### 7. Typography hierarchy
- H1 `heading3xl` is appropriate for desktop but the hero section has no semantic section wrapper
- Badge "SHOPIFY APP" — all caps is fine, already styled by Polaris Badge
- Heading green span uses raw inline style — keep (no Polaris alternative for inline colored text in headings)

### 8. Accessibility
- Wrap main content in `<main>` semantic landmark
- Form already has `name="shop"` and `autoComplete="off"` ✓
- TextField has `labelHidden` with proper `label` → screen reader accessible ✓

### 9. Footer
- `Box background="bg-surface"` ✓ valid in Polaris v13
- Add `borderBlockStartWidth="025"` to Box instead of separate `<Divider />` (removes extra component)

### 10. Remove unused dead code
- Delete `app/routes/_index/styles.module.css` (no longer imported anywhere)

---

## Final Structure After Fix

```
AppProvider
└── Box (minHeight 100dvh, bg, flex column)
    ├── Box as header (bg-surface, border-bottom, full-width)
    │   └── Box (padding, max-width inner, constrained)
    │       └── InlineStack (icon + brand name)
    ├── Box as main (flex:1, max-width 1100px, centered, padding tokens)
    │   ├── Box as hero (paddingBlockEnd, center-aligned)
    │   │   └── BlockStack (badge → h1 → subtext → form)
    │   └── Grid (xs:12, sm:6, md:4 columnSpan)
    │       └── Grid.Cell × 6
    │           └── Box (height 100%)
    │               └── Card
    │                   └── BlockStack (icon container → title → desc)
    └── Box as footer (bg-surface, border-top, padding)
        └── Text (centered, subdued)
```

---

## Verification
1. `npm run build` — must succeed with 0 errors
2. Kill old server, restart: `kill $(lsof -ti :3000) && react-router-serve ./build/server/index.js`
3. Visit `http://localhost:3000/` — verify:
   - Mobile 390px: 1 card per row, form stacks vertically, text is readable
   - Tablet 768px: 2 cards per row
   - Desktop 1280px: 3 cards per row, hero centered, header brand aligned
   - No raw pixel-based hardcoded spacing (only Polaris tokens)
   - No emojis, all Polaris Icons with green rounded containers
   - Equal height cards in each row
# Fix Orchestration Page — Full Functionality & UX

## Context
The Orchestration page in Claude Hub has a visual DAG builder with agent palette, ReactFlow canvas, and run panel. While the structure is in place, the end-to-end flow is broken: start nodes waste Claude calls, the Run button is confusing, node outputs can't be inspected, and several UX issues make the page feel incomplete. Goal: make it fully functional and polished.

## Files to Modify
- `client/src/pages/Orchestration.tsx` (832 lines — main page)
- `src/server.js` (lines 738-895 — orchestration backend)

---

## Phase 1: Backend Fixes (server.js)

### 1A. Skip Start Nodes in Execution (lines 852-858)
After `if (!node) continue;`, add guard:
```js
if (node.data?.isStart) {
  results[nodeId] = task;
  send({ type: 'done', nodeId, label: node.data?.label || nodeId, output: task, skipped: true });
  continue;
}
```
**Why:** Start/task-input nodes currently waste a Claude CLI call just to parrot the task back.

### 1B. Add Timeout to runClaudeSync (lines 803-819)
Add a `timeoutMs = 120000` parameter. Set a `setTimeout` to kill the child process if it exceeds the limit. Clear timer on close.
**Why:** A hung Claude process blocks the entire pipeline forever with no recourse.

---

## Phase 2: SSE & Event Processing Fix (Orchestration.tsx)

### 2A. Extract processSSEEvent helper
Create a `useCallback` function `processSSEEvent(event)` that handles `setRunLog` + `setNodes` updates for start/done/error events. Reuse in both the streaming loop (lines 362-384) and the flush (lines 388-395).

### 2B. Fix SSE buffer flush (lines 388-395)
Replace single-event flush with multi-line processing. Current code only handles one remaining event; multiple events could be buffered.

---

## Phase 3: Core Flow Fixes (Orchestration.tsx)

### 3A. Fix Run Button Behavior (lines 511-518)
Change from toggle (`setShowRunPanel(!showRunPanel)`) to progressive:
- First click: open run panel
- If panel open & task filled: run immediately
- If already running: just show panel
Show contextual label: "Run" / "Run Now" / "Running..."

### 3B. Fix Node Inspection During/After Run
- Change `showNodeEdit = !!selectedNode && !showRunPanel` to allow inspection
- Add `viewingOutputNodeId` state
- Modify `onNodeClick`: during run, clicking a completed/errored node opens output viewer
- When run panel is NOT open, clicking a node opens the edit panel as before

### 3C. Add Full Output Viewer Modal
New modal triggered by clicking completed nodes or "View full output" in run log. Shows full pre-formatted output with copy button. Similar pattern to existing Clear/Templates modals.

### 3D. Post-Run Controls (run panel lines 671-687)
After run completes (`!running && runLog.length > 0`), replace "Start Run" with:
- "Re-run" button (same task, re-executes)
- "Reset" button (clears outputs, resets node statuses)

---

## Phase 4: UX Polish (Orchestration.tsx)

### 4A. Delete Key on Mac (line 561)
Change `deleteKeyCode="Delete"` to `deleteKeyCode={["Backspace", "Delete"]}`

### 4B. "New Orchestration" Button
Add `newOrchestration()` function (resets all state including name/task/runLog). Add "New" button to toolbar before Clear button.

### 4C. Execution Order Numbers on Nodes
- Add `useMemo` to compute topological order → `executionOrder` map
- Create `nodesWithOrder` that injects `executionOrder` into node data
- Pass `nodesWithOrder` to `<ReactFlow nodes={...}>`
- Render numbered badge on each node (purple circle, top-left corner)

### 4D. Validate Disconnected Nodes Before Run (handleRun)
Check if any nodes have no edges when there are multiple nodes. Show toast error with disconnected node names.

### 4E. Node Count in Saved List (line 816)
Show `{o.nodes?.length} nodes` alongside the date in the saved orchestrations modal.

### 4F. Warn Before Template Replaces Canvas (loadTemplate)
Add `window.confirm()` if canvas has existing nodes before replacing.

---

## Implementation Order
1. Backend fixes (1A, 1B) — independent, fixes critical waste
2. SSE fix (2A, 2B) — establishes helper for subsequent changes
3. Core flow (3A, 3B, 3C, 3D) — main UX improvements
4. Polish (4A-4F) — independent, can be done in any order

## Verification
1. Start dev server: `cd client && npm run dev` + `node src/server.js`
2. Open http://localhost:5173/orchestration
3. Test: Load template → verify execution order badges appear
4. Test: Click Run → verify panel opens with task input
5. Test: Enter task + click Run/Start Run → verify start nodes are skipped (instant "done"), other nodes execute through Claude
6. Test: After run, click completed node → verify output viewer modal opens
7. Test: Re-run and Reset buttons work after completion
8. Test: Delete key works on Mac
9. Test: Save/Load/Delete orchestrations
10. Test: Disconnected node validation fires when nodes aren't connected
# Rankora 5-Day Production Sprint Plan

## Context

Rankora is a resume ranking SaaS that is functional but has significant production risks across security, payment integrity, concurrency, error handling, and scoring consistency. This plan addresses 36 identified issues across 3 parallel audits, prioritized by blast radius and user impact. The goal is to move from "working prototype" to "production-hardened" in 5 days.

**Current Production-Readiness Score: 4/10**
**Target After Sprint: 7.5/10**

---

## DAY 1: Security + Critical Payment Bugs (6 tasks)

### 1.1 XSS Sanitization (CRITICAL)
- **Problem**: `ChatWidget.tsx:36` and `BookDemo.tsx` use `innerHTML` with DB-sourced HTML. Admin account compromise = XSS on every page.
- **Fix**: Install `dompurify`, sanitize all `innerHTML` calls.
- **Files**: `src/components/ChatWidget.tsx`, `src/pages/BookDemo.tsx`, `package.json`
- **Regression**: Verify chat widget + BookDemo embeds still render.

### 1.2 Open Redirect in Checkout (CRITICAL)
- **Problem**: `create-dodo-checkout` accepts arbitrary `return_url` without validation.
- **Fix**: Validate `return_url` origin against allowlist (`rankora.co`, `localhost:8080`).
- **File**: `supabase/functions/create-dodo-checkout/index.ts` (lines 140-143)
- **Regression**: Payment checkout redirect still works.

### 1.3 Double-Click Deduplication (CRITICAL)
- **Problem**: No guard on Rank button. Rapid clicks = duplicate jobs + double credit deduction.
- **Fix**: Add `rankingLockRef` guard in `handleRankClick`, release in `finally` blocks.
- **File**: `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx` (~line 306)
- **Regression**: Rapidly click Rank. Only one job created.

### 1.4 Subscription Cancellation Bug (CRITICAL)
- **Problem**: `handleDowngradeToPayg` ignores Dodo cancellation errors, downgrades locally anyway. User gets charged but thinks they cancelled.
- **Fix**: If Dodo cancellation fails, show error toast and `return` — don't downgrade profile.
- **File**: `src/components/SubscriptionDialog.tsx` (lines 125-145)
- **Regression**: Test downgrade. If Dodo is down, user sees error, profile unchanged.

### 1.5 Admin Credit Adjustment Audit Trail (HIGH)
- **Problem**: `admin-adjust-credits` modifies credits with zero audit logging.
- **Fix**: Insert into `admin_audit_logs` after credit update.
- **File**: `supabase/functions/admin-adjust-credits/index.ts`
- **Regression**: Admin adjusts credits → verify audit log row created.

### 1.6 CORS Restriction on send-email (HIGH)
- **Problem**: `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` on email function.
- **Fix**: Restrict to `https://rankora.co` (keep wildcard only for OPTIONS preflight if needed).
- **File**: `supabase/functions/send-email/index.ts` (line 6)
- **Regression**: Email sending from app still works.

---

## DAY 2: Core Flow Stability (5 tasks)

### 2.1 Polling Robustness (CRITICAL — 3 sub-fixes)
- **Problem A**: Poll iterations have no try-catch. One Supabase query failure kills entire polling.
- **Problem B**: Auth token can expire during 5-min polling. No refresh mechanism.
- **Problem C**: User can re-rank while job is still processing, creating duplicates.
- **Fix A**: Wrap each poll iteration in try-catch, `continue` on transient errors, throw on final iteration.
- **Fix B**: Call `supabase.auth.refreshSession()` every 5th poll iteration.
- **Fix C**: In `handleRankExisting`, check `selectedJob?.status === "processing"` and block with toast.
- **File**: `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx` (lines 306-312, 438-509)
- **Regression**: Simulate network blip mid-poll (DevTools offline toggle). Should recover. Long ranking should not get auth errors.

### 2.2 Resume Upload Partial Failure Recovery (HIGH)
- **Problem**: Sequential upload throws on first failure. Partial uploads leave orphaned state.
- **Fix**: Continue uploading remaining files on failure. Track successes/failures. Proceed to ranking if any succeeded. Show warning toast for failures.
- **File**: `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx` (upload function, ~lines 404-420)
- **Regression**: Upload 3 files, one corrupted. Two should rank. Warning shown.

### 2.3 Feature Flag Loading Safety (HIGH)
- **Problem**: `useFeatureFlag()` returns `enabled: false` before loading. Features flash disabled momentarily.
- **Fix**: Add `ready: !isLoading` to return value. Audit consumers to check `ready` before acting on `enabled`.
- **File**: `src/hooks/useFeatureFlags.tsx`
- **Regression**: Feature flags load correctly after refresh. No flash of wrong state.

### 2.4 Stale Profile Data (HIGH)
- **Problem**: Credit count only refreshes after ranking. Subscription renewals mid-session not reflected.
- **Fix**: Add `window.addEventListener("focus", fetchProfile)` + 2-minute periodic refresh in AuthProvider.
- **File**: `src/hooks/useAuth.tsx`
- **Regression**: Buy credits in one tab, switch to another — credits update within seconds.

### 2.5 Clear Transient State on Ranking Failure (MEDIUM)
- **Problem**: `selectedResumes` and `deleteConfirm` not cleared when ranking fails, leaving UI in inconsistent state.
- **Fix**: Add `setSelectedResumes(new Set()); setDeleteConfirm(null);` to catch/finally blocks.
- **File**: `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx` (catch blocks)

---

## DAY 3: Payment Safety + Scoring Consistency (6 tasks)

### 3.1 Atomic Credit Addition (CRITICAL)
- **Problem**: Both `verify-dodo-payment` and `dodo-webhook` do non-atomic read-then-write on `profiles.credits`. Concurrent payments can lose increments.
- **Fix**: Create `add_credits` PL/pgSQL function using `SET credits = credits + _amount`. Call via `supabase.rpc("add_credits", ...)` in both functions.
- **Files**: New migration for `add_credits` function, `supabase/functions/verify-dodo-payment/index.ts`, `supabase/functions/dodo-webhook/index.ts`
- **Regression**: Simulate concurrent payment processing. Credits correctly summed.

### 3.2 Subscription Renewal Resilience (HIGH)
- **Problem**: If plan name deleted from `plans` table, renewal webhook silently skips credit addition.
- **Fix**: Log critical error + extend subscription end date even if plan lookup fails.
- **File**: `supabase/functions/dodo-webhook/index.ts` (lines 214-240)

### 3.3 Hard Fit Cap Consistency (HIGH)
- **Problem**: Hard fit cap applied in GPT scoring path but uses hardcoded `49` in heuristic fallback instead of `cfg.hard_fit_cap`.
- **Fix**: Store `hardFitCap = cfg.hard_fit_cap ?? 49` and pass to all scoring paths.
- **File**: `supabase/functions/rank-resumes/index.ts`

### 3.4 OpenAI API Timeout (HIGH)
- **Problem**: Fetch calls to OpenAI have no timeout. Can hang indefinitely, creating zombie jobs stuck in "processing".
- **Fix**: Add `AbortController` with 60-second timeout to all OpenAI fetch calls.
- **Files**: `supabase/functions/rank-resumes/index.ts`, `supabase/functions/analyze-jd/index.ts`

### 3.5 JD Analysis Output Validation (HIGH)
- **Problem**: JSON parsed without schema validation. Missing `critical_must_haves`, `tools`, `soft_skills` etc. cause downstream crashes.
- **Fix**: Default all optional arrays to `[]` and validate `evaluation_dimensions` structure (each must have `key`, `label`, `rubric`).
- **File**: `supabase/functions/analyze-jd/index.ts` (~line 123)

### 3.6 WalletSettingsTab Silent Error (MEDIUM)
- **Problem**: `.catch(() => {})` swallows product verification errors.
- **Fix**: Set error state in catch block so admin sees the failure.
- **File**: `src/components/admin/WalletSettingsTab.tsx` (line 67)

---

## DAY 4: Performance + Testing (4 tasks)

### 4.1 Code Splitting (MEDIUM)
- **Problem**: 1,516 KB bundle. No lazy loading or chunk splitting.
- **Fix**: Lazy-load route components in `App.tsx` with `React.lazy()` + `Suspense`. Add `manualChunks` in Vite config for vendor/UI/charts/supabase.
- **Files**: `src/App.tsx`, `vite.config.ts`
- **Target**: Initial bundle under 500 KB.

### 4.2 CSS Import Order (LOW)
- **Problem**: `@import` after `@tailwind` causes build warning.
- **Fix**: Move `@import url(...)` before `@tailwind` directives.
- **File**: `src/index.css`

### 4.3 Weight Normalization Bug (MEDIUM)
- **Problem**: Post-normalization weights can exceed 0.50 constraint.
- **Fix**: Apply clamp-normalize cycle iteratively (max 3 iterations) until stable.
- **File**: `supabase/functions/analyze-jd/index.ts` (lines 164-182)

### 4.4 Write Core Tests (HIGH)
- **Problem**: ~15% test coverage. Zero tests for core ranking flow, credit validation, payment logic.
- **Fix**: Write unit tests for extractable pure logic:
  - Credit validation logic (extract from JobDetailView)
  - CSV export helper
  - Feature flag loading states
- **Files**: New test files in `src/test/`

---

## DAY 5: Hardening + Final Audit (4 tasks)

### 5.1 Empty States (LOW)
- **Problem**: `ScoreSummaryCards` returns `null` when empty. User sees blank space.
- **Fix**: Return informative empty state message.
- **File**: `src/components/ScoreSummaryCards.tsx`

### 5.2 Scoring Path Transparency (MEDIUM)
- **Problem**: Users can't tell if score came from GPT, rule-based, or heuristic fallback.
- **Fix**: Show subtle confidence indicator in `CandidateRow` when `scoring_version === "heuristic"`.
- **File**: `src/components/CandidateRow.tsx`

### 5.3 Full Build + Regression Test
- Run `npm run build` — verify zero errors
- Run `npm run test` — all tests pass
- Manual QA: create job, rank resumes, buy credits, subscribe, cancel, admin credit adjust, verify audit logs

### 5.4 Deploy Edge Functions
- Deploy order (based on dependencies):
  1. DB migration: `add_credits` function → `supabase db push`
  2. `analyze-jd` (timeout + validation)
  3. `rank-resumes` (timeout + hard_fit cap)
  4. `dodo-webhook` (atomic credits + renewal resilience)
  5. `verify-dodo-payment` (atomic credits)
  6. `create-dodo-checkout` (open redirect fix)
  7. `send-email` (CORS)
  8. `admin-adjust-credits` (audit trail)

---

## Deferred (Post-Sprint)

| Issue | Reason Deferred |
|-------|----------------|
| TypeScript `strictNullChecks` | Multi-day effort, high regression risk mid-sprint |
| Accessibility (aria-live, keyboard nav) | Multi-day effort, not blocking |
| Resume upload idempotency | Medium effort, low frequency issue |
| Payment record cleanup cron | Nice-to-have, not urgent |
| Ref-based state refactor in JobDetailView | Major refactor, too risky mid-sprint |
| Structured scoring decision logging | Valuable but not blocking |
| localStorage fallback handling | Edge case, low impact |

---

## Risk Register

| Risk | Mitigation |
|------|-----------|
| Payment flow changes break checkout | Test in Dodo sandbox before deploying. Deploy payment functions last. |
| Polling changes cause ranking to appear stuck | Keep existing timeout detection. Add error recovery, don't remove timeout. |
| Code splitting breaks routing | Test all routes after lazy-loading. Keep `Suspense` fallback visible. |
| Edge function deploy order matters | Follow the specified order. DB migration first. |
| `add_credits` migration on live DB | Function is additive (CREATE OR REPLACE), no schema change needed. Safe. |

---

## Verification Plan

After each day:
1. `npm run build` — zero errors
2. `npm run test` — all pass
3. Manual smoke test of affected flows

After Day 5:
- Full end-to-end test of: signup → create job → upload resumes → rank → view results → buy credits → subscribe → cancel → admin panel
- Verify all audit logs populated
- Verify no console errors in production build
# Deep Production Audit & Fix Plan

## Context
All 5 roadmap features were implemented. Now we deep-audit every function, the core ranking algorithm (main USP), all new components, and data flows. 3 parallel audit agents found **47 issues** across the codebase. This plan fixes all CRITICAL and HIGH issues, plus key MEDIUM ones.

---

## Audit Summary: 47 Issues Found

| Severity | Count | Action |
|----------|-------|--------|
| CRITICAL | 8 | Fix ALL immediately |
| HIGH | 7 | Fix ALL |
| MEDIUM | 14 | Fix important ones |
| LOW | 18 | Skip (code quality, future cleanup) |

---

## CRITICAL FIXES (Must fix — breaks production)

### C1: rewrite-jd wrong RPC parameter names
- **File:** `supabase/functions/rewrite-jd/index.ts:54-57`
- **Bug:** Uses `p_user_id`, `p_amount` but function expects `_user_id`, `_job_id`, `_amount`
- **Impact:** Credits NEVER deducted for rewrites — free API calls, revenue loss
- **Fix:** Change params to match rank-resumes pattern: `{ _user_id, _job_id, _amount }`

### C2: JD Rewrite "Apply" doesn't update DB for existing jobs
- **File:** `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx:1170-1172`
- **Bug:** `onApply` only sets local `jobDescription` state, doesn't save to `jobs` table
- **Impact:** Users see rewritten JD but next ranking still uses original JD
- **Fix:** Add `supabase.from("jobs").update({ job_description: newJd }).eq("id", selectedJobId)` + invalidate selectedJob

### C3: EducationPanel JD Match logic false positives
- **File:** `src/components/candidate/EducationPanel.tsx:22`
- **Bug:** Any education shows "JD Match" if ANY must-have contains word "degree" or "education"
- **Fix:** Remove generic keyword matching, only match if degree/institution text overlaps with specific must-have text

### C4: ExperiencePanel year calculation with invalid dates
- **File:** `src/components/candidate/ExperiencePanel.tsx:18-27`
- **Bug:** `parseInt()` on non-numeric dates returns NaN, silently breaks total years
- **Fix:** Add `isNaN()` guard, extract 4-digit year with regex, fallback gracefully

### C5: PDF extraction fallback has no try-catch
- **File:** `supabase/functions/rank-resumes/index.ts:236-238`
- **Bug:** If `extractPdfText` throws, entire ranking job crashes
- **Fix:** Wrap fallback in try-catch, set rawText = "" on failure, mark resume as "failed"

### C6: Outreach log RLS missing job ownership check
- **File:** `supabase/migrations/20260318180000_add_outreach_log.sql`
- **Bug:** Only checks `user_id = auth.uid()`, doesn't verify user owns the job/resume
- **Fix:** Add `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jobs WHERE jobs.id = outreach_log.job_id AND jobs.user_id = auth.uid())` to policies

### C7: JDRewriteDialog "Regenerate" doesn't reset editedJd
- **File:** `src/components/JDRewriteDialog.tsx`
- **Bug:** After regeneration, old edited text persists instead of fresh rewrite
- **Fix:** Add `setEditedJd("")` before calling the API again

### C8: Duplicate UUID_REGEX in JobDetailView
- **File:** `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx:49,109`
- **Bug:** Same regex defined twice
- **Fix:** Remove duplicate

---

## HIGH FIXES (Security/data integrity)

### H1: Missing skills overflow in SkillsDetailPanel
- **File:** `src/components/candidate/SkillsDetailPanel.tsx:62-72`
- **Fix:** Limit to 8 skills, show "+X more"

### H2: StageTracker date format not validated
- **File:** `src/components/candidate/StageTracker.tsx:116-119`
- **Fix:** Wrap `format()` in try-catch

### H3: Score clamping missing (negative scores possible)
- **File:** `src/components/candidate/SkillsDetailPanel.tsx:51`
- **Fix:** `Math.max(0, Math.min(data.score ?? 0, 100))`

### H4: JDAlignedPanel mobile layout breaks
- **File:** `src/components/candidate/JDAlignedPanel.tsx:32`
- **Fix:** Change `sm:grid-cols-3` to `md:grid-cols-3`

### H5: No OpenAI response validation in rank-resumes
- **File:** `supabase/functions/rank-resumes/index.ts:782-783`
- **Fix:** Validate `deepResult` has required fields before using

### H6: Batch processing no retry on OpenAI failures
- **File:** `supabase/functions/rank-resumes/index.ts:376-487`
- **Fix:** Add 1 retry with 2s delay for failed individual resume scoring

### H7: Confidence = 0 for all heuristic resumes
- **File:** `supabase/functions/rank-resumes/index.ts:575`
- **Fix:** Compute basic confidence from keyword match quality (e.g., matched/total * 50)

---

## MEDIUM FIXES (UX + reliability)

### M1: useStageTracker needs optimistic updates
- **Fix:** Update local cache immediately, revert on error

### M2: Email tooltip should show email address
- **File:** `src/components/CandidateRow.tsx:213`
- **Fix:** Change to `r.candidate_email` in tooltip

### M3: ConfidenceReport empty state
- **Fix:** Show "Scoring in progress..." stub instead of disappearing

### M4: Remove console.log from edge functions
- **Files:** rank-resumes, analyze-jd, rewrite-jd, send-candidate-email
- **Fix:** Remove all `console.log`, keep `console.error` for actual errors only

- **File:** `supabase/functions/rank-resumes/index.ts:609-615`
- **Fix:** Remove unused `userProfile` and `authUsers` queries

---

## RANKING ALGORITHM IMPROVEMENTS (USP quality)

### R1: Move magic numbers to ranking_config
- `20` (max AI-scored), `50` (embedding threshold), `5` (batch size), `49` (hard_fit cap)
- These should be admin-configurable, not hardcoded

### R2: Add validation for evaluation dimensions
- Before scoring, verify `dim.key` exists in `categoryScores`
- Fallback to 0 with warning instead of silent failure

### R3: Better heuristic confidence
- Instead of confidence=0 for heuristic resumes, compute:
  `confidence = Math.round((matchedKeywords / totalKeywords) * 50)`
- Cap at 50 since heuristic can never be high confidence

The action bar section has no tooltips right now, so we need to add TooltipProvider around that button (or wrap the whole button bar — but only the LinkedIn button needs it, so wrapping just that one button in its own TooltipProvider is safe)
- After `<StageTracker .../>` block (inside the `space-y-6` div), add `<CandidateNotesPanel resumeId={r.resume_id} jobId={jobId ?? ""} />` — but only render when `jobId` is truthy (safe because the panel's hook has an `enabled` guard)

Exact code from spec. Uses `(supabase as any)` cast for `candidate_notes` table (not in auto-generated types). queryKey is `["candidate-notes", resumeId, jobId]`. Realtime on `postgres_changes` for the table.

### Step 5 — Create `src/components/candidate/CandidateNotesPanel.tsx` (new file)
Props: `{ resumeId: string; jobId: string }`
Imports:
- `useCandidateNotes` from `@/hooks/useCandidateNotes`
- shadcn: `Button`, `Badge`, `Textarea`
- lucide: `MessageSquare`, `Trash2`, `Loader2`
- `formatDistanceToNow` from `date-fns`
- `useState` from react

Internal state: `content` (string), `noteType` (NoteType, default "general"), `submitting` (bool derived from addNote.isPending)

Note type pill buttons: General (gray), Positive (green), Concern (amber), Flag (red) — clicking sets `noteType` state.

Note type badge colors:
- general: `bg-muted text-muted-foreground`
- positive: `bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-900/20 dark:text-emerald-400`
- concern: `bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-900/20 dark:text-amber-400`
- flag: `bg-red-100 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-900/20 dark:text-red-400`

Submit: calls `addNote.mutateAsync({ content, noteType })`, clears textarea on success, shows `toast.error` on failure.

Delete: calls `deleteNote.mutateAsync(noteId)`, only shown when `note.author_id === currentUserId`.

Author avatar: first letter of `note.author_email` or "?" in a small circle. Color varies by first char.

Panel header: "Team Notes" h3 + badge with count.

Loading: show a subtle "Loading notes..." text when `isLoading` and notes empty.

Empty state: "No notes yet. Add the first one below." small muted text, centered, when `!isLoading && notes.length === 0`.

3. Create `src/hooks/useCandidateNotes.tsx` → run build
4. Create `src/components/candidate/CandidateNotesPanel.tsx` → run build
5. Edit `CandidateDetailSheet.tsx` (add CandidateNotesPanel) → run build

## Key observations from reading source files

- `Result` type does NOT have `linkedin_url` field → `extractLinkedInUrl` receives `result: { parsed_json?: any; linkedin_url?: string }` so it will just get `undefined` for `linkedin_url` on ranked resumes — that's fine, it falls through to `parsed_json` checks
- `CandidateRow` already imports `TooltipProvider` — LinkedIn button goes inside the existing `<TooltipProvider>` block
- `CandidateDetailSheet` does NOT import `Tooltip*` components — LinkedIn button needs its own `TooltipProvider` + `Tooltip` + `TooltipTrigger` + `TooltipContent`
- The action button bar in `CandidateDetailSheet` has Archive in `ml-auto` div — LinkedIn button goes before that div, after the Resume button
- `date-fns` v3 is already installed — `formatDistanceToNow` import works
- `candidate_notes` table is not in auto-generated types — using `(supabase as any)` cast as directed
- No DB migration needed in this plan — the task assumes the table exists or will be created separately. The hook handles the case where the table doesn't exist by returning empty (error will throw from react-query, but `enabled` guard prevents calls when IDs are null)

**NOTE:** The `candidate_notes` table migration is NOT included in this task spec. If the table doesn't exist in Supabase yet, the hook will fail at runtime. Should we also write the migration SQL? Not in scope per task — proceeding without it.

## File to create
`src/pages/CareerSettings.tsx`

## What it does
Career Page configuration page. Two cards:

- useRef for debounce timer

## State
- form fields: companyName, slug, logoUrl, brandColor, tagline, about, isActive
- slugStatus: "idle" | "checking" | "available" | "taken"
- isSaving: boolean
- configId: string | null (null = insert, string = update)
- togglingJobId: string | null

## Data fetching (React Query)
- queryKey: ["career-page-config", teamId]
- queryKey: ["career-jobs", userId]

## Slug auto-gen
slugify(name): name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "").slice(0, 60)
Called when companyName changes (only if slug hasn't been manually edited)

## Slug availability check
After 600ms debounce when slug field changes:
supabase.rpc("is_career_slug_available", { p_slug: slug, p_team_id: teamId })
Shows: checking spinner → green "Available" | red "Taken"

## Save (UPSERT)
if configId: update where id = configId
else: insert with team_id

## Job toggle
update jobs set is_published = !current where id = jobId
Optimistic update via React Query

## Career page URL
`${window.location.origin}/careers/${slug}`

## Skeleton states
- Config card skeleton: 6 skeleton rows while loading
- Jobs card skeleton: 3 skeleton rows while loading

## Empty state for jobs
Globe icon + "No jobs yet" + description

SidebarLayout > main.max-w-[760px] > FadeIn header > StaggerList with 2 cards
# Plan: Team Collaboration — Migration + Edge Functions

## Files to Create
1. `supabase/migrations/20260407100000_add_teams_and_notes.sql`
2. `supabase/functions/send-team-invite/index.ts`
3. `supabase/functions/accept-team-invite/index.ts`
4. `supabase/config.toml` — append two new function entries

## Key Facts from Migration Archaeology

### Existing policy names (exact strings, for DROP):
- jobs SELECT: `"Users read own jobs"`
- resumes SELECT: `"Users read own resumes"`
- results SELECT: `"Users read own results"`
- candidate_stages SELECT: `"Users can view stages for own jobs"`
- stage_history SELECT: `"Users can view stage history for own jobs"`
- applications SELECT: `"applications_owner_select"`

### Existing helper functions that already exist:
- `is_job_owner(_user_id, _job_id)` — in initial schema ✓
- `update_updated_at_column()` — in initial schema ✓

### Most recent handle_new_user (20260315164958):
- Reads email from raw_user_meta_data
- Reads inbound domain from platform_config
- Sanitizes local_part, does collision loop
- Inserts profiles (user_id, credits=5, inbox_email)
- Does NOT insert user_roles (that was removed in this version)

### CORS pattern (from admin-adjust-credits, most recent):
```ts
const ALLOWED_ORIGINS = [Deno.env.get("APP_URL") || "https://rankora.co", ...]
function getCorsOrigin(req): string { ... }
```

### Auth pattern (admin-adjust-credits):
- Parse Authorization header → call supabase.auth.getClaims(token) → get userId
- Then use service role client for DB ops

## Migration Structure

### Section 1: ENUM + Tables
- CREATE TYPE public.team_role AS ENUM ('owner', 'admin', 'member')
- teams, team_members, team_invitations, candidate_notes

### Section 2: Column addition
- ALTER TABLE jobs ADD COLUMN team_id uuid REFERENCES teams(id) ON DELETE SET NULL

### Section 3: Helper functions
- is_team_member, is_team_admin, is_team_member_of_job (all SECURITY DEFINER)

### Section 4: Update handle_new_user
- REPLACE existing function: add team creation + team_members insert + auto-accept invitations
- Keep all existing profile/inbox logic intact

### Section 5: Backfill DO block
- For each user without a team → create team + member row
- For each job without team_id → set team_id to owner's team

### Section 6: Auto-set team_id trigger
- BEFORE INSERT on jobs → fill team_id from user's owned team

### Section 7: RLS Policies
- Enable RLS on new tables
- teams, team_members, team_invitations, candidate_notes
- Drop + recreate SELECT policies on jobs, resumes, results, candidate_stages, stage_history, applications to include team members

### Section 8: Realtime
- ADD TABLE candidate_notes to supabase_realtime publication

## Edge Functions

### send-team-invite
- CORS with getCorsOrigin pattern from admin-adjust-credits
- Auth: parse Bearer token → getClaims → check is team admin/owner via DB
- Validate: email format, team_id, role in ('admin','member')
- Generate token: crypto.randomUUID()
- INSERT into team_invitations (with expires_at = now() + 7 days)
- Call send-email function internally OR use Resend directly
  - Decision: use Resend directly (inline HTML, simpler, no template dependency)
- Return invitation record

### accept-team-invite
- No auth required at gateway level (token IS auth)
- Parse { token } from body
- Validate token: exists, not expired, status='pending'
- If Authorization header present → get user_id from it
- If not authenticated → return 401 with "Please sign in to accept invite"
- Get invitation email, check against authenticated user's email
- INSERT team_member row
- UPDATE invitation status to 'accepted'
- Return { success: true, team_id, role }
# Plan: Team Members UI for Rankora

## Files to Create/Modify

### 1. CREATE `src/hooks/useTeam.tsx`

React Query hook that fetches:
- The user's own team (where they are owner) from `team_members` joined with `teams`
- All members of that team from `team_members`
- Pending invitations from `team_invitations`
- Member emails from `profiles` (via separate fetch per member)

Key implementation details:
- Query key: `["team", userId]`
- Use `(supabase as any)` cast for all three tables (not in generated types)
- Two-step fetch: first get team, then fetch members + invitations in parallel
- Return `myRole` by finding current user in members list
- `refetch` exposed from useQuery

Interface:
```ts
interface TeamMember { id, user_id, role, joined_at, email? }
interface TeamInvitation { id, email, role, status, created_at, expires_at }
interface UseTeamReturn { team, members, invitations, myRole, isLoading, refetch }
```

### 2. CREATE `src/pages/Team.tsx`

Page structure using `SidebarLayout` (same pattern as Billing.tsx and Settings.tsx):
```
SidebarLayout
  main.w-full.max-w-3xl.mx-auto.px-4.py-8
    FadeIn
      h1 "Team Members"
      p.text-muted-foreground "Invite teammates..."
    
    StaggerList
      // Invite Form Card
      Card > CardHeader + CardContent
        form: Input (email) + Select (role: admin|member) + Button "Invite"
        Loading spinner on button while submitting
      
      // Pending Invitations Card (conditional)
      {invitations.length > 0}
        Card > CardHeader "Pending Invitations" + CardContent
          list: email, role badge, sent date, Revoke button
      
      // Members Card
      Card > CardHeader "Members" + CardContent
        Loading: 3x Skeleton rows
        Empty state: icon + "No team members yet. Invite your first teammate above."
        Table: Member (avatar initial + email), Role (badge), Joined, Actions
```

Role badges (inline className, no external lib):
- owner: amber-100/amber-700 bg, Crown icon
- admin: blue-100/blue-700 bg, Shield icon  
- member: muted bg, Users icon

Actions per row:
- Owner row: no actions (current user is owner)
- admin/member rows (visible only if myRole === "owner" || "admin"):
  - Role change Select (owner cannot change themselves)
  - Remove button (Trash2 icon, only owner can remove)

Invite flow:
- `supabase.functions.invoke("send-team-invite", { body: { email, role, team_id } })`
- toast.success / toast.error
- call refetch()

Revoke:
- `(supabase as any).from("team_invitations").delete().eq("id", inviteId)`
- refetch()

Remove member:
- `(supabase as any).from("team_members").delete().eq("id", memberId)`
- refetch()

Change role:
- `(supabase as any).from("team_members").update({ role }).eq("id", memberId)`
- refetch()

### 3. MODIFY `src/components/JobSidebar.tsx`

- Add `Users` to lucide-react imports (already imported for other things — check; if not present add it)
- Inside the `{isAuthenticated && (...)}` block, after the Settings `SidebarMenuItem`, add:
```tsx
<SidebarMenuItem>
  <SidebarMenuButton onClick={() => navigate("/team")}>
    <Users className="h-4 w-4" />
    <span>Team</span>
  </SidebarMenuButton>
</SidebarMenuItem>
```

### 4. MODIFY `src/App.tsx`

- Add lazy import after the Settings import line:
  `const Team = lazy(() => import("./pages/Team"));`
- Add route inside MaintenanceGate Routes, after the `/settings` route:
  `<Route path="/team" element={<ProtectedRoute><Team /></ProtectedRoute>} />`

## Atomic Change Order

1. Create `src/hooks/useTeam.tsx` → run build
2. Create `src/pages/Team.tsx` → run build
3. Modify `src/components/JobSidebar.tsx` (add Team nav item) → run build
4. Modify `src/App.tsx` (add lazy import + route) → run build + verify /team returns 200

# Architecture Plan: Rankora Career Page Feature

## Stack

**Stack A-Lovable** -- Vite + React 18 + TypeScript + Tailwind + shadcn/ui + Supabase
Extending existing Rankora codebase. No new stack decisions needed -- this feature uses 100% existing infrastructure.

## Summary

A branded public career page per team/company. Recruiters configure it in the dashboard; candidates see a branded job listing page at `/careers/:slug` and click through to the existing `/apply/:jobId` flow.

---

## 1. SQL Migration

### 1a. New Table: `career_page_config`

```sql
CREATE TABLE public.career_page_config (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  team_id     uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.teams(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  slug        text NOT NULL,
  company_name text NOT NULL,
  logo_url    text,
  brand_color text NOT NULL DEFAULT '#6366f1',
  tagline     text,
  about       text,
  is_active   boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (team_id),
  UNIQUE (slug)
);

-- Slug format: lowercase, alphanumeric + hyphens, 3-60 chars
ALTER TABLE public.career_page_config
  ADD CONSTRAINT career_page_slug_format
  CHECK (slug ~ '^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9\-]{1,58}[a-z0-9]$');

CREATE INDEX idx_career_page_config_slug ON public.career_page_config(slug);
CREATE INDEX idx_career_page_config_team_id ON public.career_page_config(team_id);

-- updated_at trigger
CREATE TRIGGER update_career_page_config_updated_at
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.career_page_config
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();
```

### 1b. Extend `jobs` Table

```sql
ALTER TABLE public.jobs ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS location text;
ALTER TABLE public.jobs ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS department text;
ALTER TABLE public.jobs ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS employment_type text DEFAULT 'Full-time';
ALTER TABLE public.jobs ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_published boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false;
ALTER TABLE public.jobs ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS salary_range text;
```

The `employment_type` uses free text to allow custom values, but the UI will offer common presets: Full-time, Part-time, Contract, Internship, Freelance.

### 1c. RLS Policies for `career_page_config`

```sql
ALTER TABLE public.career_page_config ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Public SELECT by slug (anyone can view an active career page)
CREATE POLICY "career_page_public_read"
  ON public.career_page_config
  FOR SELECT
  TO anon, authenticated
  USING (is_active = true);

-- Team owner/admin can SELECT their own config (even if inactive, for editing)
CREATE POLICY "career_page_team_read"
  ON public.career_page_config
  FOR SELECT
  TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id)
  );

-- Team owner/admin can INSERT their config
CREATE POLICY "career_page_team_insert"
  ON public.career_page_config
  FOR INSERT
  TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (
    public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id)
  );

-- Team owner/admin can UPDATE their config
CREATE POLICY "career_page_team_update"
  ON public.career_page_config
  FOR UPDATE
  TO authenticated
  USING (public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id))
  WITH CHECK (public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id));

-- Team owner/admin can DELETE their config
CREATE POLICY "career_page_team_delete"
  ON public.career_page_config
  FOR DELETE
  TO authenticated
  USING (public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id));
```

### 1d. Public RPC: `get_career_page`

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_career_page(p_slug text)
RETURNS json
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  config_row record;
  jobs_arr json;
BEGIN
  -- Fetch the career page config
  SELECT id, team_id, slug, company_name, logo_url, brand_color, tagline, about, is_active
  INTO config_row
  FROM public.career_page_config
  WHERE slug = p_slug AND is_active = true;

  IF config_row IS NULL THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;

  -- Fetch published jobs for this team
  SELECT COALESCE(json_agg(
    json_build_object(
      'id', j.id,
      'title', j.title,
      'location', j.location,
      'department', j.department,
      'employment_type', j.employment_type,
      'salary_range', j.salary_range,
      'created_at', j.created_at
    ) ORDER BY j.created_at DESC
  ), '[]'::json)
  INTO jobs_arr
  FROM public.jobs j
  WHERE j.team_id = config_row.team_id
    AND j.is_published = true
    AND j.status != 'failed';

  RETURN json_build_object(
    'config', json_build_object(
      'id', config_row.id,
      'slug', config_row.slug,
      'company_name', config_row.company_name,
      'logo_url', config_row.logo_url,
      'brand_color', config_row.brand_color,
      'tagline', config_row.tagline,
      'about', config_row.about
    ),
    'jobs', jobs_arr
  );
END;
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_career_page(text) TO anon, authenticated;
```

**Why SECURITY DEFINER**: The public career page is viewed by unauthenticated visitors. The function bypasses RLS to read jobs and config, but only exposes published jobs from active career pages. This follows the exact same pattern as the existing `get_job_apply_info` function.

### 1e. Slug Validation RPC

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_career_slug_available(p_slug text, p_team_id uuid DEFAULT NULL)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
SECURITY DEFINER
STABLE
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.career_page_config
    WHERE slug = p_slug
      AND (p_team_id IS NULL OR team_id != p_team_id)
  );
$$;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.is_career_slug_available(text, uuid) TO authenticated;
```

This lets the settings form check slug availability in real-time. If `p_team_id` is passed, it excludes the team's own current slug (so saving without changing the slug doesn't error).

## 2. Data Flow Diagram

```
RECRUITER (Dashboard)                    CANDIDATE (Public)
=====================                    ==================

/settings or /career-settings            /careers/:slug
        |                                      |
        v                                      v
CareerPageSettings.tsx               CareerPage.tsx
        |                                      |
        v                                      v
supabase.from('career_page_config')  supabase.rpc('get_career_page', { p_slug })
  .upsert(config)                          |
        |                              Returns: { config, jobs[] }
        v                                      |
career_page_config table                       v
        |                              Render branded page
        |                              with job listings
        |                                      |
        |                              Click "Apply" on a job
        |                                      |
        v                                      v
jobs table (is_published = true)       /apply/:jobId (existing flow)
```

```
DB RELATIONSHIPS:

  teams (1) -----> (1) career_page_config  (one config per team)
    |
    | (team_id FK)
    v
  jobs (many) --- is_published=true ---> shown on career page
    |
    | (job_id FK)
    v
  applications (many) <--- submitted via /apply/:jobId
```

## 3. Routes and Components

|-------|-----------|------|---------|

| `/career-settings` | `CareerSettings.tsx` | Protected | Career page configuration |

### 3b. Route Registration in `App.tsx`

```tsx
// Add to lazy imports
const CareerPage = lazy(() => import("./pages/CareerPage"));
const CareerSettings = lazy(() => import("./pages/CareerSettings"));

// Public route (outside MaintenanceGate, like /apply/:jobId)
<Route path="/careers/:slug" element={<CareerPage />} />

// Protected route (inside MaintenanceGate)
<Route path="/career-settings" element={<ProtectedRoute><CareerSettings /></ProtectedRoute>} />
```

The `/careers/:slug` route MUST be outside `MaintenanceGate` -- just like `/apply/:jobId`. Candidates must always be able to view career pages and apply regardless of maintenance mode.

### 3c. New Components

#### `src/pages/CareerPage.tsx` -- Public Career Page

The main public-facing component. No auth required.

**Data fetching:**
- Uses `useQuery` to call `supabase.rpc('get_career_page', { p_slug: slug })`
- Returns `{ config, jobs }` or `null` if slug not found / page inactive

**Layout structure:**
```
<div className="min-h-screen bg-background">
  <!-- Company Header -->
  <header style={{ borderBottomColor: config.brand_color }}>
    <img src={config.logo_url} /> or fallback initial
    <h1>{config.company_name}</h1>
    <p>{config.tagline}</p>
  </header>

  <!-- About Section (if exists) -->
  <section>
    <h2>About {config.company_name}</h2>
    <p>{config.about}</p>
  </section>

  <!-- Open Positions -->
  <section>
    <h2>Open Positions ({jobs.length})</h2>

    <!-- Optional department filter pills -->
    <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
      {departments.map(d => <button>d</button>)}
    </div>

    <!-- Job cards -->
    {filteredJobs.map(job => (
      <JobListingCard
        key={job.id}
        title={job.title}
        location={job.location}
        department={job.department}
        employmentType={job.employment_type}
        salaryRange={job.salary_range}
        createdAt={job.created_at}
        onApply={() => navigate(`/apply/${job.id}`)}
      />
    ))}

    <!-- Empty state if no jobs -->
  </section>

  <!-- Footer: "Powered by Rankora" -->
  <footer>
    Powered by Rankora
  </footer>
</div>
```

**Brand color application:**
- The `brand_color` from config is applied via inline CSS custom property `--career-brand` on the root div
- Used for: header accent line, "Apply" button background, department filter active state
- All other styling uses standard Tailwind classes for consistent look

**States to handle:**
1. Loading -- skeleton UI
2. Page not found (null response) -- branded 404 with Rankora branding
3. Page found but 0 published jobs -- company info displayed, "No open positions" empty state
4. Page found with jobs -- full career page

**SEO:**
- Uses existing `SeoHead` component pattern
- Sets: `<title>{company_name} - Careers</title>`
- Sets: `<meta name="description" content="{tagline or about excerpt}">`
- Sets: `<meta property="og:title" content="Careers at {company_name}">`

#### `src/pages/CareerSettings.tsx` -- Career Page Configuration

Wrapped in `SidebarLayout`. Uses `useTeam()` hook to get the user's team.

**Layout:**
```
<SidebarLayout>
  <main className="w-full max-w-[760px] mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8">
    <h1>Career Page</h1>
    <p>Configure your public career page to attract candidates.</p>

    <!-- Card: Page Settings -->
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>Career Page Settings</CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <form>
          <!-- Slug input with live availability check -->
          <div>
            <Label>Career Page URL</Label>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">rankora.co/careers/</span>
              <Input value={slug} onChange={handleSlugChange} />
              {slugAvailable ? <CheckCircle2 /> : <X />}
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Company Name -->
          <Input label="Company Name" value={companyName} />

          <!-- Logo URL -->
          <Input label="Logo URL" value={logoUrl} placeholder="https://..." />

          <!-- Brand Color -->
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Input type="color" value={brandColor} />
            <Input value={brandColor} /> <!-- hex text input -->
          </div>

          <!-- Tagline -->
          <Input label="Tagline" value={tagline} placeholder="Building the future of..." />

          <!-- About -->
          <Textarea label="About" value={about} rows={4} />

          <!-- Active toggle -->
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Switch checked={isActive} onCheckedChange={setIsActive} />
            <Label>Career page is live</Label>
          </div>

          <Button type="submit">Save Changes</Button>
        </form>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>

    <!-- Card: Published Jobs -->
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>Published Jobs</CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <p>Jobs marked as "Published" will appear on your career page.</p>
        <!-- Table of user's jobs with is_published toggle -->
        <Table>
          <thead>
            <tr><th>Job</th><th>Location</th><th>Department</th><th>Published</th></tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {jobs.map(job => (
              <tr>
                <td>{job.title}</td>
                <td>{job.location || '-'}</td>
                <td>{job.department || '-'}</td>
                <td><Switch checked={job.is_published} onChange={togglePublish(job.id)} /></td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </Table>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>

    <!-- Card: Preview Link -->
    {isActive && slug && (
      <Card>
        <CardContent className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium">Your career page is live</p>
            <a href={`/careers/${slug}`} target="_blank">
              rankora.co/careers/{slug}
            </a>
          </div>
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={copyLink}>
            <Link2 /> Copy Link
          </Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    )}
  </main>
</SidebarLayout>
```

**Data flow:**
1. On mount: fetch career page config for user's team via `supabase.from('career_page_config').select('*').eq('team_id', team.id).maybeSingle()`
2. If config exists: populate form fields
3. If config does not exist: show empty form, save will INSERT
4. On save: UPSERT to `career_page_config` (upsert on `team_id`)
5. Published jobs table: fetch user's jobs, show toggle for `is_published`

**Slug validation:**
- On slug input change, debounce 500ms, call `supabase.rpc('is_career_slug_available', { p_slug, p_team_id: team.id })`
- Show green check or red X next to the input
- Enforce client-side: lowercase, no spaces, alphanumeric + hyphens, 3-60 chars
- Auto-generate slug from company name on first setup (e.g., "Boldteq Inc" -> "boldteq-inc")

```
<Collapsible>
```

## 6. Security Design

### RLS Summary

| Table | anon SELECT | authed SELECT | authed INSERT | authed UPDATE | authed DELETE |
|-------|-------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|---------------|
| career_page_config | Only `is_active = true` rows | Team admin: all own rows | Team admin only | Team admin only | Team admin only |
| jobs (new columns) | No change (existing RLS) | No change | No change | No change | No change |

### Security Considerations

1. **Slug injection**: The slug regex constraint (`^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9\-]{1,58}[a-z0-9]$`) prevents path traversal, SQL injection via slug, and ensures URL safety.

2. **Brand color XSS**: The `brand_color` field is used in inline styles only (`style={{ backgroundColor: config.brand_color }}`). React's JSX automatically escapes attribute values, preventing XSS via CSS injection. Additionally, client-side validation enforces hex format (`/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/`).

3. **Logo URL**: Rendered in an `<img>` tag with `src={config.logo_url}`. React escapes this. No `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`. The `about` field is also rendered as plain text, not HTML.

4. **SECURITY DEFINER on get_career_page**: Intentional -- same pattern as existing `get_job_apply_info`. Only exposes: published jobs from active career pages. Never exposes: job descriptions (heavy text), user IDs, internal job statuses, team membership details, or any PII.

5. **Rate limiting**: Not needed for MVP. The RPC is a simple read query with indexed lookups. If abuse becomes an issue, Supabase's built-in rate limiting handles it.

---

## 7. Performance

- `get_career_page` RPC: Single function call, 2 indexed queries (career_page_config by slug, jobs by team_id + is_published). Expected latency: <50ms.
- Career page data cached via React Query with 60s `staleTime` -- subsequent visits within a minute use cached data.
- Jobs list is typically small (<50 jobs per team). No pagination needed for MVP.
- No additional edge functions needed -- all data fetching via direct Supabase client + RPC.

---

## 8. SEO for Career Pages

Career pages are public and should be indexable.

- Dynamic `<title>` and `<meta>` tags via the existing `SeoHead` pattern
- Structured data (JSON-LD) for `JobPosting` schema on each job card:

```json
{
  "@context": "https://schema.org",
  "@type": "JobPosting",
  "title": "Senior Engineer",
  "datePosted": "2026-04-05",
  "employmentType": "FULL_TIME",
  "hiringOrganization": {
    "@type": "Organization",
    "name": "Boldteq"
  },
  "jobLocation": {
    "@type": "Place",
    "address": "Remote"
  }
}
```

- The sitemap edge function can be extended to include `/careers/:slug` pages, but that is a separate task and not blocking for this feature.
