# Architecture Plan: Team Members + Candidate Notes + LinkedIn Button

## 1. Key Architecture Decision: Teams Table vs Direct Linking

**Decision: Use a `teams` table (workspace model).**

Why not just `team_invitations` + `team_members` linking users directly?

The direct-linking approach (owner_user_id + member_user_id) breaks down quickly:
- If the original user deletes their account, all team relationships are orphaned
- No clean place to store team-level settings (team name, shared credits, etc.)
- Upgrading to multi-team later requires a full schema rewrite

The workspace model costs 2 extra tables now but saves a painful migration later. Every user who signs up gets an implicit personal team (auto-created in the `handle_new_user` trigger). When they invite someone, that person joins the existing team. Simple, extensible, and the RLS rewrite is straightforward.

---

## 2. Complete SQL Migration

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- MIGRATION: Team Members + Candidate Notes + LinkedIn Button
-- ============================================================

-- ── 1. TEAM ROLE ENUM ─────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TYPE public.team_role AS ENUM ('owner', 'admin', 'member');

-- ── 2. TEAMS TABLE ────────────────────────────────────────────
-- Every user gets a personal team on signup. Teams own nothing
-- directly; they exist purely as a membership container.
-- Credits and billing stay on profiles (the owner's profile).
CREATE TABLE public.teams (
  id         uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  name       text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'My Team',
  owner_id   uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_teams_owner ON public.teams(owner_id);

-- ── 3. TEAM MEMBERS TABLE ─────────────────────────────────────
-- Links users to teams with a role. Owner is auto-inserted.
-- A user can belong to multiple teams (their own + invited).
CREATE TABLE public.team_members (
  id        uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  team_id   uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.teams(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id   uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  role      team_role NOT NULL DEFAULT 'member',
  joined_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE(team_id, user_id)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_team_members_team ON public.team_members(team_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_team_members_user ON public.team_members(user_id);

-- ── 4. TEAM INVITATIONS TABLE ─────────────────────────────────
-- Pending invitations by email. When invited user signs up or
-- logs in, a trigger/check auto-accepts pending invitations.
CREATE TABLE public.team_invitations (
  id           uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  team_id      uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.teams(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  email        text NOT NULL,
  role         team_role NOT NULL DEFAULT 'member',
  invited_by   uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  status       text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
                 CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'accepted', 'declined', 'expired')),
  token        text NOT NULL DEFAULT encode(gen_random_bytes(32), 'hex'),
  created_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  expires_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT (now() + interval '7 days'),
  UNIQUE(team_id, email)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_team_invitations_email ON public.team_invitations(email);
CREATE INDEX idx_team_invitations_team ON public.team_invitations(team_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_team_invitations_token ON public.team_invitations(token);

-- ── 5. CANDIDATE NOTES TABLE ──────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE public.candidate_notes (
  id         uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  resume_id  uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.resumes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  job_id     uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.jobs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  author_id  uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  note_type  text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'general'
               CHECK (note_type IN ('general', 'positive', 'concern', 'flag')),
  content    text NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_candidate_notes_resume_job ON public.candidate_notes(resume_id, job_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_candidate_notes_job ON public.candidate_notes(job_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_candidate_notes_author ON public.candidate_notes(author_id);

-- ── 6. ADD team_id TO JOBS TABLE ──────────────────────────────
-- Links jobs to a team so team members can access them.
-- Nullable initially; backfill sets it for existing jobs.
ALTER TABLE public.jobs ADD COLUMN team_id uuid REFERENCES public.teams(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_jobs_team ON public.jobs(team_id);

-- ── 7. HELPER FUNCTION: Check team membership ─────────────────
-- Returns true if user is a member of the team that owns a job.
-- Used in RLS policies across all job-scoped tables.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_team_member_of_job(_user_id uuid, _job_id uuid)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM public.jobs j
    JOIN public.team_members tm ON tm.team_id = j.team_id
    WHERE j.id = _job_id
      AND tm.user_id = _user_id
  )
$$;

-- Also a direct team membership check
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_team_member(_user_id uuid, _team_id uuid)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.team_members
    WHERE team_id = _team_id AND user_id = _user_id
  )
$$;

-- Check if user has admin+ role in a team
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_team_admin(_user_id uuid, _team_id uuid)
RETURNS boolean
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.team_members
    WHERE team_id = _team_id
      AND user_id = _user_id
      AND role IN ('owner', 'admin')
  )
$$;

-- ── 8. AUTO-CREATE TEAM ON USER SIGNUP ────────────────────────
-- Modify existing handle_new_user to also create a personal team.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  new_team_id uuid;
BEGIN
  -- Create profile (existing behavior)
  INSERT INTO public.profiles (user_id, inbox_email)
  VALUES (NEW.id, encode(gen_random_bytes(8), 'hex') || '@inbound.rankora.co');

  -- Create default user role (existing behavior)
  INSERT INTO public.user_roles (user_id, role) VALUES (NEW.id, 'user');

  -- Create personal team
  INSERT INTO public.teams (name, owner_id)
  VALUES (
    COALESCE(
      NEW.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name',
      split_part(NEW.email, '@', 1)
    ) || '''s Team',
    NEW.id
  )
  RETURNING id INTO new_team_id;

  -- Add user as owner of their team
  INSERT INTO public.team_members (team_id, user_id, role)
  VALUES (new_team_id, NEW.id, 'owner');

  -- Auto-accept any pending invitations for this email
  -- (They signed up after being invited)
  INSERT INTO public.team_members (team_id, user_id, role)
  SELECT ti.team_id, NEW.id, ti.role
  FROM public.team_invitations ti
  WHERE ti.email = NEW.email
    AND ti.status = 'pending'
    AND ti.expires_at > now()
  ON CONFLICT (team_id, user_id) DO NOTHING;

  UPDATE public.team_invitations
  SET status = 'accepted'
  WHERE email = NEW.email AND status = 'pending';

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

-- ── 9. BACKFILL: Create teams for existing users ──────────────
-- For every existing user without a team, create one and link their jobs.
DO $$
DECLARE
  r RECORD;
  new_team_id uuid;
BEGIN
  FOR r IN
    SELECT p.user_id, u.email, u.raw_user_meta_data
    FROM public.profiles p
    JOIN auth.users u ON u.id = p.user_id
    WHERE NOT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM public.team_members tm WHERE tm.user_id = p.user_id AND tm.role = 'owner'
    )
  LOOP
    INSERT INTO public.teams (name, owner_id)
    VALUES (
      COALESCE(
        r.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name',
        split_part(r.email, '@', 1)
      ) || '''s Team',
      r.user_id
    )
    RETURNING id INTO new_team_id;

    INSERT INTO public.team_members (team_id, user_id, role)
    VALUES (new_team_id, r.user_id, 'owner');

    -- Link all existing jobs to this team
    UPDATE public.jobs SET team_id = new_team_id WHERE user_id = r.user_id;
  END LOOP;
END;
$$;

-- ── 10. AUTO-SET team_id ON NEW JOBS ──────────────────────────
-- When a user creates a job, auto-fill team_id from their owned team.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.auto_set_job_team_id()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.team_id IS NULL THEN
    SELECT tm.team_id INTO NEW.team_id
    FROM public.team_members tm
    WHERE tm.user_id = NEW.user_id AND tm.role = 'owner'
    LIMIT 1;
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER set_job_team_id
  BEFORE INSERT ON public.jobs
  FOR EACH ROW
  EXECUTE FUNCTION public.auto_set_job_team_id();

-- ── 11. RLS POLICIES ──────────────────────────────────────────

-- --- Teams ---
ALTER TABLE public.teams ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Members can view their teams"
  ON public.teams FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (public.is_team_member(auth.uid(), id));

CREATE POLICY "Owner can update their team"
  ON public.teams FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
  USING (owner_id = auth.uid());

-- Teams are created by trigger only
CREATE POLICY "No direct team insert"
  ON public.teams FOR INSERT TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (false);

-- --- Team Members ---
ALTER TABLE public.team_members ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Members can view team roster"
  ON public.team_members FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (public.is_team_member(auth.uid(), team_id));

-- Only team admins/owners can insert (invite acceptance is via trigger)
CREATE POLICY "Team admins can add members"
  ON public.team_members FOR INSERT TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id));

-- Only team admins/owners can update roles
CREATE POLICY "Team admins can update member roles"
  ON public.team_members FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
  USING (public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id));

-- Only team admins/owners can remove (or self-remove)
CREATE POLICY "Team admins can remove members"
  ON public.team_members FOR DELETE TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id)
    OR user_id = auth.uid() -- members can leave
  );

-- --- Team Invitations ---
ALTER TABLE public.team_invitations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Team admins can view invitations"
  ON public.team_invitations FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id));

CREATE POLICY "Team admins can create invitations"
  ON public.team_invitations FOR INSERT TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id));

CREATE POLICY "Team admins can update invitations"
  ON public.team_invitations FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
  USING (public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id));

CREATE POLICY "Team admins can delete invitations"
  ON public.team_invitations FOR DELETE TO authenticated
  USING (public.is_team_admin(auth.uid(), team_id));

-- --- Candidate Notes ---
ALTER TABLE public.candidate_notes ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Team members can view notes on jobs they have access to
CREATE POLICY "Team members can view notes"
  ON public.candidate_notes FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_job_owner(auth.uid(), job_id)
    OR public.is_team_member_of_job(auth.uid(), job_id)
  );

-- Any team member can add notes
CREATE POLICY "Team members can add notes"
  ON public.candidate_notes FOR INSERT TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (
    author_id = auth.uid()
    AND (
      public.is_job_owner(auth.uid(), job_id)
      OR public.is_team_member_of_job(auth.uid(), job_id)
    )
  );

-- Authors can edit their own notes
CREATE POLICY "Authors can update own notes"
  ON public.candidate_notes FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
  USING (author_id = auth.uid());

-- Authors can delete their own notes; team admins can delete any
CREATE POLICY "Authors and admins can delete notes"
  ON public.candidate_notes FOR DELETE TO authenticated
  USING (
    author_id = auth.uid()
    OR EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM public.jobs j
      JOIN public.team_members tm ON tm.team_id = j.team_id
      WHERE j.id = candidate_notes.job_id
        AND tm.user_id = auth.uid()
        AND tm.role IN ('owner', 'admin')
    )
  );

-- ── 12. UPDATE EXISTING JOB RLS POLICIES ──────────────────────
-- Team members need to see jobs owned by their team.
-- We DROP and recreate the job SELECT policy.

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Users read own jobs" ON public.jobs;
CREATE POLICY "Users read own or team jobs" ON public.jobs
  FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (
    user_id = auth.uid()
    OR (team_id IS NOT NULL AND public.is_team_member(auth.uid(), team_id))
  );

-- Keep insert/update/delete restricted to job owner only
-- (team members can view but not modify jobs for MVP)

-- ── 13. UPDATE EXISTING RESUMES RLS ───────────────────────────
-- Team members need to see resumes for team jobs.

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Users read own resumes" ON public.resumes;
CREATE POLICY "Users read own or team resumes" ON public.resumes
  FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (
    public.is_job_owner(auth.uid(), job_id)
    OR public.is_team_member_of_job(auth.uid(), job_id)
  );

-- ── 14. UPDATE EXISTING RESULTS RLS ──────────────────────────
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Users read own results" ON public.results;
CREATE POLICY "Users read own or team results" ON public.results
  FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM public.resumes r
      JOIN public.jobs j ON r.job_id = j.id
      WHERE r.id = results.resume_id
        AND (
          j.user_id = auth.uid()
          OR (j.team_id IS NOT NULL AND public.is_team_member(auth.uid(), j.team_id))
        )
    )
  );

-- ── 15. UPDATE CANDIDATE_STAGES RLS ──────────────────────────
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Users can view stages for own jobs" ON public.candidate_stages;
CREATE POLICY "Users can view stages for own or team jobs" ON public.candidate_stages
  FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.jobs WHERE jobs.id = candidate_stages.job_id AND jobs.user_id = auth.uid())
    OR public.is_team_member_of_job(auth.uid(), job_id)
  );

-- Members with 'member' role can only view, not modify stages
-- (owner and admin keep existing insert/update/delete policies)

-- ── 16. UPDATE STAGE_HISTORY RLS ─────────────────────────────
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Users can view stage history for own jobs" ON public.stage_history;
CREATE POLICY "Users can view stage history for own or team jobs" ON public.stage_history
  FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (
    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.jobs WHERE jobs.id = stage_history.job_id AND jobs.user_id = auth.uid())
    OR public.is_team_member_of_job(auth.uid(), job_id)
  );

-- ── 17. UPDATE APPLICATIONS RLS ──────────────────────────────
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "applications_owner_select" ON public.applications;
CREATE POLICY "applications_owner_or_team_select" ON public.applications
  FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM jobs
      WHERE jobs.id = applications.job_id
        AND (
          jobs.user_id = auth.uid()
          OR (jobs.team_id IS NOT NULL AND public.is_team_member(auth.uid(), jobs.team_id))
        )
    )
  );

-- ── 18. TRIGGERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TRIGGER update_teams_updated_at
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.teams
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();

CREATE TRIGGER update_candidate_notes_updated_at
  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.candidate_notes
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();

-- ── 19. ENABLE REALTIME ───────────────────────────────────────
ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.candidate_notes;

-- ── 20. COMMENTS ──────────────────────────────────────────────
COMMENT ON TABLE public.teams IS 'Workspace container; every user auto-gets a personal team';
COMMENT ON TABLE public.team_members IS 'Links users to teams with role (owner/admin/member)';
COMMENT ON TABLE public.team_invitations IS 'Pending email invitations to join a team';
COMMENT ON TABLE public.candidate_notes IS 'Collaborative notes on candidates, scoped to resume+job';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.jobs.team_id IS 'Team that owns this job; auto-set from creator on insert';
```

---

## 3. RLS Data Flow Diagram

Current RLS chain (single user):
```
auth.uid() = jobs.user_id  -->  resumes via is_job_owner  -->  results via resume->job->user_id
```

New RLS chain (team-aware):
```
auth.uid() = jobs.user_id                           (owner: full CRUD)
  OR
auth.uid() IN team_members WHERE team_id = jobs.team_id  (member: SELECT only)
  |
  +--> resumes: SELECT if is_job_owner OR is_team_member_of_job
  +--> results: SELECT if job owner OR team member
  +--> candidate_stages: SELECT if job owner OR team member (INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE: owner only)
  +--> candidate_notes: SELECT/INSERT if team member; UPDATE/DELETE own notes only
  +--> applications: SELECT if job owner OR team member
```

Key principle: **Team members get read access to everything in the team's jobs. Only the job owner retains write access to jobs, resumes, results, and stages. Notes are the one exception -- all team members can create notes, but can only edit/delete their own.**

```tsx
        onClick={() => window.open(linkedinUrl, '_blank')}>
```

```tsx
      </Button>
    </TooltipTrigger>
    <TooltipContent>LinkedIn Profile</TooltipContent>
  </Tooltip>

```

**LinkedIn URL extraction logic** (utility function):
```ts
// src/lib/linkedin.ts
export function extractLinkedInUrl(result: Result): string | null {
  const pj = result.parsed_json;
  if (!pj) return null;

  // Check common locations in parsed resume JSON
  if (typeof pj.linkedin === 'string' && pj.linkedin) return normalizeLinkedInUrl(pj.linkedin);
  if (pj.contact?.linkedin) return normalizeLinkedInUrl(pj.contact.linkedin);
  if (pj.links?.linkedin) return normalizeLinkedInUrl(pj.links.linkedin);

  // Check applications table data (if merged into result)
  if ((result as any).linkedin_url) return normalizeLinkedInUrl((result as any).linkedin_url);

  return null;
}

function normalizeLinkedInUrl(url: string): string {
  if (url.startsWith('http')) return url;
  if (url.startsWith('linkedin.com') || url.startsWith('www.linkedin.com')) return 'https://' + url;
  // Handle bare profile IDs like "in/johndoe"
  if (url.startsWith('in/')) return 'https://www.linkedin.com/' + url;
  return 'https://www.linkedin.com/in/' + url;
}
```

---

## 7. Data Flow: Team Membership Check

### On Login / Auth State Change

```
1. User logs in
2. useAuth fetches profile (existing)
3. NEW: useTeam hook fetches user's teams:
     SELECT t.*, tm.role
     FROM team_members tm
     JOIN teams t ON t.id = tm.team_id
     WHERE tm.user_id = auth.uid()
4. Store in TeamContext: { teams, activeTeam, activeRole }
5. For MVP, activeTeam = the team where role = 'owner'
```

### Invite Flow

```
1. Owner/Admin enters email on /team page
2. INSERT into team_invitations (email, role, team_id, invited_by)
3. Call send-email edge function to send invite email with link
4. Link format: https://rankora.co/auth?invite=<token>
5. When invitee visits link:
   a. If already has account: POST to accept-invitation edge function
      -> Validates token, inserts team_member, updates invitation status
   b. If new user: signs up normally
      -> handle_new_user trigger auto-accepts pending invitations for that email
6. React Query invalidates team_members query
```

### Accepting Invitation (existing user)

```
1. User clicks invite link -> /auth?invite=<token>
2. After login, check for `invite` search param
3. Call accept-invitation edge function:
   - Validate token exists, not expired, status = 'pending'
   - Insert into team_members
   - Update invitation status to 'accepted'
4. Redirect to /team showing new team membership
```

## 9. Edge Functions Needed

### `send-team-invite` (new)
- Input: `{ email, role, teamId }`
- Validates caller is team admin/owner
- Inserts invitation record
- Calls existing `send-email` function with invite template
- Returns invitation record

### `accept-team-invite` (new)
- Input: `{ token }`
- Validates token, not expired, status = pending
- Inserts team_member record
- Updates invitation status
- Returns team info

No new edge function needed for notes -- notes use direct Supabase client INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE with RLS enforcement.

No new edge function needed for LinkedIn -- it is purely a frontend display feature extracting URLs from existing `parsed_json` data.

## 12. Risks and Mitigations

| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
|------|-----------|--------|------------|
| RLS policy update breaks existing single-user queries | Medium | High | The new policies use OR logic: `user_id = auth.uid() OR team_member`. Existing users with no team_id on jobs still pass via `user_id = auth.uid()`. Backfill ensures all jobs get team_id. |
| `is_team_member_of_job` SECURITY DEFINER function has performance impact on large tables | Low | Medium | Function does a simple join on indexed columns (jobs.id, team_members.team_id, team_members.user_id). Acceptable for MVP scale. |
| Backfill fails for users with edge-case profiles | Low | Medium | Backfill is wrapped in a DO block with per-user iteration. If one user fails, others still process. Log and fix manually. |
| Invitation email spam | Low | Low | Rate limit invitations to 10 per team per day. Unique constraint on (team_id, email) prevents duplicate invites. |
| `handle_new_user` trigger becomes complex | Low | Medium | Now handles 3 responsibilities (profile, role, team). Still a single transaction. If team creation fails, the entire signup fails -- this is acceptable because the team is mandatory for the product to work. |

---

## 13. What is NOT in Scope (Deferred)

- **Team-level billing / shared credits**: Credits stay on the owner's profile. Team members use the owner's credits when they process jobs. This is V2.
- **Multiple teams per user**: A user can technically belong to multiple teams (via invitations), but the UI only shows one active team. Multi-team switching is V2.
- **Granular permissions**: No per-job or per-feature permissions. Team roles (owner/admin/member) control everything. V2.
- **Team member job creation**: Only the job owner can create jobs. Team members can view and add notes but cannot create new jobs under the team. V2.
- **@mention notifications**: The content supports @mention syntax (text stored as-is), but no notification is sent. V2.
- **Note attachments**: Text only. No file attachments on notes. V2.

---

## 14. Technical Decisions Summary

| Decision | Choice | Reasoning |
|----------|--------|-----------|
| Team model | Dedicated `teams` table | Extensible for multi-team, team settings, team billing in V2. Direct user-linking would need a rewrite later. |
| Team creation | Auto on signup via trigger | Every user always has a team. No "create team" flow needed. Simpler UX. |
| Job scoping | `team_id` column on `jobs` | Clean FK relationship. Backfill is simple. Auto-set via trigger on insert. |
| RLS approach | OR-based policies | `user_id = auth.uid() OR is_team_member(...)`. Backwards compatible -- solo users unaffected. |
| Team member permissions | Read-only for `member` role | MVP simplicity. Members can view everything + add notes. Only owner/admin can modify jobs/stages. |
| Notes location | Inside CandidateDetailSheet | Notes are contextual to a specific candidate evaluation. Not a standalone page. |
| LinkedIn URL | Extract from existing `parsed_json` | No new data needed. GPT-4o already extracts LinkedIn URLs during resume parsing when available. |
| Realtime for notes | Supabase realtime subscription | Team members see each other's notes instantly without polling. Already have the pattern from feature_flags. |
| Invite acceptance | Trigger-based for new users, edge function for existing | New users auto-join via `handle_new_user`. Existing users need explicit acceptance (security: don't auto-add existing users to teams without consent). |

  - Preview URL text below: `rankora.co/careers/{slug}`

- Tagline Input
- About Textarea
- Active Switch ("Publish career page")
- Save button (with Loader2 spinner while saving)

**Card 2 — Published Jobs:**
- Table columns: Title | Location | Department | Published (Switch)
- Toggle per row: updates `is_published` on the jobs table
- Skeleton loading state (3 rows)
- Empty state: Globe icon + "No jobs yet. Create a job first." + Button to navigate "/"

**Data fetching:**
- `useTeam()` for teamId and team.name
- `useAuth()` for user.id
- career_page_config: `(supabase as any).from("career_page_config").select("*").eq("team_id", teamId).maybeSingle()`
- jobs: `(supabase as any).from("jobs").select("id, title, location, department, employment_type, is_published, status").eq("user_id", user.id).order("created_at", { ascending: false })`
- React Query with proper queryKey arrays

**Save logic:**
- Check if config exists (has an id) → use .update().eq("id", existing.id) : .insert()
- Toast success "Career page saved" / toast error

**Slug helpers:**
- `toSlug(name: string)` pure function: lowercase, replace non-alphanumeric with `-`, trim dashes, slice to 60
- Debounced availability check on slug change (300ms)
- Show availability indicator: green check / red X / loader

**Icons used:** Globe, Copy, ExternalLink, Loader2, Save, Eye, EyeOff, CheckCircle, XCircle

# Plan: Career Page + JobListingCard

## Summary
Build two files for the candidate-facing public career page:
1. `src/components/career/JobListingCard.tsx` — presentational card
2. `src/pages/CareerPage.tsx` — public route at `/careers/:slug`
3. Register the route in `src/App.tsx`

## Pre-flight observations
- Stack: React 18 + Vite + React Router 6 + Tailwind + shadcn/ui + Supabase + React Query 5
- `date-fns` v3.6 is already installed → use `formatDistanceToNow` from `date-fns`
- Existing shadcn components: `Badge`, `Button`, `Card`, `Skeleton` — all present
- Pattern to follow: `Apply.tsx` — standalone header + footer, no SidebarLayout, no auth
- Route goes outside `MaintenanceGate` (same as `/apply/:jobId`) so candidates can always access it
- `career` subdirectory under `src/components/` does not exist yet — must create

## File 1: `src/components/career/JobListingCard.tsx`

Props interface as specified. Design:
- Outer: `Card` wrapper with `group` class for hover shadow (`hover:shadow-md transition-shadow duration-200`)
- Inner padding: `p-5` or `CardContent`
- Title: `text-lg font-semibold`
- Metadata row: conditional rendering, each item only shown when value is non-null/non-empty
  - MapPin + location
  - Building2 + department  
  - Clock + employment_type
  - DollarSign + salary_range
  - Each rendered as `flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground`
- Posted badge: `formatDistanceToNow(new Date(createdAt), { addSuffix: true })` → e.g. "3 days ago"
  - Rendered as small text or Badge variant="outline"
- Apply Now button: inline style `backgroundColor: brandColor` with `text-white` override
  - Use `Button` from shadcn but override bg via style prop since brandColor is dynamic
  - Hover: slight opacity reduction via `hover:opacity-90`

## File 2: `src/pages/CareerPage.tsx`

Structure (no auth, no sidebar):
1. `div.min-h-screen.bg-background.flex.flex-col` root
2. Sticky header: company logo (if exists) + company name
3. Hero section: colored background at `brandColor + '10'` (hex alpha append for light tint), company name h1, tagline, about, open positions count badge
4. Department filter pills (shown only if >1 department exists): "All" + each department name
5. Main content: grid of `JobListingCard` components
6. Footer: "Powered by Rankora" link

States to handle:
- `isLoading` → skeleton cards (3 skeleton cards matching the card height ~160px)
- `error || !data` → "Career page not found" centered + back link
- `filteredJobs.length === 0` (but page exists) → empty state: Briefcase icon + "No open positions right now" message
- Normal → full page

Data fetching via `supabase.rpc("get_career_page", { p_slug: slug })` — returns `{ config, jobs }`.

Types defined locally:
```typescript
interface CareerConfig {
  company_name: string;
  logo_url: string | null;
  brand_color: string;
  tagline: string | null;
  about: string | null;
}

interface CareerJob {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  location: string | null;
  department: string | null;
  employment_type: string | null;
  salary_range: string | null;
  created_at: string;
}
```

Department filter: local `useState<string | null>(null)` for selectedDept.

Apply handler: `navigate(\`/apply/\${jobId}\`)` — goes to existing Apply page.

SEO: `useEffect` sets document.title, cleans up on unmount.

## File 3: Register route in `src/App.tsx`

Add lazy import for `CareerPage` alongside `Apply`:
```typescript
const CareerPage = lazy(() => import("./pages/CareerPage"));
```

Add route outside `MaintenanceGate` (same position as Apply):
```tsx
<Route path="/careers/:slug" element={<CareerPage />} />
```

## Execution order
1. Create `src/components/career/JobListingCard.tsx`
2. Create `src/pages/CareerPage.tsx`
3. Edit `src/App.tsx` to add lazy import + route
4. Run `npm run build` to verify zero TypeScript errors

## Key decisions
- `brandColor` is a hex string (e.g. `#4F46E5`). For the hero tint, append `'1a'` (hex for 10% alpha) rather than `+ '10'` which would mean 16/255. Actually the spec says `brandColor + '10'` which in CSS hex means appending "10" as last two hex digits = ~6% opacity. That matches the spec literally so we use it as given.
- The Apply Now button uses `style={{ backgroundColor: brandColor }}` since Tailwind can't generate dynamic colors at runtime. Add `className="text-white hover:opacity-90"` to ensure contrast.
- Department filter buttons: use `<button>` elements styled with Tailwind, active state uses `brandColor` background via inline style for consistency.

# Postmark Email Integration Plan

## Context
The app currently has **no email sending capability**. Waitlist notifications are handled manually — merchants copy/paste emails or use mailto: links. The `ShopSettings.notificationEmail` field exists in the DB but is never used. The user has a Postmark account ready to test.

## Scope — 3 Email Flows

| # | Email | From → To | Trigger |
|---|-------|-----------|---------|
| 1 | **Waitlist Confirmation** | App → Customer | Customer joins waitlist via storefront widget |
| 2 | **Merchant Alert** | App → Merchant | Customer joins waitlist (if `notificationEmail` is set) |
| 3 | **ZIP Available** | App → Customer(s) | Merchant clicks Notify on waitlist page |

## Implementation Steps

### Step 1: Install Postmark + Add Env Vars
- `npm install postmark`
- Add to `.env`:
  ```
  POSTMARK_API_KEY=<server-token>
  POSTMARK_FROM_EMAIL=<verified-sender>
  ```

### Step 2: Create `app/email.server.ts` (new file)
Centralized email service following the `billing.server.ts` pattern:
- Lazy-init `ServerClient` from `postmark` package
- Graceful fallback: if `POSTMARK_API_KEY` not set, log warning and skip sends
- **4 exported functions**, all return `Promise<boolean>` (never throw):

| Function | Purpose |
|----------|---------|
| `sendWaitlistConfirmation(to, zipCode, shopName)` | Email to customer confirming waitlist signup |
| `sendMerchantWaitlistAlert(to, customerEmail, zipCode, shopName)` | Email to merchant about new signup |
| `sendZipAvailableNotification(to, zipCode, shopName, shopUrl)` | Email to customer that ZIP is now available |
| `sendTestEmail(to)` | Test email from settings page |

- Simple inline HTML templates with plain text fallbacks
- All wrapped in try/catch, log errors, return false on failure

### Step 3: Modify `app/routes/api.waitlist.tsx`
After the successful `db.waitlistEntry.upsert` (line 145), before the success response:
1. **Fire-and-forget** `sendWaitlistConfirmation()` to the customer
2. Look up `ShopSettings.notificationEmail` for this shop
3. If set, **fire-and-forget** `sendMerchantWaitlistAlert()` to merchant
- Email failures must NOT block the waitlist signup response

### Step 4: Modify `app/routes/app.waitlist.tsx`
Add actual email sending to all 3 notify actions:

**4a. `notify-zip` action (line 134-158)**
- After `updateMany`, call `sendZipAvailableNotification()` for each entry via `Promise.allSettled`
- Add `emailsSent` count to return value

**4b. `notify-selected-zips` action (line 161-193)**
- Same pattern — send to all waiting entries, return `emailsSent`

**4c. `bulk-action` notify (line 204-224)**
- Same pattern — send to all entries, return `emailsSent`

**4d. Update Notification Result Modal UI (line 1176-1248)**
- Show email delivery status: "Emails sent to X of Y customers"
- Keep copy/paste and mailto: as fallback options
- Update `notifyResult` state type to include optional `emailsSent` field

**4e. Update confirm modal text (line 1141-1174)**
- Change text from "you'll receive email addresses" to "emails will be sent automatically"

### Step 5: Modify `app/routes/app.settings.tsx`
- Add `send-test-email` intent to action handler
- Add "Send Test Email" button below the notification email field
- Show toast on success, banner on failure
- Need `shop` context to construct a nice test email body

### Step 6: Derive Shop Name
- Use `shop.replace(".myshopify.com", "")` for display name (simple, no extra API call)
- In `api.waitlist.tsx` the `shop` param is already available
- In `app.waitlist.tsx` get `shop` from `session.shop` in the action

## Files Changed

| File | Action |
|------|--------|
| `app/email.server.ts` | **Create** — Postmark email service |
| `app/routes/api.waitlist.tsx` | **Modify** — Add confirmation + merchant alert emails |
| `app/routes/app.waitlist.tsx` | **Modify** — Add email sending to notify actions + update UI |
| `app/routes/app.settings.tsx` | **Modify** — Add test email button |
| `.env` | **Modify** — Add POSTMARK_API_KEY and POSTMARK_FROM_EMAIL |
| `package.json` | **Modify** — via npm install postmark |

## Key Design Decisions
- **No queue** — Postmark handles delivery; sends happen in-action
- **Fire-and-forget** for public API (don't slow down widget response)
- **Awaited** for admin notify actions (merchant wants to see send count)
- **Graceful degradation** — email failure never breaks core functionality
- **No schema changes** — `ShopSettings.notificationEmail` already exists

## Verification
1. Run `npm run typecheck` after all changes
2. Test storefront waitlist signup → customer gets confirmation + merchant gets alert
3. Test Notify action from admin → customers get ZIP available email
4. Test with no POSTMARK_API_KEY set → app works normally, emails silently skipped
5. Test "Send Test Email" from settings page
# CROBOT — Production-Grade UI/UX Build Plan

## Context

CROBOT is an AI-powered CRO (Conversion Rate Optimization) audit tool. Users paste a URL and get an instant analysis of what's hurting their conversions with AI-generated suggestions. The current codebase has only the app shell built (~15-20% complete) — a dark sidebar, top bar, and placeholder pages. We need to build out all pages to production-grade quality matching Hotjar/Linear/Vercel, applying marketing psychology (urgency, social proof, premium feel, blurred premium content as upgrade triggers).

**Stack:** React 18 + TypeScript + Vite + Tailwind CSS + shadcn/ui (49 components ready) + React Router v6 + Recharts + React Hook Form + Zod + Lucide Icons

---

## Phase 0: Foundation Layer (Build First — Everything Depends on This)

### 0A. New File Structure
```
src/
  types/index.ts           — TypeScript interfaces (Scan, Finding, PillarResult, etc.)
  lib/constants.ts         — CRO pillars, score thresholds, pricing tiers, scan steps
  lib/score-utils.ts       — getScoreColor(), getScoreLabel(), getSeverityColor()
  lib/mock-data.ts         — Realistic mock data for all pages
  hooks/use-count-up.ts    — Animated number counter for KPI cards
  hooks/use-auth.ts        — Mock auth context (React Context + localStorage)
  components/shared/       — Reusable CROBOT-specific components:
    ScoreRing.tsx           — SVG circular progress (score-colored, animated)
    KpiCard.tsx             — 48px bold number + label + trend indicator
    SeverityBadge.tsx       — Critical/Warning/Info colored badges
    PillarProgressBar.tsx   — Horizontal bar with pillar label + score
    UrlInput.tsx            — 48px tall URL input + page type select + CTA button
    EmptyState.tsx          — Icon + title + description + CTA
    PremiumLock.tsx         — Blurred overlay with lock icon + "Upgrade" CTA
    PageHeader.tsx          — Page title (28px bold) + description + action button
    ScanProgressTimeline.tsx — Vertical 7-step animated timeline
    BrowserFrame.tsx        — Fake browser chrome for screenshot display
    FindingCard.tsx         — Individual audit finding with severity + recommendation
    PageTransition.tsx      — Fade-in animation wrapper
    AuthLayout.tsx          — Split-screen layout for login/signup
    ProtectedRoute.tsx      — Auth check wrapper
  components/landing/      — Landing page section components (8 files)
```

### 0B. Key Types (`src/types/index.ts`)
- `UserProfile` (id, email, full_name, plan, scans_used, scan_limit)
- `Scan` (id, url, page_type, status, overall_score, issues_found, timestamps)
- `CroPillar` union type (7 pillars)
- `Finding` (title, severity, current_state, recommendation, ai_suggestion, is_premium)
- `PillarResult` (scan_id, pillar, score, findings[])
- `PricingTier` (name, prices, features, highlighted)

### 0C. Constants (`src/lib/constants.ts`)
- `CRO_PILLARS` — 7 pillars with key, label, icon, description
- `SCORE_THRESHOLDS` — { poor: 40, average: 70 }
- `PRICING_TIERS` — Free/Pro/Agency data
- `SCAN_STEPS` — 7 analysis step labels
- `PLAN_LIMITS` — { free: 3, pro: 50, agency: 500 }

### 0D. Score Utils (`src/lib/score-utils.ts`)
- `getScoreColor(score)` → red (<40), amber (40-70), green (70+)
- `getScoreLabel(score)` → "Poor" / "Needs Work" / "Good" / "Excellent"
- `getSeverityColor(severity)` → red/amber/blue classes

### 0E. Mock Data (`src/lib/mock-data.ts`)
- 8-10 realistic scans with varied scores (35-92)
- Full pillar results for 3 scans with 3-5 findings each
- Dashboard stats aggregated from mock scans

### 0F. Animations (add to `index.css` + `tailwind.config.ts`)
- `fade-in` — opacity + translateY
- `scale-in` — scale + fade for cards
- `slide-up` — staggered list items
- `pulse-ring` — scan progress active indicator

### 0G. Shared Components (see descriptions above)
Most critical: **ScoreRing** (SVG circle, animated stroke-dashoffset) and **PremiumLock** (backdrop-blur overlay, key monetization UX)

**Files to modify:** `src/index.css`, `tailwind.config.ts`

---

## Phase 1: Dashboard Page

**Layout (top to bottom):**
1. PageHeader — "Welcome back!" + subtitle

- Centered card (max-w-2xl), UrlInput (48px), page type dropdown, "Start Analysis" full-width button

   - Free ($0) — basic

## Phase 5: Public Landing Page

# Pricing Page Cleanup — Remove Noise, Fix UX

## Context
The pricing page has several UX issues creating noise: duplicate downgrade paths, a test banner showing in production, redundant success banners, and the Free plan card being unclear. All functions work correctly — this is purely a cleanup pass.

## Issues Found & Fixes

### 1. Free plan card — no downgrade button needed
**Problem:** When on Free plan, the compact Free card shows but there's nothing to click. When on a paid plan, the "Downgrade to Free plan" link at the bottom is the correct path — but PlanCard ALSO has a "Downgrade to Free" button callback that's never rendered (Free is filtered from cards). This is fine but confusing in code.
**Fix:** The Free plan compact card is correct as-is. No change needed.

### 2. "Set Plan Manually" banner — hide in production
**Problem:** The testing banner (lines ~692-716) shows for ALL users, not just dev.
**Fix:** The action handler already checks `NODE_ENV`, but the UI renders unconditionally. Wrap the banner in a condition — only show when the app is in dev mode. Since we can't check `NODE_ENV` on the client, pass a `isDev` flag from the loader.

### 3. Success banner duplicates toast
**Problem:** Both a Banner AND a toast appear on successful plan change.
**Fix:** Remove the success Banner — keep only the toast (cleaner, less noise).

### 4. Current plan status banner — simplify
**Problem:** Complex conditional banner at top (info vs success) with upgrade action button is redundant since the cards already have upgrade buttons.
**Fix:** Replace with a simple one-line text showing current plan, no action button.

### 5. "Downgrade to Free plan" link at bottom — keep, it's correct
The bottom link is the single downgrade path. Keep as-is.

### 6. Trial footnote — only shows for free/starter, correct
No change needed.

## File: `app/routes/app.pricing.tsx`

### Changes:
1. **Loader** — Add `isDev: process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production"` to return value
2. **Remove** success Banner (keep toast only)
3. **Simplify** current plan status — one-line text, no action button
4. **Wrap** "Set Plan Manually" banner in `{isDev && (...)}`
5. **Remove** the current plan info banner at top (redundant with Free card + Active badges on cards)

### Net effect:
- Remove ~30 lines of conditional banner logic
- Gate test banner behind dev mode
- Cleaner top section: billing toggle → free card → paid cards → comparison

## Verification
- `npm run typecheck`
- Test as Free user: see Free card (no downgrade), 3 paid cards, comparison table, no test banner (prod)
- Test as Starter user: see Free card, 3 cards (Starter=Current), downgrade link, comparison
- Test as Ultimate: see Free card, 3 cards (Ultimate=Current), downgrade link

#### 2c. Action row: icon-only buttons + overflow dropdown
Show top 4 actions as icon-only buttons with TooltipProvider.
Conditional rendering: only show Call/WhatsApp if candidate_phone exists, Email if email exists, Schedule if jobId exists.
Always show "More" dropdown.

```
<div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-4">
  {/* Call — show if phone */}
  {r.candidate_phone && (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <Tooltip>
        <TooltipTrigger asChild>
          <Button variant="outline" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8"
            onClick={() => { navigator.clipboard.writeText(r.candidate_phone!); toast.success("Phone copied!"); }}>
            <Phone className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Button>
        </TooltipTrigger>
        <TooltipContent>Copy phone</TooltipContent>
      </Tooltip>
    </TooltipProvider>
  )}

  {/* WhatsApp — show if phone */}
  {r.candidate_phone && (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <Tooltip>
        <TooltipTrigger asChild>
          <Button variant="outline" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8 border-[#25D366]/30 text-[#25D366] hover:bg-[#25D366]/10"
            onClick={() => setWhatsappOpen(true)}>
            <MessageCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Button>
        </TooltipTrigger>
        <TooltipContent>WhatsApp</TooltipContent>
      </Tooltip>
    </TooltipProvider>
  )}

  {/* Email — show if email */}
  {r.candidate_email && (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <Tooltip>
        <TooltipTrigger asChild>
          <Button variant="outline" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8"
            onClick={() => setEmailDialogOpen(true)}>
            <Mail className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Button>
        </TooltipTrigger>
        <TooltipContent>Send email</TooltipContent>
      </Tooltip>
    </TooltipProvider>
  )}

  {/* Schedule — show if jobId */}
  {jobId && (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <Tooltip>
        <TooltipTrigger asChild>
          <Button variant="outline" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8"
            onClick={() => setScheduleOpen(true)}>
            <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Button>
        </TooltipTrigger>
        <TooltipContent>Schedule interview</TooltipContent>
      </Tooltip>
    </TooltipProvider>
  )}

  {/* More dropdown */}
  <DropdownMenu>
    <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
      <Button variant="outline" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8">
        <MoreHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
      </Button>
    </DropdownMenuTrigger>
    <DropdownMenuContent align="start">
      {r.file_path && (
        <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => onDownload(r.file_path!, r.filename)}>
          <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5 mr-2" />Resume
        </DropdownMenuItem>
      )}
      {linkedinUrl && (
        <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => window.open(linkedinUrl, "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer")}>
          <Linkedin className="h-3.5 w-3.5 mr-2" />LinkedIn
        </DropdownMenuItem>
      )}
      {jobId && teamId && (
        <DropdownMenuItem onClick={/* trigger AddToPoolButton logic — render AddToPoolButton inside, or extract onClick */}>
          <UserPlus className="h-3.5 w-3.5 mr-2" />Save to Pool
        </DropdownMenuItem>
      )}
      <DropdownMenuSeparator />
      {onStageChange && (
        <DropdownMenuItem
          className="text-destructive focus:text-destructive"
          onClick={() => setRejectDialogOpen(true)}>
          <UserX className="h-3.5 w-3.5 mr-2" />Reject
        </DropdownMenuItem>
      )}
      <DropdownMenuItem
        className="text-destructive focus:text-destructive"
        onClick={() => { onDismiss(r.resume_id); onClose(); }}>
        <Archive className="h-3.5 w-3.5 mr-2" />Archive
      </DropdownMenuItem>
    </DropdownMenuContent>
  </DropdownMenu>

  {/* Stage tracker — pushed right */}
  {onStageChange && (
    <div className="ml-auto">
      <StageTracker
        currentStage={currentStage || "applied"}
        updatedAt={stageUpdatedAt}
        onStageChange={(stage) => onStageChange(r.resume_id, stage)}
      />
    </div>
  )}
</div>
```

NOTE on AddToPoolButton: AddToPoolButton is a self-contained component with its own state. We cannot easily pull out its onClick. Instead, render it hidden in the overflow dropdown area and use a ref/wrapper, OR — simpler — render AddToPoolButton with `variant="ghost"` as a DropdownMenuItem child using `asChild` prop if supported. If not, add a local `saveToPool` boolean state and render AddToPoolButton conditionally. Simplest approach: render AddToPoolButton with a wrapper div inside DropdownMenuContent that looks like a menu item. 

Actually the cleanest approach: keep AddToPoolButton as-is in the overflow dropdown by wrapping it in a styled div that mimics DropdownMenuItem, and prevent the dropdown from closing on that click. Or even simpler: since AddToPoolButton has its own internal state (isInPool), just render it inside a `<div className="px-2 py-1.5">` inside the DropdownMenuContent. This avoids re-implementing its logic.

MustHaveSkillsGrid — inline component (no separate file needed, defined locally in CandidateDetailSheet):
```tsx
function MustHaveSkillsGrid({ matched = [], missing = [] }: { matched?: string[]; missing?: string[] }) {
  if (!matched.length && !missing.length) return null;
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
        Must-Have Skills
      </p>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {matched.map((skill) => (
          <span
            key={skill}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400 dark:border-emerald-800"
          >
            <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" />{skill}
          </span>
        ))}
        {missing.map((skill) => (
          <span
            key={skill}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 dark:bg-red-950/40 dark:text-red-400 dark:border-red-800"
          >
            <XCircle className="h-3 w-3" />{skill}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### 5. LOCAL HELPERS to define at top of file
```ts
function getInitials(name: string): string {
  return name
    .split(" ")
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((n) => n[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? "")
    .join("");
}

function scoreRingColor(score: number): string {
  if (score >= 70) return "#22c55e";
  if (score >= 40) return "#f59e0b";
  return "#ef4444";
}

function ScoreRingMini({ score }: { score: number }) {
  const size = 56;
  const strokeWidth = 5;
  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const clamped = Math.min(Math.max(score, 0), 100);
  const dash = (clamped / 100) * circumference;
  const color = scoreRingColor(score);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-flex items-center justify-center shrink-0" style={{ width: size, height: size }}>
      <svg width={size} height={size} className="-rotate-90">
        <circle
          cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius}
          fill="none" stroke="currentColor"
          strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
          className="text-muted/30"
        />
        <circle
          cx={size / 2} cy={size / 2} r={radius}
          fill="none" stroke={color}
          strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
          strokeDasharray={`${dash} ${circumference - dash}`}
          strokeLinecap="round"
        />
      </svg>
      <span className="absolute text-sm font-bold tabular-nums" style={{ color }}>
        {Math.round(score)}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## Key Tailwind decisions

| Element | Class |
|---|---|
| Header container | `px-6 pt-5 pb-4 border-b border-border bg-card shrink-0 relative` |
| Avatar circle | `h-12 w-12 rounded-full bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0` |
| Avatar initials | `text-primary text-base font-semibold` |
| Candidate name | `text-lg font-semibold text-foreground truncate` |
| Meta row | `flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5 flex-wrap` |
| Meta text | `text-xs text-muted-foreground` |
| Score+badge row | `flex items-center gap-4 mt-4` |
| Fit badge + confidence | `flex items-center gap-2` |
| Progress bar track | `h-1 w-40 rounded-full bg-muted overflow-hidden` |
| Action row | `flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-4` |
| Icon button | `h-8 w-8` (size="icon") |
| TabsList | `px-6 h-10 border-b border-border bg-transparent rounded-none justify-start gap-0 shrink-0` |
| TabsTrigger | `text-sm rounded-none border-b-2 border-transparent data-[state=active]:border-primary data-[state=active]:text-foreground text-muted-foreground px-3 h-10` |
| Tab content | `mt-0 px-6 py-5 space-y-5` |
| Must-have matched | `inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400 dark:border-emerald-800` |
| Must-have missing | `inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 dark:bg-red-950/40 dark:text-red-400 dark:border-red-800` |
| Section label | `text-[11px] font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider mb-2` |

## Accessibility notes
- Close button: add `aria-label="Close candidate detail"` 
- Icon-only action buttons: each has a Tooltip and the TooltipContent serves as the accessible label; also add `aria-label` prop to the Button
- Tabs: shadcn Tabs already includes role="tablist", role="tab", aria-selected — no extra work needed
- Must-have skill tags: they are presentational spans; add `aria-label` to the icon (CheckCircle2/XCircle) or wrap with sr-only text

# Workstream 2: Custom Application Form Builder + Knockout Questions + Auto-Ack Emails

## Summary

Build 3 integrated features for Rankora's application flow:
- **Feature A**: Visual form builder (recruiter adds custom fields per job)
- **Feature B**: Knockout questions (integrated into form builder — auto-disqualifies candidates)
- **Feature C**: Auto-acknowledgement emails sent to candidates on submission

---

## Files Read & Understood

| File | Key Findings |
|------|-------------|
| `src/pages/Apply.tsx` | Public apply page (no auth). Uses RPC `get_job_apply_info`. Fetches job, renders static fields (name, email, phone, linkedin, resume). Uses direct fetch to `submit-application` edge function with FormData. |
| `src/components/ApplicationsTab.tsx` | Realtime-enabled list of applications per job. Interface `Application` needs `custom_fields` and `auto_disqualified` added. Has filter summary and status select. |
| `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx` | Has `activeTab` state (`"resumes" \| "applications"`). Renders `<ApplicationsTab>` when applications tab active. Uses `JobMetadataSection` collapsible pattern — can mirror this for AutoAckSettings and FormBuilder. Uses `(supabase as any)` pattern for new columns. Has `Collapsible` imported. |
| `supabase/functions/submit-application/index.ts` | Handles FormData + JSON. Validates, uploads resume, inserts application. Need to add: `custom_fields` parsing, knockout logic, auto-ack email. |
| `supabase/functions/send-candidate-email/index.ts` | Auth-required email sending via Resend. Different pattern — auto-ack should be internal (no auth token needed, runs server-side in submit-application). |
| `supabase/functions/send-email/index.ts` | Template-based email via Resend. Has `sendViaResend` helper + platform_config fetch pattern. This is the model to follow for auto-ack inside submit-application. |
| `supabase/migrations/20260404000000_add_applications.sql` | Applications table: id, job_id, name, email, phone, linkedin_url, resume_path, resume_filename, status. Missing custom_fields, auto_disqualified, disqualify_reason. |

## What I'm Building

### New Files

2. `src/components/FormBuilder.tsx` — Visual form builder component
3. `src/components/AutoAckSettings.tsx` — Auto-ack email settings card

### Modified Files
4. `src/pages/Apply.tsx` — Add custom fields fetching + dynamic rendering + submit
5. `src/components/ApplicationsTab.tsx` — Show custom field responses + auto-disqualified badge + hide filter
6. `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx` — Add FormBuilder + AutoAckSettings sections to Applications tab area
7. `supabase/functions/submit-application/index.ts` — Add custom_fields, knockout logic, auto-ack email

---

## Data Flow

### Apply Page
```
Candidate opens /apply/:jobId
→ Fetch job info via get_job_apply_info RPC (anon safe)
→ Fetch custom fields via (supabase as any).from("job_form_fields").select("*").eq("job_id", jobId).order("sort_order") [anon policy allows this]
→ Render standard fields (name, email, phone, linkedin, resume)
→ Render dynamic custom fields below
→ On submit: collect custom_fields as { [field_id]: value }
→ Send as JSON field "custom_fields" in FormData (JSON stringified)
→ submit-application handles the rest
```

### Form Builder (Recruiter)
```
Recruiter opens job → Applications tab → sees FormBuilder section
→ Fetch job_form_fields for this job
→ Add/edit/delete/reorder fields
→ Each field: label, type, placeholder, required, options (for select), is_knockout, knockout_answer
→ Save: upsert to job_form_fields
```

### Auto-Ack Settings (Recruiter)
```
Recruiter opens job → Applications tab → sees AutoAckSettings section  
→ Fetch auto_ack_enabled, auto_ack_subject, auto_ack_body from jobs table
→ Toggle + edit subject/body
→ Save: update jobs table
```

### Submit Application Edge Function
```
Receive FormData
→ Parse custom_fields from form field (JSON string)
→ Insert application with custom_fields JSON
→ Query job_form_fields where is_knockout = true for this job
→ Check each knockout field against submitted custom_fields
→ If any match: UPDATE application SET auto_disqualified=true, status='rejected', disqualify_reason=...
→ Query job for auto_ack_enabled, auto_ack_subject, auto_ack_body
→ If auto_ack_enabled: send email via Resend (same sendViaResend pattern from send-email)
→ Return success
```

---

## Migration Strategy

Single migration file `20260408400000_add_custom_form_fields.sql`:
1. Create `job_form_fields` table with RLS (job owner + public read)
2. ALTER applications ADD COLUMN custom_fields jsonb
3. ALTER applications ADD COLUMN auto_disqualified boolean
4. ALTER applications ADD COLUMN disqualify_reason text
5. ALTER jobs ADD COLUMN auto_ack_enabled boolean DEFAULT true
6. ALTER jobs ADD COLUMN auto_ack_subject text DEFAULT '...'
7. ALTER jobs ADD COLUMN auto_ack_body text DEFAULT '...'

**RLS note**: The migration spec includes a `team_members` join for form field policy. The codebase has `useTeam` hook but I need to check if `team_members` table exists. If it doesn't, I'll simplify the policy to just `jobs.user_id = auth.uid()` to stay safe — matches the pattern used in the existing applications migration.

---

## Edge Cases

1. **Custom fields on Apply page with no fields defined** → renders nothing extra, form works as before
2. **Knockout field with no knockout_answer set** → skip knockout check for that field (defensive null check)
3. **select/multiselect custom fields** → options stored as text[] in DB, passed to frontend as array
4. **multiselect values** → stored as array of strings in custom_fields JSON: `{ [field_id]: ["option1", "option2"] }`
5. **checkbox knockout** → knockout_answer = "true" or "false" (stored as string), compare to String(value)
6. **auto_ack email failure** → non-fatal, logged to console, application still succeeds
7. **Resend API key not configured** → skip email silently, don't fail the application
8. **get_job_apply_info RPC** → doesn't return job_description currently. The custom fields fetch is a separate query, which is fine. The job_description was probably already in the apply page RPC or it's a mismatch — I'll ensure the custom fields query works independently.
9. **Anon policy on job_form_fields** → uses `USING (true)` for SELECT — allows any anon user to read all form fields. This is intentional (public apply page needs them).
10. **FormBuilder sort_order** → when adding a new field, default sort_order = max existing + 1

## Risks

**Risk 1**: `team_members` table may not exist — the spec's RLS policy joins it.  
**Mitigation**: Simplify RLS to just `jobs.user_id = auth.uid()` (matches existing applications policies). No team scoping for form fields.

**Risk 2**: `auto_ack` email uses Resend — needs `RESEND_API_KEY` and `platform_config.resend_api_key` to be set.  
**Mitigation**: Wrap in try/catch, skip silently if key missing. Same pattern as existing send-email function.

**Risk 3**: `job_form_fields` options column is `text[]` — Supabase JS client returns it as string[] in TypeScript but we're using `(supabase as any)`, so we need to handle it correctly.  
**Mitigation**: Type the fetched data locally in the component, access options as `field.options as string[] | null`.

**Risk 4**: Apply.tsx currently passes custom_fields as JSON string in FormData. Edge function needs to parse it.  
**Mitigation**: In edge function, use `JSON.parse(form.get("custom_fields") as string)` with try/catch, default to `{}` on parse error.

- Sonner toasts
- Apply page is PUBLIC — custom fields must work with anon policy
- ONE migration file only
- `npm run build` must pass with zero errors at the end

## Tab state via useSearchParams

```tsx
const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
const activeTab = searchParams.get("tab") ?? "overview";

function setActiveTab(tab: string) {
  setSearchParams({ tab }, { replace: true });
}
```

This makes `/team?tab=activity` work as a deep link.

```tsx
const { activities, isLoading: actLoading, isFetching: actFetching, hasMore, loadMore, error: actError } =
```

## Page Structure (exact implementation plan)

### Imports needed
```
useState, useEffect, useRef from react
useQuery from @tanstack/react-query
useAuth from @/hooks/useAuth
supabase from @/integrations/supabase/client
SidebarLayout from @/components/SidebarLayout
ChangePasswordCard from @/components/settings/ChangePasswordCard
Card, CardContent from @/components/ui/card
Button from @/components/ui/button
Input from @/components/ui/input
Label from @/components/ui/label
Textarea from @/components/ui/textarea
Separator from @/components/ui/separator
Skeleton from @/components/ui/skeleton
Badge from @/components/ui/badge
toast from sonner
Upload, X, Globe, Twitter, Linkedin, Github, Briefcase, MapPin, Crown, Zap, FileText from lucide-react
```

### State
```typescript
const [form, setForm] = useState({
  full_name: "", username: "", bio: "",
  avatar_url: "", job_title: "", company: "",
  location: "", website: "", twitter: "",
  linkedin: "", github: "",
});
const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
const [avatarUploading, setAvatarUploading] = useState(false);
const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
```

### Data load (useEffect on profile)
When `profile` loads (from useAuth), populate form state via `(profile as any).field_name ?? ""`.

### Stats query (React Query — same as current)
Query `jobs` and `usage_logs` tables for totalJobs, totalResumes, creditsUsed.

### Avatar upload handler
- Accept image/* files
- Upload to Supabase Storage bucket `avatars` at path `{user.id}/{filename}`
- Get public URL via `supabase.storage.from("avatars").getPublicUrl(path)`
- Update `form.avatar_url` + save to profiles immediately
- Show toast on success/error

### Avatar remove handler
- Delete from storage (best-effort)
- Set form.avatar_url = ""
- Save null to profiles immediately

### handleSave
```typescript
const handleSave = async () => {
  setSaving(true);
  try {
    const { error } = await (supabase as any)
      .from("profiles")
      .update({ ...form })
      .eq("user_id", user!.id);
    if (error) throw error;
    await refreshProfile();
    toast.success("Profile updated");
  } catch {
    toast.error("Failed to save profile");
  } finally {
    setSaving(false);
  }
};
```

### handleCancel
Reset form to current profile values (same as the useEffect on profile).

## Input Prefix Addon Pattern
```tsx
<div className="flex rounded-md border border-input focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-ring focus-within:ring-offset-0 overflow-hidden">
  <span className="flex items-center px-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground bg-muted border-r border-input shrink-0 select-none">
    twitter.com/
  </span>
  <input
    className="flex-1 h-9 px-3 py-1 text-sm bg-background outline-none"
    value={form.twitter}
    onChange={(e) => setForm(f => ({ ...f, twitter: e.target.value }))}
    placeholder="handle"
  />
</div>
```
(This avoids shadcn Input breaking the visual grouping — raw input inside the styled wrapper.)

---

## Migration File
```sql
-- 20260410200000_profile_fields.sql
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS full_name text;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS username text;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS bio text;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS avatar_url text;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS job_title text;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS company text;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS location text;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS website text;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS twitter text;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS linkedin text;
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS github text;
```

1. Write migration file
2. Write new Profile.tsx
3. Run `npm run build` — verify zero errors
4. Check no `types.ts` was touched

## Constraints
- `(supabase as any)` for new columns not in generated types
- Never edit `src/integrations/supabase/types.ts`
- `@/` imports only
- shadcn-ui components only (Input, Label, Textarea, Button, Separator, Card, Badge, Skeleton)
- Sonner toasts only
- SidebarLayout wrapper with `w-full px-6 sm:px-8 lg:px-10 py-8`
- Zero TypeScript errors

---

## Risk Assessment

Risk 1: Avatar storage bucket `avatars` may not exist in Supabase
  Mitigation: Wrap upload in try-catch with specific error message; user sees toast. No crash.

Risk 2: New profile columns don't exist in DB until migration runs — `(supabase as any)` update will silently do nothing
  Mitigation: Migration file is delivered alongside the code. Document that it must be applied first.

Risk 3: `refreshProfile()` in useAuth only fetches the original hard-coded columns — new fields won't be in `profile` after save
  Mitigation: Form state is the source of truth for display. `refreshProfile()` is called for credit/plan sync only. New columns read directly from a separate `useQuery` fetch on the page, not from the auth profile context.

  Actually simpler: Load the full profile row via a dedicated React Query on the page (separate from useAuth), pre-populate form from it. This avoids touching useAuth entirely.

### Updated data load strategy
```typescript
const { data: fullProfile } = useQuery({
  queryKey: ["profileFull", user?.id],
  queryFn: async () => {
    const { data } = await (supabase as any)
      .from("profiles")
      .select("full_name, username, bio, avatar_url, job_title, company, location, website, twitter, linkedin, github")
      .eq("user_id", user!.id)
      .single();
    return data;
  },
  enabled: !!user,
});

useEffect(() => {
  if (fullProfile) {
    setForm({
      full_name: fullProfile.full_name ?? "",
      // ... etc
    });
  }
}, [fullProfile]);
```

After save, invalidate `["profileFull", user?.id]` so the form reloads from DB truth.

# Plan: Pricing Plan Dialog with Feature Comparison Table

## Task
Rewrite `src/components/SubscriptionDialog.tsx` to a premium pricing modal with feature comparison table.

## Call Sites (must not break)
1. `src/components/settings/AccountInfoCard.tsx` — uses `trigger` prop (2 instances)
2. `src/components/BuyCreditsDialog.tsx` — uses `trigger` prop (1 instance)

## Props Strategy
The task says `open/onOpenChange` but all 3 existing call sites use `trigger`. Solution: support both via union/optional props:
```tsx
interface SubscriptionDialogProps {
  trigger?: React.ReactNode;
  open?: boolean;
  onOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void;
}
```
When `open` is provided externally, use it (controlled). When only `trigger` is provided, use internal state (uncontrolled). This is zero-breaking-change.

## Layout Plan

### Dialog: max-w-5xl, max-h-[90vh], overflow hidden
- **Header row**: "Choose your plan" title + Monthly/Annual toggle with Switch + "Save 20%" badge
- **Plan cards**: 4-column grid (Free, Starter, Growth [Popular], Agency)
- **Feature comparison table**: scrollable section below cards, sticky column headers

### Plan Card Data (static, not from DB for display — DB only for checkout)
```
Free     $0   /mo  — baseline
Starter  $29  /mo  — 
Growth   $79  /mo  — POPULAR (highlighted)
Agency   $149 /mo  — 
```
Annual: multiply by 0.8 (20% off), show "Save 20%" badge on toggle.

CTA logic:
- Current plan → "Current Plan" button, disabled
- Higher plan → "Upgrade" button → triggers checkout via `handleSubscribe(plan.id)`
- Lower plan → "Downgrade" button → triggers confirm dialog

### Plan Card Layout (per card)
```
[Plan Name]  [Popular badge if applicable]
$XX /mo (or /yr)  [annual shows discounted]
Key limits (2-3 lines)
[CTA Button]
```

### Feature Comparison Table
- Sticky thead with plan name columns
- Feature name column: `flex-1` wide
- Plan columns: `w-[100px]` each, `text-center`
- Category rows: full-width section dividers, `bg-muted/50`, bold text
- Cell values:
  - `true` → `<CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" />`
  - `false` → `<span className="text-muted-foreground/40">—</span>`
  - string → `<span className="font-medium text-sm">{value}</span>`

### Checkout Logic (preserved from current)
- `handleSubscribe(planId)` → `supabase.functions.invoke("create-dodo-checkout", { body: { plan_id: planId, return_url: ... } })`
- On success: redirect to `data.checkout_url`
- On error: `toast.error()`

### Cancel / Downgrade logic (preserved from current)
- Keep `handleCancelSubscription()` and `handleDowngradeToPayg()`
- Keep `AlertDialog` for confirmation
- Current plan status banner preserved

### Annual Toggle
```tsx
const [annual, setAnnual] = useState(false);
const getPrice = (monthly: number) => annual ? Math.round(monthly * 0.8) : monthly;
```

## Feature 1: AI Resume Authenticity Scoring

### New Files
- `supabase/functions/check-authenticity/index.ts`
- `src/components/candidate/AuthenticityBadge.tsx`
- `src/components/candidate/AuthenticityReport.tsx`

### Modified Files
- `supabase/migrations/20260410100000_automation_and_features.sql` (adds `authenticity_json` to results + other tables)
- `src/components/CandidateRow.tsx` — add `AuthenticityBadge` next to score
- `src/components/CandidateDetailSheet.tsx` — add `AuthenticityReport` + "Check Authenticity" button

### Edge Function Pattern
Copy from `rewrite-jd/index.ts`:
- Same CORS `getCorsOrigin`, same ALLOWED_ORIGINS
- Auth via `supabase.auth.getClaims(token)` (not getUser)
- Input: `{ resume_text: string, candidate_name: string }`
- NO credit deduction (spec says so)
- Calls GPT-4o with the specified system prompt
- Returns `{ authenticity_score, risk_level, flags, details, summary }`

### AuthenticityBadge
- Props: `score: number | null, riskLevel: string | undefined`
- ShieldCheck (green, score ≥ 80): "Verified"
- ShieldAlert (amber, 60-79): "Review"  
- ShieldX (red, < 60): "Risk"
- null: just nothing or grey "–"
- Wrapped in `TooltipProvider` showing score + flags on hover
- Very compact — fits in CandidateRow next to existing score bar

### AuthenticityReport
- Props: `resumeId: string, jobId: string`
- React Query to fetch `(supabase as any).from("results").select("authenticity_json").eq("resume_id", resumeId).single()`
- Parse `authenticity_json` — if null show "Run Check" state
- Score ring (circular SVG), risk level badge, flags list, details breakdown
- "Run Check" button calls `supabase.functions.invoke("check-authenticity", { body: { resume_text, candidate_name } })`
- Then calls `(supabase as any).from("results").update({ authenticity_json: result }).eq("resume_id", resumeId)` to persist
- Loading state during check

### CandidateRow integration
- Add `authenticityScore?: number | null` and `authenticityRisk?: string` to `CandidateRowProps`
- Render `<AuthenticityBadge score={authenticityScore} riskLevel={authenticityRisk} />` after the score column

### CandidateDetailSheet integration
- Add `AuthenticityReport` section after the existing panels
- Add "Check Authenticity" button in the action buttons row
- Need to pass `resumeId` and `jobId` (already available as `r.resume_id` and `jobId` prop)

## Feature 5: AI-Powered Natural Language Search

### New Files
- `supabase/functions/ai-search/index.ts`
- `src/components/AICandidateSearch.tsx`

### Edge Function
- Auth required, no credit deduction
- Input: `{ query: string, job_id?: string, user_id: string }`
- GPT-4o call to parse query → structured filters JSON
- Then query `resumes` table: `parsed_text` ILIKE for skills, location; `candidate_name` ILIKE for names
- If `job_id`, also join `results` to include scores
- Return array of matching candidates with `{ id, filename, candidate_name, job_id, overall_score?, skills[] }`

### AICandidateSearch Component
- Props: `jobId?: string`
- A search bar with "AI" label/sparkle icon
- Debounced input — on submit (Enter or click Search) calls the edge function
- Shows results in a Popover/Dialog: candidate name, job name (if cross-job), score
- Clicking a result navigates to that candidate (or calls an `onSelectCandidate` callback)
- Loading spinner during search
- Empty state: "No candidates found matching your query"
- Error state with retry

### Integration in JobDetailView
- Add `<AICandidateSearch jobId={selectedJobId} />` above the results table, inside the results section
- Add global search in sidebar footer (optional, smaller scope — just within JobDetailView for now)

Note on FK: spec uses `REFERENCES public.teams(id)` which exists. `is_team_member` and `is_team_admin` are confirmed to exist from earlier migrations.

## Key Type Decisions

### CandidateRow additions
```typescript
interface CandidateRowProps {
  // ...existing...
  blindMode?: boolean;
  authenticityScore?: number | null;
  authenticityRisk?: string | null;
}
```

### Result type — no change needed
`authenticity_json` will be fetched separately via `(supabase as any)` cast; it won't be in the main Result type from Index.tsx.

### TalentPool candidate shape
```typescript
interface PoolCandidate {
  id: string; // talent_pool.id
  resume_id: string;
  original_job_id: string | null;
  notes: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  resume: { filename: string; candidate_name: string | null; parsed_json: any };
  job: { title: string | null } | null;
  result: { overall_score: number } | null;
}
```

---

## Risk Assessment

### Risk 1: `teams` table — solo users have no team
- Many users won't have a `team_id`. 
- Talent pool and stage automations require `team_id` FK.
- Mitigation: For solo users without a team, disable the Talent Pool UI with a prompt to create a team first. The AddToPoolButton checks `teamId !== null` before rendering.

### Risk 2: CandidateDetailSheet — adding blindMode changes PII display
- Passing `blindMode` into the sheet requires knowing the candidate's rank index.
- Mitigation: Pass `blindModeIndex?: number` alongside `blindMode` so the sheet shows "Candidate #X" correctly. Get the index from the results array in JobDetailView.

### Risk 3: authenticity_json column — casting
- The column isn't in auto-generated types.
- Mitigation: Use `(supabase as any)` for all reads/writes of `authenticity_json` — consistent with existing SEO pattern in CLAUDE.md.

---

## What Does NOT Change
- No modifications to `src/integrations/supabase/types.ts`
- No changes to payment/billing logic
- No changes to RLS policies on existing tables (only new tables get new RLS)
- Auth/CORS pattern in edge functions copied exactly from rewrite-jd

# Plan: ZIP Upload + Candidate Tags + Candidate Assignment

## Feature Summary
Three independent but related features added to the Rankora resume ranking app:
1. **ZIP Upload** — Users can upload a .zip file containing multiple resumes; the app extracts them client-side using jszip, adds them to the file queue, and uploads them like normal files.
2. **Candidate Tags** — Free-form per-candidate tags (stored in DB) that appear inline on CandidateRow and in the detail sheet. Users can add/remove tags from the candidate row and detail sheet.
3. **Candidate Assignment** — Assign a team member to a candidate (stored in DB). The assigned member shows inline on CandidateRow. Requires team feature.

---

## Step-by-Step Plan

### Step 1: Install jszip
```bash
npm install jszip
```
Verify: `npm run build` passes.

### Step 2: Add migration
File: `supabase/migrations/20260410000000_candidate_tags_and_assignments.sql`

```sql
-- Candidate tags: many tags per resume, per job context
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS candidate_tags (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  resume_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES resumes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  job_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES jobs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  tag TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(tag) BETWEEN 1 AND 50),
  created_by UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS candidate_tags_resume_job_tag_uidx
  ON candidate_tags(resume_id, job_id, lower(tag));

ALTER TABLE candidate_tags ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Users can manage tags for their jobs" ON candidate_tags
  USING (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM jobs j WHERE j.id = candidate_tags.job_id AND j.user_id = auth.uid()
    )
    OR
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM team_members tm
      JOIN jobs j ON j.id = candidate_tags.job_id
      WHERE tm.user_id = auth.uid() AND tm.team_id = j.team_id
    )
  )
  WITH CHECK (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM jobs j WHERE j.id = candidate_tags.job_id AND j.user_id = auth.uid()
    )
    OR
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM team_members tm
      JOIN jobs j ON j.id = candidate_tags.job_id
      WHERE tm.user_id = auth.uid() AND tm.team_id = j.team_id
    )
  );

-- Candidate assignments: one assignee per (resume, job)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS candidate_assignments (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  resume_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES resumes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  job_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES jobs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  assigned_to UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  assigned_by UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id),
  assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS candidate_assignments_resume_job_uidx
  ON candidate_assignments(resume_id, job_id);

ALTER TABLE candidate_assignments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Users can manage assignments for their jobs" ON candidate_assignments
  USING (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM jobs j WHERE j.id = candidate_assignments.job_id AND j.user_id = auth.uid()
    )
    OR
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM team_members tm
      JOIN jobs j ON j.id = candidate_assignments.job_id
      WHERE tm.user_id = auth.uid() AND tm.team_id = j.team_id
    )
  )
  WITH CHECK (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM jobs j WHERE j.id = candidate_assignments.job_id AND j.user_id = auth.uid()
    )
    OR
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM team_members tm
      JOIN jobs j ON j.id = candidate_assignments.job_id
      WHERE tm.user_id = auth.uid() AND tm.team_id = j.team_id
    )
  );
```

```typescript
// Uses (supabase as any) for the new table

```

- Tags and assignment editors open in the detail sheet, not inline in the row (too compact)

### Step 10: Wire tags + assignment into `CandidateDetailSheet.tsx`
- Pass jobId + resumeId
- Render `CandidateTagsEditor` in the detail sheet
- Render `CandidateAssignMenu` in the detail sheet header area

### Step 11: Wire everything into `JobDetailView.tsx`
- Call `useCandidateTags(selectedJobId)` → `tagsMap`
- Call `useCandidateAssignments(selectedJobId)` → `assignmentsMap`
- Pass `tags={tagsMap.get(r.resume_id) ?? []}` to CandidateRow
- Pass `assignedTo={assignmentsMap.get(r.resume_id)?.assigned_to ?? null}` to CandidateRow
- Pass hooks down to detail sheet via props or re-query inside sheet
