## Files to Create
1. `supabase/migrations/20260410000000_candidate_tags_and_assignments.sql`
2. `src/hooks/useCandidateTags.ts`
3. `src/hooks/useCandidateAssignments.ts`
4. `src/components/candidate/CandidateTagsEditor.tsx`
5. `src/components/candidate/CandidateAssignMenu.tsx`

## Files to Modify
1. `src/components/BulkUploadZone.tsx` — ZIP support
2. `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx` — ZIP in regular upload, wire tags/assignment
3. `src/components/CandidateRow.tsx` — show tag chips + assignee
4. `src/components/CandidateDetailSheet.tsx` — full tags/assignment editors
5. `package.json` (via npm install jszip)

## TypeScript / Safety Rules
- `(supabase as any)` for new tables (not in generated types.ts)
- Never edit `src/integrations/supabase/types.ts`
- Graceful fallback pattern (same as useStageTracker): catch `42P01` / "does not exist" errors → return []
- All hooks use `retry: false` on query
- Zero `any` on function params/returns — use proper typed interfaces

## Risks and Mitigations
1. **jszip bundle size** — jszip is ~90KB gzipped. Import dynamically inside the zip handler function (`const JSZip = (await import('jszip')).default`) to avoid bloating the initial bundle.
2. **ZIP with non-resume files** — Filter by extension inside zip extraction. Skip files > maxFileSizeMb. Show toast: "Extracted X resumes, skipped Y non-resume files."
3. **New tables not in Supabase types** — Use `(supabase as any)` consistently. Never touch types.ts.
4. **CandidateRow grows too wide** — Tags are inline chips capped at 3, overflow hidden behind "+N". Assignment is a small avatar icon. No layout breakage.
5. **Team feature not enabled** — Assignment feature gracefully hides when `team` is null/undefined from `useTeam()`.

# Plan: Custom Pipeline Stages + Structured Evaluation Scorecards

## Task
Build two features for Rankora:
- Feature A: Custom Pipeline Stages (per-team configurable Kanban stages)
- Feature B: Structured Evaluation Scorecards (template-based candidate scoring)

## Context Verified
- Stack: React 18.3 + Vite + TypeScript + shadcn/ui + React Query 5 + Supabase
- Dev port: 8080
- `(supabase as any)` cast for new tables (not in types.ts)
- Sonner toasts only
- `@/` imports throughout
- `is_team_member()` and `is_team_admin()` SQL functions already exist
- Teams table and team_members table already exist with owner/admin/member roles
- KanbanBoard already accepts `stages: string[]` prop — partially stage-agnostic
- StageTracker component is tightly coupled to hardcoded STAGE_ORDER
- `effectiveStageCounts` in JobDetailView is hardcoded to 6 fixed stages
- CandidateDetailSheet has section ordering: StageTracker → Notes → AI panels
- Team page at `/team` (src/pages/Team.tsx) — good place to add PipelineStageManager
- Migration file will be: `supabase/migrations/20260408800000_custom_stages_and_scorecards.sql`

## What Exists (Do Not Break)
- `src/hooks/useStageTracker.ts` — exports `HiringStage`, `STAGE_ORDER`, `useStages`, `useUpdateStage`, `useStageSummary`
- `src/components/KanbanBoard.tsx` — already accepts `stages: string[]`, STAGE_CONFIG is internal
- `src/components/candidate/StageTracker.tsx` — imports `STAGE_ORDER` from useStageTracker, renders dots
- `src/components/CandidateRow.tsx` — has `STAGE_BADGE_CLASSES` hardcoded map
- `src/pages/SharedPortal.tsx` — has `STAGE_LABELS` and `STAGE_COLORS` hardcoded
- `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx` — uses `STAGE_ORDER` for kanban, `effectiveStageCounts` hardcoded
- Existing migration files through `20260408700000_enhance_career_settings.sql`

## Risk Assessment
1. Risk: Breaking `StageTracker.tsx` and `CandidateDetailSheet.tsx` which use `HiringStage` type
   Mitigation: Keep `HiringStage` type and `STAGE_ORDER` in useStageTracker.ts as-is (backward compat). New `usePipelineStages` hook is additive only.

2. Risk: `effectiveStageCounts` in JobDetailView is hardcoded to 6 fixed stage names
   Mitigation: Update it to derive counts from custom stages dynamically; keep "rejected" special.

3. Risk: KanbanBoard's mobile `<select>` uses `STAGE_CONFIG[s]?.label` to get display labels  
   Mitigation: Add `stageConfig` prop to KanbanBoard for display overrides; keep fallback to capitalize.

## Implementation Plan (Step-by-Step)

### Step 1: DB Migration
Create `supabase/migrations/20260408800000_custom_stages_and_scorecards.sql`
- `pipeline_stages` table (id, team_id FK teams, name, color, sort_order, is_default, created_at)
- RLS: team members SELECT, team admins ALL
- Seed default stages for existing teams
- `scorecard_templates` table (id, team_id FK teams, name, criteria JSONB, created_at)
- RLS: team members SELECT, team admins ALL
- `scorecard_responses` table (id, template_id FK, resume_id FK, job_id FK, evaluator_id FK auth.users, scores JSONB, overall_comment, recommendation CHECK, created_at, updated_at, UNIQUE(template_id, resume_id, evaluator_id))
- RLS: team members of the job SELECT, authenticated INSERT (evaluator_id = auth.uid()), evaluator UPDATE own

### Step 2: usePipelineStages hook
Create `src/hooks/usePipelineStages.tsx`
- Query pipeline_stages for teamId ordered by sort_order
- Append synthetic "rejected" entry (not stored in DB, always last)
- If no custom stages (or null teamId), return DEFAULT_STAGES as fallback
- Mutations: createStage, updateStage, deleteStage, reorderStages
- Realtime subscription on pipeline_stages table

Types exported:
```typescript
export interface PipelineStage {
  id: string;
  team_id: string;
  name: string;
  color: string;
  sort_order: number;
  is_default: boolean;
}
export const DEFAULT_STAGES: PipelineStage[] = [...] // matches hardcoded STAGE_ORDER
```

### Step 3: useScorecardTemplates hook
Create `src/hooks/useScorecardTemplates.tsx`
- Query scorecard_templates for teamId
- Mutations: createTemplate, updateTemplate, deleteTemplate

Types:
```typescript
export interface ScorecardCriterion { id: string; label: string; description: string; max_score: number; }
export interface ScorecardTemplate { id: string; team_id: string; name: string; criteria: ScorecardCriterion[]; created_at: string; }
```

### Step 4: useScorecardResponses hook
Create `src/hooks/useScorecardResponses.tsx`
- Query scorecard_responses for resumeId + jobId
- Mutations: submitResponse (upsert), updateResponse

Types:
```typescript
export type Recommendation = 'strong_yes' | 'yes' | 'neutral' | 'no' | 'strong_no';
export interface ScorecardResponse { id: string; template_id: string; resume_id: string; job_id: string; evaluator_id: string; scores: Record<string, { score: number; comment?: string }>; overall_comment?: string; recommendation?: Recommendation; }
```

### Step 5: PipelineStageManager component
Create `src/components/PipelineStageManager.tsx`
Props: `teamId: string`

UI:
- Header: "Pipeline Stages" title + "Add Stage" button
- List of stages: colored dot, name, reorder (up/down arrows), edit, delete
- Add/edit inline form: name input + color picker (6 preset colors as swatches)
- Cannot delete if isDefault or name === "Rejected" or total ≤ 2
- On delete: confirm AlertDialog
- Save reorder on each up/down click (immediate mutation)
- Uses usePipelineStages hook

### Step 6: Update KanbanBoard.tsx
Add optional `stageConfig` prop:
```typescript
interface StageDisplayConfig {
  label: string;
  color?: string; // hex
}
interface KanbanBoardProps {
  // existing props...
  stageConfig?: Record<string, StageDisplayConfig>;
}
```
- getStageConfig(): first checks `stageConfig` prop, falls back to hardcoded STAGE_CONFIG, then generic fallback
- Mobile `<select>` option labels use stageConfig prop labels
- Keep STAGE_CONFIG as internal fallback (backward compat, no removal)

### Step 7: Update StageTracker.tsx

```typescript
interface StageTrackerProps {
  currentStage: string; // was HiringStage — widen to string

  onStageChange: (stage: string) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;

```
- If `customStages` provided, iterate those instead of STAGE_ORDER
- Keep HiringStage import for backward compat
- currentStage widened to string (still accepts HiringStage values)

### Step 8: Update JobDetailView.tsx

### Step 11: ScorecardPanel component

UI:
- Header: "Evaluations" with count badge

- If no templates: "No scorecard templates yet. Create one in Team Settings." link
- For selected template, show:
  - Each criterion: label + description + star rating (1-5, or up to max_score) + optional comment textarea
  - Overall comment textarea
  - Recommendation radio: Strong Yes / Yes / Neutral / No / Strong No (color coded)
  - Submit/Update button (disabled while pending)
- If current user already submitted: load their existing response pre-filled (editable)
- Uses useScorecardTemplates + useScorecardResponses hooks

Create `src/components/candidate/ScorecardSummary.tsx`
Props: `resumeId: string, jobId: string, compact?: boolean`

- Compact: "N evaluated" badge with recommendation breakdown as colored dots
- Full:
  - "X of Y team members evaluated" header

## Data Flow

### Custom Stages Flow:
Team page → PipelineStageManager → usePipelineStages → pipeline_stages table
JobDetailView → usePipelineStages(team.id) → stages list
→ KanbanBoard (stages=[...stageNames], stageConfig={name→label+color})
→ StageTracker (customStages=[{name, label}])
candidate_stages.stage column stores raw stage name text (already freeform)

### Scorecard Flow:
Team page → ScorecardTemplateManager → useScorecardTemplates → scorecard_templates table
CandidateDetailSheet → ScorecardPanel → useScorecardTemplates + useScorecardResponses
→ scorecard_responses table (UNIQUE constraint prevents duplicates per evaluator per resume per template)
CandidateDetailSheet → ScorecardSummary (aggregate view)

## Edge Cases Handled
- No team: usePipelineStages returns DEFAULT_STAGES (hardcoded fallback = current behavior)
- No custom stages in DB: same fallback
- "Rejected" is always synthetic (never in pipeline_stages table, always appended)
- Cannot delete below 2 stages (guard in PipelineStageManager)
- Scorecard template has no criteria: UI shows empty state inside ScorecardPanel
- Evaluator submits scorecard twice: UNIQUE constraint in DB → upsert handles update
- CustomStages unknown in StageTracker: widened to string, dots render for each custom stage

## Acceptance Criteria
- [ ] Team admins can create/edit/delete/reorder pipeline stages from Team page
- [ ] KanbanBoard uses custom stage names and colors when team has custom stages
- [ ] StageTracker renders custom stages (not hardcoded 5 stages) when custom stages provided
- [ ] Default fallback still works for users without teams or without custom stages
- [ ] Team admins can create scorecard templates with criteria from Team page
- [ ] Evaluators can submit scorecards from CandidateDetailSheet (ScorecardPanel)
- [ ] ScorecardSummary shows aggregated scores per candidate
- [ ] npm run build exits with 0 errors

## Integration Details

### JD Generator Dialog onGenerated callback
In new-job mode: `onGenerated={(jd) => setJobDescription(jd)}`

### CSV Import onImportComplete
Need to refetch `existingResumes`. In `JobDetailView`, after import, call the same refetch logic used elsewhere — likely `queryClient.invalidateQueries` or re-trigger the existing resumes fetch. The existing resumes come from Index.tsx via props. The `onJobUpdated` callback can trigger a refetch.

Simpler approach: after import completes, the component can call `window.location.reload()` — no, too heavy. Better: pass a refresh callback prop or use the existing `setExistingResumes` to update state. The CSVImportDialog inserts rows to the resumes table, so after complete we should re-fetch from DB and update existingResumes via setExistingResumes.

For the import complete handler in JobDetailView:
```typescript
const handleCSVImportComplete = useCallback(async () => {
  if (!selectedJobId) return;
  const { data } = await supabase.from("resumes")
    .select("id, filename, file_path, candidate_name, created_at")
    .eq("job_id", selectedJobId)
    .order("created_at", { ascending: false });
  if (data) setExistingResumes(data);
}, [selectedJobId, setExistingResumes]);
```

Fallback: If category_scores_json is not present, don't render sub-score bars.

## Feature A: KanbanBoard Component

### New file: `src/components/KanbanBoard.tsx`

#### Props interface
```typescript
interface KanbanBoardProps {
  jobId: string;
  candidates: Array<{
    id: string;         // resume_id
    name: string;
    score: number | null;
    stage: string;
    email?: string;
  }>;
  onStageChange: (resumeId: string, newStage: string) => void;
  stages: string[];     // STAGE_ORDER + "rejected"
}
```

#### Column order
Use `[...STAGE_ORDER, "rejected"]` = `["applied", "screened", "interview", "offer", "hired", "rejected"]`

#### Column color tints (subtle background per stage)
- applied → `bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-900/30` 
- screened → `bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20`
- interview → `bg-violet-50 dark:bg-violet-900/20`
- offer → `bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-900/20`
- hired → `bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-900/20`
- rejected → `bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20`

#### Drag behavior
- `draggable="true"` on KanbanCard
- `onDragStart`: `e.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", candidate.id)`
- `onDragOver`: `e.preventDefault()` + set `dragOverColumn` state
- `onDragLeave`: clear `dragOverColumn` state
- `onDrop`: read `e.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain")`, call `onStageChange(resumeId, stage)`
- Drop target column: dashed border + subtle highlight when `dragOverColumn === stage`
- Dragging card: `opacity-50` via CSS `active:opacity-50` or `dragging` state

#### Score badge colors
- >= 80 → emerald
- >= 60 → amber  
- < 60 → red

#### Mobile
- Horizontal scroll: `flex overflow-x-auto gap-3 pb-4` on the columns container

#### KanbanCard subcomponent
- Shows: name (bold, truncate), score badge, "6 dots" drag indicator (GripVertical icon)
- `cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing`

## Feature B: BulkUploadZone Component

### New file: `src/components/BulkUploadZone.tsx`

#### Props interface
```typescript
interface BulkUploadZoneProps {
  jobId: string;
  userId: string;
  onUploadComplete: () => void;
  maxFiles?: number;       // default 200
  maxFileSize?: number;    // MB, default 5
  disabled?: boolean;
}
```

#### State
```typescript
type FileStatus = "pending" | "uploading" | "done" | "failed";
interface UploadFile {
  file: File;
  status: FileStatus;
  error?: string;
}
const [uploadFiles, setUploadFiles] = useState<UploadFile[]>([]);
const [isDragOver, setIsDragOver] = useState(false);
const [isCancelled, setIsCancelled] = useState(false);
const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
const cancelRef = useRef(false);
```

#### Drop zone
- Large zone: min-h-[160px], dashed border, icon + text
- Accept: `.pdf,.docx,.doc,.rtf,.txt`
- `multiple` attribute on file input
- webkitdirectory NOT used — just multi-file select
- On drop: validate + set files with "pending" status
- On file select: same

#### Upload queue execution
```
startUpload() {
  Process files in parallel batches of 5:
  - Split files into chunks of 5
  - For each chunk: await Promise.all(chunk.map(uploadSingleFile))
  - Check cancelRef.current before each batch
  - Update status per file as it moves through states
}

uploadSingleFile(file: UploadFile) {
  setFileStatus(file, "uploading")
  try {
    filePath = `${userId}/${jobId}/${Date.now()}_${file.file.name}`
    await supabase.storage.from("resumes").upload(filePath, file.file)
    await (supabase as any).from("resumes").insert({
      job_id: jobId,
      filename: file.file.name,
      file_path: filePath,
      status: "pending",
      candidate_name: file.file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ""),
    })
    setFileStatus(file, "done")
  } catch {
    setFileStatus(file, "failed")
  }
}
```

#### Progress tracking
- `uploadedCount = files.filter(f => f.status === "done").length`
- `failedCount = files.filter(f => f.status === "failed").length`
- Overall progress bar: `(done + failed) / total * 100`
- Counter label: "Uploaded 47 of 200 resumes"

#### File list display
- Scrollable list (max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto)
- Per file: filename | size | status icon (spinner for uploading, check for done, X for failed)
- Failed files show retry button (re-queues that file)

#### Cancel
- Sets `cancelRef.current = true`
- Stops processing next batches
- Shows "Upload cancelled" toast

#### On complete
- All files done/failed → toast.success("All resumes uploaded") + call onUploadComplete()

#### Validation per file
- Size > maxFileSize → skip with toast
- Wrong extension → skip
- Deduplicate by filename within batch

---

## Feature B Integration into JobDetailView

### Changes to `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx`

The existing upload zone (lines ~1219-1306 inside the "Upload Resumes" Card) handles up to `maxResumes` files (default 10). 

The task says to "replace or augment" for existing jobs when no ranking has been done yet. However, given the existing `maxResumes` cap (from platform config, typically 10), we need to think carefully.

**Decision**: Keep existing upload for new jobs (limited by maxResumes). Add BulkUploadZone as a **separate card** that only appears for existing jobs, below the existing "Add More Resumes" card. This preserves all existing behavior and doesn't break the ranking pipeline. The BulkUploadZone is for pre-loading 200+ resumes before ranking — it uses `maxFiles=200` overriding the per-ranking limit.

Actually re-reading the spec: "Replace or augment the existing file upload area in JobDetailView with BulkUploadZone when no ranking has been done yet (before the 'Rank' button is pressed)."

**Revised decision**: For existing jobs with NO ranked results yet (`results.length === 0 && existingResumes.length === 0`), show `BulkUploadZone` prominently instead of the small upload zone. For existing jobs that already have results, keep the existing small upload zone.

But we need to be careful: for NEW jobs (not yet created), the existing upload flow is part of the ranking UX. Don't replace that.

**Final approach**:
- New job mode: keep existing upload zone (unchanged)
- Existing job with no resumes yet: show BulkUploadZone as primary upload method (replaces "Add More Resumes" card)
- Existing job with resumes: show existing small "Add More Resumes" card + optionally a toggle to BulkUploadZone

**Simpler approach (less risk of breaking things)**:
- Add a "Bulk Upload" button in the "Add More Resumes" card header that toggles a BulkUploadZone modal/expanded section
- OR: Show BulkUploadZone when the upload count would exceed maxResumes

Given the spec says "up to 200+ files" and the platform config maxResumes is likely 10, we need BulkUploadZone to bypass the per-job-ranking limit and just pre-load resumes into the DB for later ranking. The `maxFiles=200` here is about storage, not the ranking limit.

**Implementation plan**:
1. For existing jobs: Add a second "Bulk Upload" button/section in the upload card
2. When user clicks it, show BulkUploadZone expanded in place
3. Keep existing single-file upload for normal use
4. onUploadComplete: reload existingResumes query

The BulkUploadZone's `maxFiles` prop defaults to 200, independent of `maxResumes` (which governs ranking, not pre-loading).

For `onUploadComplete`, we need to refresh `existingResumes`. In JobDetailView, `existingResumes` is passed as a prop and populated from the Index.tsx query. We'll call `queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["resumes", selectedJobId] })` or use the existing pattern.

Looking at Index.tsx, `existingResumes` is fetched there and passed down. We can add a `onResumesAdded` callback prop, OR simply call the existing result fetch mechanism. The simpler approach: pass the supabase query invalidation or a refresh callback.

Actually, looking at existing code: in `handleRankExisting`, after upload it does:
```typescript
const { data: allResumes } = await supabase.from("resumes").select(...).eq("job_id", selectedJobId)
if (allResumes) setExistingResumes(allResumes);
```

So we can pass `setExistingResumes` down to BulkUploadZone's `onUploadComplete` callback which re-fetches resumes.

**Simplest clean approach**: Add a `refreshExistingResumes` callback as a prop or define it in JobDetailView and pass to BulkUploadZone. Inside that callback:
```typescript
const { data: allResumes } = await supabase.from("resumes").select("id, filename, file_path, candidate_name, created_at").eq("job_id", selectedJobId).order("created_at", { ascending: false });
if (allResumes) setExistingResumes(allResumes);
```

This is already the exact pattern used in handleRankExisting.

## Risk Assessment

**Risk 1**: KanbanBoard DnD events might conflict with existing drag behavior in JobDetailView (existing `handleFileDrop` on the upload zone)
- Mitigation: KanbanBoard is a completely separate component below the upload zone. DnD events don't bubble between unrelated elements. No conflict.

**Risk 2**: BulkUploadZone bypasses the `maxResumes` per-job ranking limit
- Mitigation: BulkUploadZone is for pre-loading resumes into DB only — not triggering ranking. The ranking enforces `maxResumes` at rank time. Pre-loading more resumes is valid (they show as "pending" in existingResumes). This is correct behavior by design.

**Risk 3**: Pipeline tab shows empty state when no results exist — confusing UX
- Mitigation: Show empty state message "No ranked candidates yet. Run ranking to see the pipeline." inside KanbanBoard when candidates array is empty.

**Risk 4**: `activeTab` type extension breaking the reset logic
- Mitigation: The reset in `useEffect([selectedJobId])` already sets `setActiveTab("resumes")` — changing the union type doesn't affect runtime behavior there.

- Top Jobs by Applications: Table with Job Title, Applications, Avg Score, Status
- Loading: Skeleton matching card + chart shapes
- Empty states for all sections

Data fetch strategy (all `(supabase as any)` casts):
- `jobs` table: `select('id, title, status, created_at').eq('user_id', user.id)`
- `resumes` table joined to jobs for candidate counts and source
- `results` table for scores
- All aggregated client-side (no new edge function needed — data volumes are small for per-user reporting)

The "Avg Time-to-Hire" will be computed from `candidate_stages` if it exists, otherwise show "—" (graceful fallback).

Charts use Recharts patterns identical to `DashboardTab.tsx`: `ResponsiveContainer`, `tooltipStyle` from CSS vars, same color tokens.

---

## Key Design Decisions

**Reports data fetching:**
- Query keys: `["user_reports", userId]` for all stats, `["user_reports_jobs", userId]` for job list
- Single query fetches all jobs + nested resumes + results for the user
- Funnel stages: derive from `candidate_stages` table if it exists, otherwise static counts from results (ranked=Screened, hard_fit=Interview, placeholder for rest)
- Source: try `resumes.parsed_json->>'source'` — if null, default to "Direct Upload"
- All computation client-side, no edge functions

**AgencyHub tabs:**
- Tabs are purely static / presentational
- No data fetching, no state beyond active tab
- `defaultValue="client-management"` on Tabs

**Sidebar "Agency" placement:**
- In `settingsNav` (not mainNav) since it's a feature-tier upsell, not a core navigation item
- Insert after the existing `Code2` API entry

**Chrome icon fallback:**
- Lucide has `Chrome` icon. If build fails, fallback to `Puzzle` (extension metaphor).

---

## Risk Assessment

Risk 1: `candidate_stages` table might not exist → funnel chart falls back to graceful zeros/skeleton with "No stage data" empty state. Never throws.

Risk 2: `BarChart3` and `Building2` already exist in lucide-react (confirmed in DashboardTab and Integrations imports).

Risk 3: Adding two routes while `App.tsx` already has 15+ routes — no naming conflicts. Both new paths (`/reports`, `/agency`) don't exist yet.

## Type Definitions

```typescript
interface Tag {
  id: string;
  resume_id: string;
  job_id: string;
  tag: string;
  color: string;
  created_by: string | null;
  created_at: string;
}
```

## Data Flow Notes

**Tags:**
- useCandidateTags(jobId) → tagsByResume Map<resumeId, Tag[]>
- In CandidateRow: `const myTags = tagsByResume.get(r.resume_id) ?? []` → show first 3
- In KanbanBoard: extend candidate objects with `tags: tagsByResume.get(c.id) ?? []`
- In CandidateDetailSheet: pass full tags for that resume + onAdd/onRemove callbacks

**Assignee:**
- team.members from useTeam() gives us the list
- Need to fetch assigned_to for each resume — can add to the result merge or a separate query
- AssigneeSelector updates resumes.assigned_to directly → need a query invalidation callback
- In JobDetailView: maintain assigneeMap: Map<resumeId, string|null> as state, updated when AssigneeSelector changes

**Assignee fetch strategy:**
- Since resumes are already queried (existingResumes), we can add assigned_to to that select 
- Or: fetch from the results query — but results come from the results table, not resumes
- Best approach: after results load, fetch assigned_to for all resume_ids in one query via supabase
- OR simpler: add `assigned_to` to the existingResumes select in JobDetailView → propagate through allResults

## Risk Assessment

1. **Risk: KanbanBoard's KanbanCandidate interface needs extension** — resolved by adding optional tag/assignee props
2. **Risk: assigned_to not in existing resume queries** — resolved by adding to handleBulkUploadComplete and the main existingResumes fetch
3. **Risk: JSZip dynamic import in addFiles which is sync** — addFiles needs to become async OR zip handling extracted to a separate async function before calling addFiles with the extracted files

## ZIP Upload Key Decision
The drop handler is sync, but JSZip loading is async. Strategy:
- In BulkUploadZone: create a new `handleFilesWithZip` async helper
- Called from handleDrop and handleFileSelect INSTEAD of addFiles
- It separates zip files from non-zip files, processes zips async, then calls addFiles with combined results
- addFiles itself stays sync (no change needed)
# Plan: Rankora — ZIP Upload + Candidate Tags + Assignee

## Feature 1: ZIP Upload in BulkUploadZone

### What needs to change
- `package.json` — add `jszip` dependency (but the task says run `npm install jszip` first)
- `src/components/BulkUploadZone.tsx` — accept `.zip` files, extract resume files from ZIP using JSZip dynamic import, add extracted files to queue, toast "Extracted X resumes from ZIP", update UI text

### Key implementation details
- ACCEPTED_TYPES must include `"application/zip"`, `"application/x-zip-compressed"` (browsers send different MIME types for ZIP)
- ACCEPTED_EXTENSIONS must include `,.zip`
- In `addFiles()`, detect ZIP files and handle async extraction before adding to queue
- Need to convert `addFiles` to async, or handle ZIPs as a special async path before calling regular `addFiles`
- Pattern: for each ZIP, use `const JSZip = (await import('jszip')).default;`, load the file as ArrayBuffer, then iterate `zip.files`, filter by resume extension, generate File objects from blob, call regular file validation/add logic
- Toast: `toast.success(\`Extracted ${count} resumes from ZIP\`)`
- UI drop zone text: mention ZIP in the format list

### Risk: JSZip creates `Blob` objects from zip entries — need to construct `new File([blob], name, {type})` with correct MIME types based on extension

---

## Feature 2: Candidate Tags

### 2a. Migration
File: `supabase/migrations/20260409200000_candidate_tags_and_assignment.sql`
Content is exactly as specified in the task — no changes needed.

### 2b. Hook: `src/hooks/useCandidateTags.tsx`
- Fetches all tags for a job_id via `(supabase as any).from('candidate_tags').select('*').eq('job_id', jobId)`
- Groups tags by resume_id for O(1) lookup: `Map<resumeId, CandidateTag[]>`
- `addTag(resumeId, jobId, tag, color)` mutation — inserts into candidate_tags, toast success
- `removeTag(tagId)` mutation — deletes by id, toast success
- React Query with queryKey `['candidate-tags', jobId]`
- Realtime subscription on `candidate_tags` filtered by `job_id=eq.${jobId}` → invalidate query on INSERT/DELETE/UPDATE
- Cleanup subscription on unmount
- Export: `CandidateTag` type, `useCandidateTags(jobId)` hook returning `{ tagsByResumeId, addTag, removeTag, isLoading }`

### 2c. Component: `src/components/candidate/CandidateTagInput.tsx`
Props: `resumeId: string`, `jobId: string`, `tags: CandidateTag[]`, `onAdd: (tag, color) => void`, `onRemove: (tagId) => void`

- Preset tags: `['Shortlisted', 'On Hold', 'Top Choice', 'Follow Up', 'Culture Fit']`
- Color palette: 6 hex colors (indigo, emerald, amber, rose, sky, violet)
- Displays existing tags as colored chips with X button
- Input with autocomplete dropdown showing presets + already-used tags in this job (from all tags)
- Enter/comma to confirm a tag
- Max ~10 tags per candidate
- No extra UI libraries — just shadcn Popover + Input

### 2d. Component: `src/components/candidate/AssigneeSelector.tsx`
Props: `resumeId: string`, `teamMembers: TeamMember[]`, `currentAssigneeId?: string | null`, `onAssign: (userId: string | null) => void`

- Select dropdown (shadcn Select) with team member names/initials
- Shows "Unassigned" as default option
- On change: calls `(supabase as any).from('resumes').update({ assigned_to: userId }).eq('id', resumeId)` then invalidate resumes query
- Shows small avatar/initials badge next to the selector when assigned

### 2e. Integration points

**CandidateDetailSheet.tsx**
- Import `useCandidateTags`, `CandidateTagInput`, `AssigneeSelector`
- Add `teamMembers?: TeamMember[]` to props
- In scrollable panels section (after StageTracker, before CandidateNotesPanel): add Tags section + Assignee section
- Call `useCandidateTags(jobId)` inside the component (already has jobId)
- For AssigneeSelector, need to call `useTeam()` inside or receive teamMembers from parent

**CandidateRow.tsx**
- Import `CandidateTag` type
- Add props: `tags?: CandidateTag[]`, `assigneeName?: string | null`  
- After the candidate name/location in the cell, render small tag chips (max 3, then "+N more")
- After name: show assignee badge (small circle with initials) if assigned

**KanbanBoard.tsx**
- `KanbanCandidate` interface needs `tags?: CandidateTag[]`, `assigneeName?: string | null`
- In `KanbanCard`: below email, render tag chips (max 2) and assignee badge

**JobDetailView.tsx**
- Call `useCandidateTags(selectedJobId)` hook
- Pass `tagsByResumeId.get(r.resume_id) ?? []` as `tags` prop to `CandidateRow`

# ConvertScan MVP — Make It Fully Functional

## Context
The app is architecturally complete (frontend, edge functions, DB schema, billing code all exist). However the entire scan pipeline is blocked by missing environment secrets and a few code gaps. This plan wires everything up so the MVP runs end-to-end: URL in → AI analysis → scored report → billing upgrade.

---

## What's Already Built (Don't Touch)
- `analyze-url` edge function — full 11-step orchestration ✅
- `stripe-checkout`, `stripe-webhook`, `stripe-portal` edge functions ✅
- `pagespeed` edge function ✅
- `send-email` edge function ✅
- All frontend pages + hooks ✅
- Database schema + RLS ✅

---

## Blockers to Fix

### 1. Missing Supabase Edge Function Secrets (CRITICAL)
All of these must be set via `supabase secrets set` on the Supabase dashboard or CLI:

| Secret | Where to Get | Priority |
|--------|-------------|----------|
| `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` | console.anthropic.com | 🔴 Blocking — AI won't run |
| `SCREENSHOTONE_ACCESS_KEY` | screenshotone.com (free tier: 100/mo) | 🔴 Blocking — no screenshots |
| `PAGESPEED_API_KEY` | console.cloud.google.com → PageSpeed Insights API | 🟡 Falls back to 65/100 |
| `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` | dashboard.stripe.com → Developers → API Keys | 🟡 Billing broken |
| `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` | Stripe → Webhooks → signing secret | 🟡 Billing broken |
| `STRIPE_PRICE_PRO_MONTHLY` | Create product in Stripe | 🟡 Billing broken |
| `STRIPE_PRICE_PRO_ANNUAL` | Create product in Stripe | 🟡 Billing broken |
| `STRIPE_PRICE_AGENCY_MONTHLY` | Create product in Stripe | 🟡 Billing broken |
| `STRIPE_PRICE_AGENCY_ANNUAL` | Create product in Stripe | 🟡 Billing broken |
| `RESEND_API_KEY` | resend.com | 🟢 Non-fatal, email silent-fails |
| `SITE_URL` | Production URL of the app | 🟡 Needed for Stripe redirects |

### 2. Screenshot Service Gap
The `analyze-url` function checks `SCREENSHOT_SERVICE_URL` (Railway Puppeteer) first, then falls back to `SCREENSHOTONE_ACCESS_KEY`. For MVP, use ScreenshotOne (no infra needed). The fallback code is already at line 358 of `analyze-url/index.ts` — just needs the API key as a secret.

### 3. Supabase Storage Bucket Missing
The edge function uploads screenshots to a `screenshots` bucket. This bucket needs to be created in Supabase Storage with public read access.

### 4. Edge Functions Not Deployed
All 5 edge functions need to be deployed to the Supabase project `hyxlmmkrbipufoqkkhba`.

### 5. Free Scan Flow (Landing Page)
Currently the landing page URL input redirects to `/scan` which requires auth. Per the strategy doc, the MVP free scan should require creating a free account (Google OAuth — frictionless). The scan page already handles the free tier limit (3 scans/month). No code change needed — just ensure the CTA on Landing.tsx routes to `/signup?redirect=/scan` not just `/scan`.

### 6. Stripe Products Setup
Need to create 4 products in Stripe dashboard:
- Pro Monthly ($49)
- Pro Annual ($39 × 12 = $468)  
- Agency Monthly ($199)
- Agency Annual ($159 × 12 = $1,908)

**File**: `src/pages/Landing.tsx`  
**Change**: The "Start Free Scan" / "Analyze Free" buttons should route to `/signup` (not `/scan`) for unauthenticated users, which then redirects to `/scan` after auth.

Currently the URL input on landing likely just links to `/scan`. We need to check auth state and redirect properly.

**Logic**:
```tsx
// In Landing.tsx URL input submit handler:
if (!user) {
  navigate(`/signup?redirect=/scan&url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`);
} else {
  navigate(`/scan?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`);
}
```

And in `Scan.tsx`, pre-fill the URL input from `?url=` query param.

### Step 5: Code Fix — Pre-fill Scan URL from Query Param
**File**: `src/pages/Scan.tsx`  
**Change**: Read `url` from URL query params and pre-populate the input field.

```tsx
const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
const initialUrl = searchParams.get('url') ?? '';
// Set as default value in the URL input field
```

In Stripe Dashboard → Webhooks → Add endpoint:
- URL: `https://hyxlmmkrbipufoqkkhba.supabase.co/functions/v1/stripe-webhook`
- Events: `checkout.session.completed`, `customer.subscription.updated`, `customer.subscription.deleted`, `invoice.payment_failed`

### Step 7: Scan Limit Display Fix
Verify that `src/components/AppSidebar.tsx` correctly shows remaining scans from `profile.scan_limit - profile.scans_used`. The sidebar already has this but confirm it reads live data.

# Production-Grade Shopify App Restructure Plan

## Context
The app is a Shopify Zip Code Checker (React Router 7 + Polaris + Prisma + SQLite).
It was bootstrapped from the official Shopify app template but still contains
template leftovers, is missing Shopify App Store mandatory GDPR webhooks, has
over-privileged OAuth scopes, and has a broken .gitignore for its SQLite file.
Goal: clean up all template artifacts, add missing mandatory compliance items,
fix dependency classification errors, and make the app submission-ready.

---

## Full File/Folder Audit

### Current Structure (clean — keep as-is)
```
app/
  billing.server.ts       ✅ billing logic
  db.server.ts            ✅ Prisma singleton
  entry.server.tsx        ✅ SSR entry
  globals.d.ts            ✅ global type declarations
  plans.ts                ✅ plan constants & limits
  root.tsx                ✅ HTML shell
  routes.ts               ✅ React Router route manifest
  shopify.server.ts       ✅ Shopify app init + billing config
  routes/
    app.tsx               ✅ layout + nav (Polaris + AppProvider)
    app._index.tsx        ✅ Dashboard
    app.zip-codes.tsx     ✅ Zip code manager
    app.delivery-rules.tsx ✅ Delivery rules
    app.waitlist.tsx      ✅ Customer waitlist
    app.widget.tsx        ✅ Widget customisation
    app.settings.tsx      ✅ Settings & billing
    app.pricing.tsx       ✅ Pricing / upgrade
    auth.$.tsx            ✅ OAuth catch-all
    auth.login/           ✅ Login page
    webhooks.app.scopes_update.tsx      ✅ Mandatory
    webhooks.app.uninstalled.tsx        ✅ Mandatory
```

### Files to DELETE
| File | Reason |
|------|--------|
| `app/routes/app.additional.tsx` | Template boilerplate "About" page with links to Shopify dev docs. Not in the nav. No real user value. Should be removed entirely. |

### No unwanted folders found
All directories serve a purpose. `extensions/` with `.gitkeep` is correct — Shopify CLI requires it for workspace configuration.

---

## Issues & Required Changes

### 1. CRITICAL — Missing GDPR Mandatory Webhooks
Shopify **requires** these 3 webhooks for any App Store submission. Without them the app will be rejected during review.

**Add these 3 new route files:**
- `app/routes/webhooks.customers.data_request.tsx`
- `app/routes/webhooks.customers.redact.tsx`
- `app/routes/webhooks.shop.redact.tsx`

**Update `shopify.app.toml`** to register them:
```toml
[[webhooks.subscriptions]]
topics = [ "customers/data_request" ]
uri = "/webhooks/customers/data_request"

[[webhooks.subscriptions]]
topics = [ "customers/redact" ]
uri = "/webhooks/customers/redact"

[[webhooks.subscriptions]]
topics = [ "shop/redact" ]
uri = "/webhooks/shop/redact"
```

### 2. CRITICAL — Template Demo Artifacts in shopify.app.toml
The `shopify.app.toml` contains two sections that are copy-pasted from the Shopify app template and have nothing to do with a Zip Code Checker:

**Remove entirely:**
```toml
[product.metafields.app.demo_info]   ← "Demo Source Info / Tracks products created by the Shopify app template"
[metaobjects.app.example]            ← "An example metaobject definition created by this template"
```
These create unnecessary metafield/metaobject definitions in every merchant's store.

### 3. CRITICAL — Over-Privileged OAuth Scopes
Current scopes: `write_products, write_metaobject_definitions, write_metaobjects`

The entire app stores data in its **own** Prisma database — no Shopify API calls to products or metaobjects exist anywhere in the codebase. These scopes are completely unused.

Shopify's app review will flag over-privileged scopes as a policy violation.

**Fix in `shopify.app.toml`:**
```toml
[access_scopes]
scopes = ""    # or remove line entirely; app needs no Shopify data scopes
```
If the storefront widget public API is added (see #6), it needs no scopes either.

### 4. IMPORTANT — .gitignore Missing dev.db Entry
Current `.gitignore` has `prisma/dev.sqlite` but the actual database file is `prisma/dev.db`. This means **dev.db is currently committed to git**, which is a security/data risk.

**Fix `.gitignore`:**
```
prisma/dev.db
prisma/dev.db-journal
prisma/*.db
prisma/*.db-journal
```

### 5. IMPORTANT — @prisma/client in devDependencies
`@prisma/client` is in `devDependencies` but is required at **runtime** in production (imported by `db.server.ts`). The Docker/production build will fail without it in `dependencies`.

**Fix `package.json`:** move `@prisma/client` from `devDependencies` → `dependencies`.

### 6. IMPORTANT — No Public API Endpoint for Storefront Widget
The app has a full `WidgetConfig` model and widget customisation UI, but there is **no public API route** for the storefront to actually check zip codes. Without this, the widget cannot function.

**Add:** `app/routes/api.zip-check.tsx` — unauthenticated GET/POST endpoint:
- Accepts: `?shop=store.myshopify.com&zip=12345`
- Returns: JSON `{ allowed: true/false, message, eta, zone }`
- Uses `unauthenticated.public` from shopify.server
- Enforces CORS headers for storefront cross-origin requests

### 7. MINOR — Template Artifacts in package.json
- `"author": "codespace"` → update to real author/company name
- `@shopify/plugin-cloudflare` in `trustedDependencies` → template artifact, this app uses Node.js/Docker not Cloudflare; remove it

### 8. MINOR — API Version Inconsistency
- `shopify.app.toml` webhooks: `api_version = "2026-01"`
- `shopify.server.ts`: `ApiVersion.October25` (= `2025-10`)

**Fix:** Align both to `2026-01` / `ApiVersion.January26` in `shopify.server.ts`.

### 9. MINOR — Template CHANGELOG.md & README.md
Both files contain generic Shopify template boilerplate. Replace with app-specific content describing Zip Code Checker.

---

## Summary of All Changes

### DELETE (1 file)
- `app/routes/app.additional.tsx`

### MODIFY (5 files)
| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `shopify.app.toml` | Remove demo metafield + example metaobject; remove unused scopes; add 3 GDPR webhook subscriptions |
| `package.json` | Move `@prisma/client` to dependencies; update author; remove cloudflare trustedDependency |
| `.gitignore` | Add `prisma/dev.db` and `prisma/*.db-journal` |
| `app/shopify.server.ts` | Align API version to January26 (2026-01) |
| `CHANGELOG.md` / `README.md` | Replace with app-specific content |

### ADD (4 files)
| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `app/routes/webhooks.customers.data_request.tsx` | GDPR: customer data request (mandatory for App Store) |
| `app/routes/webhooks.customers.redact.tsx` | GDPR: customer data deletion (mandatory for App Store) |
| `app/routes/webhooks.shop.redact.tsx` | GDPR: shop data deletion after uninstall (mandatory for App Store) |
| `app/routes/api.zip-check.tsx` | Public API for storefront widget zip lookup |

---

## Production Database Note
SQLite is fine for development but NOT for production (no concurrent writes, single file).
For App Store deployment, migrate to **PostgreSQL**:
- Change `prisma/schema.prisma` provider from `sqlite` → `postgresql`
- Set `DATABASE_URL` to a real Postgres connection string
- Run `prisma migrate deploy`
This is a deployment config change, not a code change.

2. `npm run lint` — no linting errors
3. `npm run dev` — app starts, dashboard loads, nav works (no /additional link should be accessible)
4. Verify GDPR webhooks are registered in Shopify Partners dashboard after `shopify app deploy`
5. Verify access scopes show correctly in Partners dashboard (no product/metaobject scopes)
6. Test public API: `GET /api/zip-check?shop=test.myshopify.com&zip=12345` returns JSON
7. Shopify App Store pre-submission checklist: https://shopify.dev/docs/apps/launch/app-requirements

# ConvertScan (CROBOT) -- Production Build Plan

## Context

CROBOT is an AI-powered CRO (Conversion Rate Optimization) audit tool where users paste a URL and get instant, specific suggestions to improve conversions. The frontend is 95% complete (13 pages, 50+ components, React 18 + TypeScript + Vite + shadcn/ui), but **the entire backend is missing** -- all data is mock. This plan turns the prototype into a production-grade SaaS.

**Product**: Paste URL -> AI analyzes screenshot + DOM + performance -> scored report with 7 CRO pillars -> AI-rewritten alternatives
**Stack**: Vite React (frontend) + Supabase (auth + DB + edge functions + storage) + Railway (Puppeteer screenshots) + Claude API (AI analysis) + Stripe (billing) + Resend (email)

---

## Phase 1: Supabase Foundation (Auth + DB + Storage)

### 1.1 Install & Configure Supabase Client
- Install `@supabase/supabase-js`
- **Create** `src/lib/supabase.ts` -- client init from `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` + `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`
- **Create** `.env.example` with all required env vars
- **Create** `.gitignore` entry for `.env.local`

### 1.2 Database Schema (SQL Migrations)
- **Create** `supabase/migrations/001_schema.sql`:
  - `profiles` table (id, email, full_name, company_name, website_url, avatar_url, plan, scans_used, scan_limit, stripe_customer_id, stripe_subscription_id, billing_cycle_end, created_at)
  - `scans` table (id, user_id, url, favicon_url, page_title, page_type, status, overall_score, issues_found, critical_issues, warnings, quick_wins, screenshot_url, device, error_message, created_at, completed_at)
  - `scan_results` table (id, scan_id, pillar, score, weight, benchmark, findings JSONB, created_at)
  - `integrations` table (id, user_id, provider, access_token, is_active, connected_at)
- **Create** `supabase/migrations/002_rls.sql` -- RLS policies: users can only access their own data
- **Create** `supabase/migrations/003_triggers.sql` -- Auto-create profile on auth.users INSERT

### 1.3 Auth System
- **Create** `src/contexts/AuthContext.tsx` -- wraps `supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange`, provides user/profile/signIn/signUp/signOut
- **Create** `src/hooks/use-auth.ts` -- convenience hook
- **Create** `src/components/ProtectedRoute.tsx` -- redirects to /login if unauthenticated
- **Modify** `src/App.tsx` -- wrap in AuthProvider, protect dashboard routes
- **Modify** `src/pages/Login.tsx` -- real `supabase.auth.signInWithPassword()` + Google OAuth
- **Modify** `src/pages/Signup.tsx` -- real `supabase.auth.signUp()` + Google OAuth

### 1.4 Wire Auth to Layout
- **Modify** `src/components/TopBar.tsx` -- replace `MOCK_USER` with real profile from useAuth
- **Modify** `src/components/AppSidebar.tsx` -- replace `MOCK_USER` with real profile, real scan usage

---

## Phase 2: Replace Mock Data with Real Queries

### 2.1 React Query Hooks
- **Create** `src/hooks/use-scans.ts` -- `useScans()`, `useScan(id)`, `useCreateScan()`, `useDeleteScan()`
- **Create** `src/hooks/use-scan-results.ts` -- `useScanResults(scanId)`
- **Create** `src/hooks/use-dashboard-stats.ts` -- aggregation queries for KPIs
- **Create** `src/hooks/use-profile.ts` -- `useProfile()`, `useUpdateProfile()`

### 2.2 Wire Pages to Real Data
- **Modify** `src/pages/Dashboard.tsx` -- replace `MOCK_DASHBOARD_STATS`, `MOCK_SCANS`, `MOCK_SCORE_DISTRIBUTION`, `MOCK_SCORE_TREND`, `MOCK_USER`, `MOCK_PILLAR_RESULTS` with real hooks
- **Modify** `src/pages/Reports.tsx` -- replace `MOCK_SCANS` with `useScans()`
- **Modify** `src/pages/AuditReport.tsx` -- replace `MOCK_SCANS`, `MOCK_PILLAR_RESULTS` with `useScan(id)` + `useScanResults(id)`

### 2.3 Loading & Error States
- **Create** `src/components/shared/PageSkeleton.tsx` -- skeleton loaders
- Add loading/error handling to all pages using React Query states

---

## Phase 3: Scan Engine (Core Product -- The Money Feature)

### 3.1 Railway Puppeteer Screenshot Service
- **Create** `services/screenshot/` directory:
  - `index.ts` -- Express server: `POST /screenshot` accepts URL, captures full-page PNG, scrapes DOM (headings, CTAs, images, meta, links, text), returns base64 screenshot + structured DOM data
  - `Dockerfile` -- Puppeteer-compatible image for Railway
  - `package.json`
- Handles edge cases: cookie banners, popups, lazy loading, SPAs
- Auth via `X-API-Key` header

### 3.2 Supabase Edge Functions

#### `supabase/functions/analyze-url/index.ts` (Orchestrator)
Pipeline:
1. Validate URL + auth + scan limits
2. Create scan record (status: 'pending')
3. Update status to 'analyzing'
4. Call Railway screenshot service -> get screenshot + DOM data
5. Upload screenshot to Supabase Storage `screenshots` bucket
6. Call Claude Vision API with screenshot (visual analysis)
7. Call Claude Text API with DOM data (content analysis)
8. Call Google PageSpeed API (performance metrics)
9. Score all 7 pillars with structured rubrics
10. Calculate weighted overall score
11. Insert 7 `scan_results` rows with findings as JSONB
12. Update scan: status='completed', overall_score, issues counts, screenshot_url
13. On failure: status='failed', error_message

#### `supabase/functions/ai-analyze/index.ts` (Claude API)
- **Vision call**: Send screenshot to `claude-sonnet-4-20250514`, structured CRO rubric for visual pillars (Visual Hierarchy, CTA visibility, Trust Signals, Mobile layout)
- **Text call**: Send scraped DOM to Claude, rubric for content pillars (Value Proposition, Content & Copy, CTA copy quality)
- Output: JSON matching the `Finding` type exactly (title, severity, current_state, recommendation, ai_suggestion, effort, estimated_lift)

#### `supabase/functions/ai-analyze/prompts.ts`
- System prompts for vision + text analysis
- Structured output format matching existing types
- Page-type-specific rubrics (landing, product, homepage, pricing, signup)

#### `supabase/functions/pagespeed/index.ts`
- Calls Google PageSpeed Insights API
- Extracts LCP, CLS, TTFB, total page weight
- Converts to 0-100 score for `page_performance` pillar
- Generates findings based on metric thresholds

### 3.3 Frontend Scan Flow Rework
- **Modify** `src/pages/Scan.tsx`:
  - `startAnalysis` calls edge function via `useCreateScan` mutation
  - Poll scan status every 2s with `useScan(scanId, { refetchInterval: 2000 })`
  - Map scan status to progress steps
  - Navigate to `/reports/${scanId}` on completion
  - Show error with retry on failure

### 3.4 Storage
- Create `screenshots` bucket in Supabase Storage (public read)
- **Modify** `src/pages/AuditReport.tsx` -- display real screenshot in BrowserFrame

# Full App Audit — Bug Fixes & Code Cleanup

## Context
Full audit of the Zip Code Checker Shopify app to fix all bugs, remove unused code, ensure proper Polaris UI usage, and harden error handling across all routes. The app is live in production — these fixes improve stability, code quality, and prevent potential crashes.

## Issues Found & Fixes

### 1. ESLint Unused Variables/Imports (7 errors) — CRITICAL
These cause ESLint failures and indicate dead code.

| File | Line | Unused Item | Fix |
|------|------|-------------|-----|
| `app/routes/app.help.tsx` | 16 | `Badge` import | Remove from import |
| `app/routes/app.pricing.tsx` | 12 | `ALL_PAID_PLANS` import | Remove from import |
| `app/routes/app.widget.tsx` | 35 | `admin` variable | Remove destructuring of `admin` |
| `app/routes/app.widget.tsx` | 93 | `metaJson` variable | Remove unused variable |
| `app/routes/app.widget.tsx` | 288 | `cfg` parameter | Prefix with `_` or remove |
| `app/routes/app.widget.tsx` | 997 | `positionOptions` variable | Remove unused variable |
| `app/routes/webhooks.app.scopes_update.tsx` | 6 | `shop` variable | Remove or prefix with `_` |

### 2. Prisma Migration Mismatch — CRITICAL
- Migration `20260317000000_add_widget_style` adds `widgetStyle` column to WidgetConfig
- But `prisma/schema.prisma` does NOT define this field (removed in commit `54d5010`)
- **Fix:** Delete the orphaned migration directory since the feature was reverted. Then run `npx prisma migrate dev` to ensure schema is in sync.

### 3. Missing Error Handling in API Routes — HIGH
**`api.zip-check.tsx`** (lines 71-79): Three DB calls (`db.zipCode.findUnique`, `db.widgetConfig.findUnique`, `db.shopSettings.findUnique`) with no try-catch. If DB is down, returns unhandled 500.
- **Fix:** Wrap `handleZipCheck` body in try-catch, return JSON error with 500 status + CORS headers.

**`api.widget-config.tsx`** (lines 92-95): `Promise.all()` with DB calls, no try-catch.
- **Fix:** Wrap in try-catch, return JSON error with 500 status + CORS headers.

### 4. Raw HTML `<div style>` in Delivery Rules — WARNING
**`app/routes/app.delivery-rules.tsx`** (lines 494, 503, 530, 541, 570, 579): Uses `<div style={{ flex: 1 }}>` for layout.
- **Fix:** Replace with Polaris `<Box>` with `width="fill"` or wrap pairs in `<InlineStack>`.

### 5. Dead Code in GDPR Webhook — WARNING
**`webhooks.customers.data_request.tsx`** (lines 23-26): DB query result never stored or used.
- **Fix:** Store result and log it, or add a TODO comment explaining the 30-day GDPR response requirement.

### 6. Duplicate `normalizeZipCode` — MINOR
Duplicated in `api.zip-check.tsx` (line 22-27) and `api.waitlist.tsx` (line 23-28).
- **Fix:** Extract to `app/utils/zip.ts` and import from both files.

# Fix: Pending resumes need visible Rank button

## Problem
When resumes arrive via email (or auto-rank is OFF), they show as "Pending" in the results table. The "Rank" button exists in the table header's right side but users can't find it — it's small, in a flex-wrap, easy to miss.

## Fix
Add a prominent banner ABOVE the results table when there are unranked pending resumes. Full-width, primary color, impossible to miss.

## Change in `src/components/JobDetailView.tsx`
Before the `{/* Results Table */}` section (~line 1173), add:

```tsx
{/* Pending resumes banner — prompt to rank */}
{isExistingJob && visibleResults.some(r => r.pending) && visibleResults.every(r => r.pending) && !ranking && (
  <Card className="border-primary/30 bg-primary/5">
    <CardContent className="px-5 py-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
      <div>
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground">
          {visibleResults.filter(r => r.pending).length} resumes ready to rank
        </p>

        </p>

      </Button>
    </CardContent>
  </Card>
)}
```

# CROBOT — Full UI/UX Overhaul Plan

## Context
The app works functionally but looks and behaves like a prototype. Issues: no QueryClient config causing redundant network calls; forceUpdate anti-pattern causing excessive re-renders; hardcoded arbitrary colors/sizes breaking the design system; missing error states, loading states, and responsive fixes that make it feel unpolished. Goal is production-grade, mature SaaS quality across all 27 pages.

---

## Phase 1 — Critical Performance Fixes (invisible to users until they hit them)

### 1.1 `src/App.tsx` — Configure QueryClient (line 52)
Replace bare `new QueryClient()` with:
```ts
const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      staleTime: 1000 * 60 * 2,
      gcTime: 1000 * 60 * 10,
      retry: (failureCount, error) => {
        const msg = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "";
        if (msg.includes("401") || msg.includes("403")) return false;
        return failureCount < 2;
      },
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
    },
    mutations: { retry: 0 },
  },
});
```
Also wrap entire `<App>` JSX in `<ThemeProvider attribute="class" defaultTheme="light" enableSystem={false}>` (import from `next-themes`).

### 1.2 `src/pages/AgentWorkspace.tsx` — Replace `forceUpdate` anti-pattern
Lines 36-47 use `useState(0)` + `forceUpdate((n)=>n+1)` as subscription callback. Replace with `useSyncExternalStore` (built into React 18):
```ts
const engineSnapshot = useSyncExternalStore(
  agentEngine.subscribe.bind(agentEngine),
  () => ({ messages: agentEngine.getMessages(taskId ?? ""), progress: agentEngine.getTaskProgress(taskId ?? "") }),
  () => ({ messages: [], progress: 0 })
);
```
Destructure `messages` and `progress` from `engineSnapshot` instead of calling `agentEngine.get*()` directly. Remove `useState(0)` + `forceUpdate`.

### 1.3 `src/lib/agent-engine.ts` — Fix timer leak on navigation
The recursive `deliverNextMessage` chain spawns new timers after `cleanup()` has run. Add a `deliveryToken` guard:
- Add `private deliveryToken = 0` field
- In `watchTask()`: increment token, pass it to `deliverNextMessage(taskId, token)`
- In `deliverNextMessage()`: add `if (token !== this.deliveryToken) return` at top AND inside the `setTimeout` callback

### 1.4 `src/pages/Dashboard.tsx` — Memoize derived data
- Import `useMemo` from React
- Wrap `completedScans` filter in `useMemo(() => recentScans.filter(...), [recentScans])`
- Wrap `topQuickWins` map in `useMemo` keyed on its input array

### 1.5 NEW FILE: `src/hooks/use-debounce.ts`
```ts
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
export function useDebounce<T>(value: T, delay = 300): T {
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState<T>(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(value), delay);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [value, delay]);
  return debounced;
}
```
Then in `src/pages/admin/Users.tsx` and `src/pages/admin/Scans.tsx`:
- Remove `debouncedSearch` state + `handleSearchKeyDown` + `handleSearchBlur`
- Add `const debouncedSearch = useDebounce(search, 350)`
- Remove `onKeyDown`/`onBlur` from search Input
- Move `SortableHeader` component above the page component (not defined inside render)
- Wrap `handleSort` in `useCallback`

## Phase 3 — UX Bug Fixes

### 3.1 `src/pages/AuditReport.tsx` + `src/components/shared/FindingCard.tsx` — Copy button timer fix
In both `CopyButton` and `CopyInline`, add `useRef` to store the timeout:
```ts
const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
const handleCopy = async () => {
  if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
  await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
  setCopied(true);
  timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
};
useEffect(() => () => { if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current); }, []);
```

```ts
toast.error("Analysis engine failed to start. Please try re-scanning in a moment.", { duration: 6000 });
```

```tsx
{isError && (
  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-destructive/20 bg-destructive/5 p-4">
    <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5 text-destructive shrink-0" />
    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-destructive">Failed to load audit log. Check permissions or refresh.</p>
  </div>
)}
```

---

## Phase 4 — Polish

### 4.1 `src/components/AppLayout.tsx` — Restore sidebar open state from cookie
The shadcn sidebar already writes `sidebar:state` cookie. In `AppLayout.tsx`, read it:
```ts
const defaultOpen = (() => {
  const match = typeof document !== "undefined"
    ? document.cookie.split(";").find(c => c.trim().startsWith("sidebar:state="))
    : undefined;
  return match ? match.split("=")[1]?.trim() === "true" : true;
})();
// Pass: <SidebarProvider defaultOpen={defaultOpen}>
```

### 4.2 `src/components/AdminLayout.tsx` — Persist admin collapse state
```ts
const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState<boolean>(() => {
  try { return sessionStorage.getItem("admin:sidebar") === "true"; } catch { return false; }
});
const handleToggle = () => setCollapsed(c => {
  const next = !c;
  try { sessionStorage.setItem("admin:sidebar", String(next)); } catch {}
  return next;
});
```

### 4.3 NEW FILE: `src/components/shared/ChartErrorBoundary.tsx`
Class component `ChartErrorBoundary` that catches chart render errors and shows a "Chart unavailable" fallback div. Wrap every `<ResponsiveContainer>` in Dashboard and AuditReport with `<ChartErrorBoundary>`.

### 4.4 `src/hooks/use-dashboard-stats.ts` — Real week labels
Replace static `"3w ago"/"This week"` labels with actual Monday dates:
```ts
function getWeekLabel(weeksAgo: number): string {
  const d = new Date(Date.now() - weeksAgo * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const day = d.getDay();
  d.setDate(d.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return d.toLocaleDateString("en-US", { month: "short", day: "numeric" });
}
```

### 4.5 `src/pages/Dashboard.tsx` + Admin tables — Mobile horizontal scroll
Wrap `<Table>` in `<div className="overflow-x-auto scrollbar-thin">` in:
- `src/pages/Dashboard.tsx` (Recent Scans table)
- `src/pages/admin/Users.tsx`
- `src/pages/admin/Scans.tsx`

### 4.6 `src/components/TopBar.tsx` — Remove hardcoded max-w
Change `max-w-[200px]` on breadcrumb span → `max-w-[160px] sm:max-w-xs`. Add `overflow-hidden` to left flex group.

### 4.7 `src/pages/Settings.tsx` — Theme toggle (appearance section)
Add at bottom of Profile tab card:
```tsx
import { useTheme } from "next-themes";
const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();
// Render a simple toggle: Light / Dark buttons
```

## MEDIUM PRIORITY ISSUES

### 9. Loose TypeScript Configuration
**File:** `/Users/yashbaldha/Desktop/Boldteq App/Rankora/tsconfig.json`  
**Lines:** 4-6, 13  
**Issue:** Non-strict TypeScript settings  
**Severity:** MEDIUM  
**Details:**
```json
"noImplicitAny": false,        // ❌ Should be true
"noUnusedLocals": false,       // ❌ Should be true
"noUnusedParameters": false,   // ❌ Should be true
"strictNullChecks": false      // ❌ Should be true
```

**Impact:**
- Allows implicit `any` types (defeats TypeScript purpose)
- Unused variables/parameters silently ignored
- Null/undefined not properly checked
- Will accumulate technical debt

**Fix:**
```json
{
  "compilerOptions": {
    "allowJs": true,
    "noImplicitAny": true,          // Enable
    "noUnusedLocals": true,         // Enable
    "noUnusedParameters": true,     // Enable
    "strict": true,                 // Master setting
    "strictNullChecks": true,       // Enable
    // ... rest
  }
}
```

### 10. TypeScript Inconsistency Between Config Files
**Files:** `tsconfig.json` vs `tsconfig.app.json`  
**Issue:** Different settings in different configs  
**Severity:** MEDIUM  
**Details:**
- `tsconfig.json`: `"strict": false, "noImplicitAny": false`
- `tsconfig.app.json`: `"strict": false, "noImplicitAny": false`
- But `tsconfig.node.json`: `"strict": true`

**Fix:** Use consistent strict settings across all configs

### 11. Supabase Config Missing Auth Settings
**File:** `/Users/yashbaldha/Desktop/Boldteq App/Rankora/supabase/config.toml`  
**Issue:** ALL Supabase functions have `verify_jwt = false`  
**Severity:** MEDIUM - SECURITY ISSUE  
**Details:**
- Lines 4-64: Every single function listed has `verify_jwt = false`
- This disables JWT authentication for all edge functions
- Allows unauthenticated access to sensitive operations

**Current:**
```toml
[functions.rank-resumes]
verify_jwt = false  # ❌ Security risk

[functions.admin-adjust-credits]
verify_jwt = false  # ❌ Should be true
```

**Fix:**
```toml
# For public functions only:
[functions.serve-robots]
verify_jwt = false

# For authenticated functions:
[functions.admin-adjust-credits]
verify_jwt = true
```

# Size Chart & Recommender — Comprehensive Testing & Fix Plan

## Overview

This plan covers: (A) adding Vitest for unit/integration tests, (B) adding Playwright for API endpoint tests, (C) fixing 4 known issues, and (D) verification strategy.

The app is a Shopify embedded app. Admin UI routes require Shopify OAuth and cannot be tested via Playwright without a dev store session. The testable surface is:

- **Pure logic functions** (ai.server.ts, plans.ts, api.server.ts, rate-limit.server.ts, templates.ts)
- **Public API endpoints** (api.size-chart, api.recommend, api.widget-config, api.analytics-event) — via HTTP against a running server with a seeded test database
- **Analytics aggregation** (analytics.server.ts) — requires DB but can use a test SQLite instance

---

## Part 1: Test Infrastructure Setup

### 1A. Install Vitest (Unit Tests)

Vitest is the natural choice — it uses the same Vite config, supports TypeScript natively, and requires zero extra bundler config.

**New devDependency:** `vitest`

**New file: `/vitest.config.ts`**
```ts
import { defineConfig } from "vitest/config";
import tsconfigPaths from "vite-tsconfig-paths";

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [tsconfigPaths()],
  test: {
    globals: true,
    environment: "node",
    include: ["tests/unit/**/*.test.ts"],
    setupFiles: ["tests/setup.ts"],
  },
});
```

**New file: `/tests/setup.ts`**
- Sets `process.env.NODE_ENV = "test"`
- Any global test setup needed

**New npm scripts in package.json:**
```json
"test": "vitest run",
"test:watch": "vitest",
"test:unit": "vitest run --project unit",
"test:api": "npx playwright test",
"test:all": "vitest run && npx playwright test"
```

### 1B. Configure Playwright (API Tests)

Playwright is already installed (v1.52.0). It needs configuration for API testing (no browser needed for most tests).

**New file: `/playwright.config.ts`**
```ts
import { defineConfig } from "playwright/test"; // note: using playwright not @playwright/test since that's what's installed

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: "tests/api",
  timeout: 30_000,
  retries: 0,
  use: {
    baseURL: "http://localhost:3000",
  },
  webServer: {
    command: "npm run start",
    url: "http://localhost:3000",
    reuseExistingServer: true,
    timeout: 60_000,
  },
});
```

> **Important note:** The `playwright` package (not `@playwright/test`) is installed. Playwright's test runner requires `@playwright/test`. Either: (a) switch the devDependency to `@playwright/test`, or (b) write API tests as plain Vitest tests using `fetch()`. **Recommendation: use Vitest + fetch() for API tests** to avoid adding another dependency. This keeps one test runner for everything.

**Revised approach — all tests via Vitest:**

Instead of Playwright for API tests, use Vitest with native `fetch()` (available in Node 20+). This is simpler and avoids the `playwright` vs `@playwright/test` mismatch.

### 1C. Directory Structure

```
tests/
  setup.ts                         ← Global test setup
  unit/
    ai.server.test.ts              ← AI recommendation engine
    plans.test.ts                  ← Plan logic (getPlan, hasFeature, canCreateChart)
    api-utils.test.ts              ← CORS, shop validation, CSS sanitization
    rate-limit.test.ts             ← Rate limiter
    templates.test.ts              ← Template helpers
  integration/
    api-size-chart.test.ts         ← GET /api/size-chart endpoint
    api-recommend.test.ts          ← POST /api/recommend endpoint
    api-widget-config.test.ts      ← GET /api/widget-config endpoint
    api-analytics-event.test.ts    ← POST /api/analytics-event endpoint
  helpers/
    seed-db.ts                     ← Seed test database with fixtures
    test-server.ts                 ← Start/stop app server for integration tests
```

## Part 2: Unit Tests (Pure Logic — No Server Required)

### Test File: `tests/unit/ai.server.test.ts`

Tests for the AI recommendation engine in `/app/lib/ai.server.ts`.

| Test | What it validates |
|------|-------------------|
| `estimateMeasurements returns correct values for average male` | Known input (175cm, 75kg, male, regular) produces expected chest/waist/hips values within tolerance |
| `estimateMeasurements applies body type adjustments` | "slim" produces smaller chest than "plus" for same height/weight |
| `estimateMeasurements handles edge case BMI` | Very low and very high BMI don't produce NaN or negative values |
| `recommendSize returns best match from chart data` | Given a Men's Shirts chart and a 175cm/75kg male, returns "M" or "L" |
| `recommendSize returns empty when chart has no rows` | Empty chart data returns `recommendedSize: ""`, `confidence: 0` |
| `recommendSize returns empty when chart has no headers` | No headers returns empty result |
| `recommendSize returns alternatives sorted by confidence` | Alternatives are in descending confidence order |
| `recommendSize handles range values in chart cells` | Cells like "34-36" are parsed as midpoint 35 |
| `recommendSize handles unit-annotated values` | Cells like "34 cm" or "34 in" are parsed correctly |
| `recommendSize confidence decreases with distance` | A perfect match has higher confidence than a distant one |
| `parseMeasurementValue (internal, test via recommendSize)` | Dashes, empty strings, ranges, plain numbers all handled |
| `findSizeColumnKey picks "Size" header` | When "Size" is present, it's chosen over other headers |
| `headerToMeasurementKey maps common names` | "Bust" -> chest, "Waist" -> waist, "Hip" -> hips, "Sleeve" -> armLength |

### Test File: `tests/unit/plans.test.ts`

Tests for `/app/lib/plans.ts`.

| Test | What it validates |
|------|-------------------|
| `getPlan returns correct plan by id` | Each of "free", "starter", "growth", "pro" returns the right plan |
| `getPlan returns free plan for unknown id` | Unknown ID falls back to free |
| `hasFeature returns false for free plan AI` | `hasFeature("free", "aiRecommender")` is false |
| `hasFeature returns true for growth plan AI` | `hasFeature("growth", "aiRecommender")` is true |
| `hasFeature returns true for pro plan advanced AI` | `hasFeature("pro", "advancedAi")` is true |
| `canCreateChart respects free plan limit of 3` | `canCreateChart("free", 2)` is true, `canCreateChart("free", 3)` is false |
| `canCreateChart allows unlimited for paid plans` | `canCreateChart("starter", 999)` is true |
| `PLANS array has exactly 4 entries` | Guards against accidental plan deletion |
| `all plans have required fields` | Every plan has id, name, price, limits with all keys |

### Test File: `tests/unit/api-utils.test.ts`

Tests for `/app/lib/api.server.ts`.

| Test | What it validates |
|------|-------------------|
| `validateShop accepts valid .myshopify.com domain` | `"my-store.myshopify.com"` returns null |
| `validateShop rejects null` | Returns error Response with status 400 |
| `validateShop rejects empty string` | Returns error Response |
| `validateShop rejects domain without .myshopify.com` | `"evil.com"` rejected |
| `validateShop rejects domain with path injection` | `"store.myshopify.com/admin"` rejected |
| `validateShop rejects domain starting with hyphen` | `"-bad.myshopify.com"` rejected |
| `corsJson includes CORS headers` | Response has Access-Control-Allow-Origin: * |
| `corsJson merges custom headers` | Custom headers don't overwrite CORS headers |
| `corsOptions returns 204 with CORS headers` | Correct preflight response |
| `sanitizeCss blocks @import` | `"@import url(evil)"` becomes `"/* blocked */ url(evil)"` |
| `sanitizeCss blocks javascript: URLs` | `"url(javascript:alert(1))"` is neutralized |
| `sanitizeCss blocks expression()` | IE expression syntax is blocked |
| `sanitizeCss blocks </style` | Style breakout attempts are blocked |
| `sanitizeCss blocks <script` | Script injection attempts are blocked |
| `sanitizeCss passes through valid CSS` | `"color: red; font-size: 14px;"` is unchanged |
| `sanitizeCss handles empty string` | Returns empty string |
| `SHOP_RE regex matches valid domains` | Pattern test against known-good domains |
| `SHOP_RE regex rejects invalid domains` | Pattern test against known-bad domains |

### Test File: `tests/unit/rate-limit.test.ts`

Tests for `/app/lib/rate-limit.server.ts`.

| Test | What it validates |
|------|-------------------|
| `first request returns null (allowed)` | Fresh key is not rate limited |
| `requests under limit return null` | 30 requests on a 30-limit key all return null |
| `request exceeding limit returns 429 Response` | 31st request returns Response with status 429 |
| `429 response includes Retry-After header` | Header is present and is a positive integer |
| `429 response includes CORS headers` | Rate limit responses still have CORS |
| `different keys are independent` | Key A at limit doesn't affect key B |
| `window resets after windowMs` | After advancing time past window, requests are allowed again (requires mocking Date.now) |
| `cleanup removes stale entries` | After CLEANUP_INTERVAL, expired entries are purged (test via repeated calls) |

### Test File: `tests/unit/templates.test.ts`

Tests for `/app/lib/templates.ts`.

| Test | What it validates |
|------|-------------------|
| `SIZE_CHART_TEMPLATES has 9 templates` | Correct count |
| `every template has required fields` | name, category, gender, data with headers/units/rows |
| `every template has at least one row` | No empty templates |
| `getTemplatesByCategory returns correct templates` | "tops" returns Women's Tops and Men's Shirts |
| `getTemplatesByCategory returns empty for unknown category` | Unknown category returns [] |
| `getCategories returns unique categories` | No duplicates, includes all expected categories |

## Part 3: Integration Tests (API Endpoints — Server Required)

These tests require a running server with a seeded SQLite database. They use Vitest with `fetch()`.

### Test Helper: `tests/helpers/seed-db.ts`

Seeds the test database with:
- A `SizeChart` for shop `test-shop.myshopify.com` with known chart data (Men's Shirts template)
- A `ChartAssignment` linking the chart to a product ID
- A `RecommenderConfig` with `enabled: true`
- A `Subscription` with `planId: "growth"` (has aiRecommender access)
- A `WidgetConfig` with custom colors
- A few `AnalyticsEvent` records

### Test Helper: `tests/helpers/test-server.ts`

- Starts the app server on a test port (e.g., 3001) using `npm run start` with `PORT=3001`
- Uses a separate test SQLite database (`prisma/test.sqlite`)
- Provides `beforeAll` / `afterAll` hooks for Vitest globalSetup

### Vitest Config Update for Integration Tests

Add a second project in vitest.config.ts:
```ts
test: {
  projects: [
    {
      name: "unit",
      include: ["tests/unit/**/*.test.ts"],
    },
    {
      name: "integration", 
      include: ["tests/integration/**/*.test.ts"],
      globalSetup: ["tests/helpers/test-server.ts"],
    },
  ],
}
```

## Part 4: Fix Plan for Known Issues

### Fix 1: Import Inconsistency in `api.recommend.tsx`

**File:** `/app/routes/api.recommend.tsx`

**Problem:** Lines 2-7 use `~/` path aliases while all other route files use `../` relative imports.

**Fix:** Change 6 import lines:
```
import db from "~/db.server"           → import db from "../db.server"
import { corsJson, ... } from "~/..."  → import { corsJson, ... } from "../lib/api.server"
import { checkRateLimit } from "~/..." → import { checkRateLimit } from "../lib/rate-limit.server"
import { hasFeature } from "~/..."     → import { hasFeature } from "../lib/plans"
import { recommendSize } from "~/..."  → import { recommendSize } from "../lib/ai.server"
import type { RecommendInput } from "~/..." → import type { RecommendInput } from "../lib/ai.server"
```

Both `~/` and `../` resolve correctly (tsconfig.json has `paths: {"~/*": ["./app/*"]}`), so this is a consistency fix, not a bug fix. Either convention is valid — `../` is chosen because it matches every other file.

### Fix 2: Missing `.env.example`

**New file:** `/.env.example`

```bash
# Shopify App Credentials (provided by Shopify CLI / Partner Dashboard)
SHOPIFY_API_KEY=
SHOPIFY_API_SECRET=

# App URL (set automatically by `shopify app dev`, or set manually for production)
SHOPIFY_APP_URL=https://your-app-domain.com

# OAuth scopes (comma-separated)
SCOPES=write_products

# Optional: Custom shop domain (for Shopify Plus stores with custom domains)
# SHOP_CUSTOM_DOMAIN=

# Node environment
NODE_ENV=development

# Port (defaults to 3000)
# PORT=3000
```

This documents the 5 environment variables referenced in the codebase (found via grep for `process.env.`):
- `SHOPIFY_API_KEY` (shopify.server.ts, app.tsx)
- `SHOPIFY_API_SECRET` (shopify.server.ts)
- `SHOPIFY_APP_URL` (shopify.server.ts, vite.config.ts)
- `SCOPES` (shopify.server.ts)
- `SHOP_CUSTOM_DOMAIN` (shopify.server.ts)
- `NODE_ENV` (db.server.ts, billing.server.ts)
- `PORT` (vite.config.ts)
- `HOST` / `FRONTEND_PORT` (vite.config.ts — set by Shopify CLI, not user-configured)

### Fix 3: `shopify.app.toml` Placeholder URL

**File:** `/shopify.app.toml`

**Problem:** Lines 4 and 41-44 contain `https://example.com` as placeholder.

**Fix:** This cannot be fixed generically — it requires the actual production domain. The fix is:
1. Add a comment in the file making it clearer this MUST be updated
2. Add a pre-deploy check script that rejects `example.com`
3. Document in README.md

**New file: `/scripts/pre-deploy-check.mjs`**
```js
// Reads shopify.app.toml, exits with error if example.com is still present
import { readFileSync } from "fs";
const toml = readFileSync("shopify.app.toml", "utf-8");
if (toml.includes("example.com")) {
  console.error("ERROR: shopify.app.toml still contains example.com placeholder.");
  console.error("Update application_url and redirect_urls before deploying.");
  process.exit(1);
}
console.log("Pre-deploy check passed.");
```

**New npm script:**
```json
"predeploy": "node scripts/pre-deploy-check.mjs"
```

### Fix 4: No Test Infrastructure

This is addressed by Parts 1-3 above (the entire test infrastructure setup).

## Part 5: Verification Strategy

### Local Verification Flow

1. **After adding Vitest + unit tests:**
   ```bash
   npm run test:unit
   ```
   All unit tests must pass. No server needed.

2. **After adding integration tests:**
   ```bash
   npm run build
   npm run setup          # Ensure Prisma client + migrations
   npm run test:api       # Starts server, seeds DB, runs tests
   ```

3. **After fixing imports:**
   ```bash
   npm run typecheck      # Zero errors
   npm run lint           # Zero errors  
   npm run build          # Builds successfully
   ```

4. **After adding .env.example:**
   - Verify .gitignore already has `.env` and `.env.*` (confirmed: it does)
   - Verify .env.example lists all variables from the grep results

5. **After adding pre-deploy check:**
   ```bash
   node scripts/pre-deploy-check.mjs   # Should fail with current example.com
   ```

6. **Full verification:**
   ```bash
   npm run typecheck && npm run lint && npm run test
   ```

### CI Integration (Future)

Add a GitHub Actions workflow at `.github/workflows/test.yml`:
```yaml
- npm ci
- npm run typecheck
- npm run lint  
- npm run test:unit
- npm run build
# Integration tests need a running server + DB — run in a separate job
```

Execute in this order to minimize risk:

| Step | Task | Dependencies |
|------|------|--------------|
| 1 | Install vitest as devDependency | None |

| 3 | Create `tests/setup.ts` | Step 2 |

| 5 | Create `tests/unit/api-utils.test.ts` | Step 3 |
| 6 | Create `tests/unit/templates.test.ts` | Step 3 |
| 7 | Create `tests/unit/rate-limit.test.ts` | Step 3 (needs Date.now mocking) |
| 8 | Create `tests/unit/ai.server.test.ts` | Step 3 (most complex unit tests) |
| 9 | Fix import inconsistency in `api.recommend.tsx` | None (independent) |
| 10 | Create `.env.example` | None (independent) |
| 11 | Create `scripts/pre-deploy-check.mjs` + npm script | None (independent) |

| 16 | Create `tests/integration/api-widget-config.test.ts` | Steps 12-13 |
| 17 | Create `tests/integration/api-analytics-event.test.ts` | Steps 12-13 |
| 18 | Run full verification | All steps |

Steps 4-8 can be done in parallel. Steps 9-11 can be done in parallel with everything else. Steps 14-17 can be done in parallel after 12-13.

---

## Key Design Decisions

1. **Vitest over Jest**: Vitest shares the Vite config, understands tsconfig paths natively, and has faster execution. The project already uses Vite.

2. **Vitest + fetch() over Playwright for API tests**: The installed package is `playwright` (browser automation library), not `@playwright/test` (test runner). Rather than swap dependencies, using Vitest + Node's built-in fetch() for API tests keeps one unified test runner.

3. **Separate test SQLite database**: Integration tests need a real database but must not pollute the dev database. Using `DATABASE_URL=file:test.sqlite` in the test environment ensures isolation.

4. **Rate limiter tests need time mocking**: The rate limiter uses `Date.now()`. Tests should use `vi.useFakeTimers()` from Vitest to control time advancement.

5. **No admin UI testing**: Shopify admin routes require OAuth. Testing them requires either a real dev store session or mocking the entire Shopify auth stack. This is deferred — the public API and server logic tests cover the highest-risk code paths.
