# Plan: Widget Page Full QA — Production-Grade Fix

## Context
The Widget Customization page (`app.widget.tsx`) has multiple bugs preventing it from being production-quality:
1. No unsaved-changes feedback — user edits fields with no warning they haven't saved
2. Live preview does NOT apply custom CSS — breaks the entire Custom CSS feature
3. Position setting ("floating", "popup") is completely unimplemented in the Liquid block — cfg.position is fetched but ignored, widget always renders inline
4. API response is cached for 60s on the storefront — changes don't appear for a minute
5. Lint error: unused `successMsg` variable in `api.zip-check.tsx`

---

## Files to Modify

| File | Nature of Change |
|---|---|
| `app/routes/app.widget.tsx` | Unsaved-changes tracking, fix preview (custom CSS + position) |
| `extensions/zip-code-widget/blocks/zip-code-checker.liquid` | Implement floating + popup position modes |
| `app/routes/api.widget-config.tsx` | Remove 60-second cache |
| `app/routes/api.zip-check.tsx` | Remove unused `successMsg` on line 44 |

---

## Detailed Changes

### 1. `app/routes/app.widget.tsx`

#### A. Unsaved-Changes Detection
Add `isDirty` boolean state. Every setter call (color, text, checkbox, etc.) sets `isDirty(true)`.
After successful save (via `useEffect` watching `fetcher.data`) and after reset, set `isDirty(false)`.

```tsx
const [isDirty, setIsDirty] = useState(false);
// wrap each setter: e.g. const handlePositionChange = (v) => { setPosition(v); setIsDirty(true); }
// in useEffect: if fetcher.data?.success → setIsDirty(false)
// in handleReset: setIsDirty(false)
```

Show a `Banner tone="warning" title="You have unsaved changes"` in the form when `isDirty && !isSaving`.

#### B. Fix Live Preview — Custom CSS + Correct Structure
Current `WidgetPreview` uses inline React styles. Custom CSS targets class names (`.zcc-heading`, `.zcc-btn`, etc.) that don't exist in the preview, so it has zero effect.

**Fix**: Move `WidgetPreview` OUTSIDE the `WidgetPage` function (pass config + previewState as props). Generate the same CSS string that the real Liquid widget generates, scoped to a stable preview ID (`"zcc-admin-preview"`). Render a `<style>` tag using `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`. Use class names matching the real widget in the preview HTML.

Preview component signature:
```tsx
function WidgetPreview({ cfg, previewState }: { cfg: WidgetConfig & { customCss: string }, previewState: string }) {
  const previewId = "zcc-admin-preview";
  const rad = cfg.borderRadius + "px";
  const css = buildWidgetCss(previewId, cfg); // same CSS generation logic as Liquid block
  // ... render
}
```

The preview should:
- Render `<style dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: css }} />`
- Use `<p className="zcc-heading">`, `<div className="zcc-row">`, `<input className="zcc-input">`, `<button className="zcc-btn">`, `<div className="zcc-result ok/fail">`, `<div className="zcc-meta">`, `<div className="zcc-wl">` class names
- Show the appropriate result block based on `previewState`
- For `showEta` + success → show ETA sample text
- For `showZone` + success → show zone sample text
- For `showWaitlistOnFailure` + error/notfound → show email input + "Join Waitlist" button

#### C. Position-Aware Preview
Wrap the preview widget differently based on `position`:
- `"inline"`: render normally (current look)
- `"floating"`: render a mock "bottom-right fixed button" inside the preview container to show how it will look (using `position: absolute; bottom: 12px; right: 12px` within the container since we can't use `position: fixed` in a preview)
- `"popup"`: render a centered "Check Delivery" button and a shadow-overlay hint to show the popup concept

#### D. Position Option Labels (clearer)
Current: "Inline (embedded in page)", "Floating button", "Popup modal"
Keep the same values, just ensure labels are clear.

#### E. Remove `saved` Banner from Layout.Section
The `saved` Banner is currently inside `<Layout.Section>` at the top. It should either:
- Be removed entirely (toast already shows on save) — simplest
- OR kept but moved inline near the save button area

Recommendation: Remove the Banner, rely on the Shopify `toast.show("Widget settings saved")` which already fires. This is cleaner and matches Shopify's own app patterns.

#### F. Page-level "Save" in secondaryActions
Change `destructive: true` on "Reset to Defaults" to `tone="critical"` is not needed — remove `destructive: true` so it's not styled as a danger button. Reset to defaults is not a destructive irreversible action.

---

### 2. `extensions/zip-code-widget/blocks/zip-code-checker.liquid`

**Problem**: `cfg.position` is fetched via `/api/widget-config` but `renderWidget(cfg)` never uses it. All three positions render as inline.

**Fix**: In `renderWidget(cfg)`, branch on `cfg.position`:

#### Inline (default)
Current behavior — render widget directly. No change needed.

#### Floating (`cfg.position === "floating"`)
Render a fixed trigger button (bottom-right of screen). Clicking it toggles a panel above the button.

HTML structure:
```html
<div id="WID-float" style="position:fixed;bottom:20px;right:20px;z-index:9998">
  <button id="WID-trigger" style="background:primaryColor;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:50px;padding:12px 20px;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.2)">
    📍 Check Delivery
  </button>
  <div id="WID-panel" style="display:none;position:absolute;bottom:calc(100% + 12px);right:0;width:340px;background:backgroundColor;border-radius:rad;box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.15)">
    <!-- close button + widget content here -->
  </div>
</div>
```

Toggle logic: clicking trigger shows/hides panel. Close button inside panel also hides it. Click outside closes it.

#### Popup (`cfg.position === "popup"`)
Render an in-place trigger button (styled to look like a call-to-action). Clicking it shows a modal overlay.

HTML structure:
```html
<!-- trigger rendered in-block -->
<div id="WID-popup-trigger-wrap">
  <button id="WID-popup-trigger" style="background:primaryColor;color:#fff;...">Check Delivery Availability</button>
</div>

<!-- modal rendered at end of body -->
<div id="WID-overlay" style="display:none;position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.5);z-index:9999;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center">
  <div id="WID-modal" style="background:backgroundColor;border-radius:rad;max-width:480px;width:90%;padding:32px;position:relative;box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.3)">
    <button id="WID-close" style="position:absolute;top:12px;right:16px;background:none;border:none;font-size:20px;cursor:pointer;color:textColor">×</button>
    <!-- widget content here (same as inline) -->
  </div>
</div>
```

Close: clicking overlay background or × button. Append modal to `document.body` to avoid z-index/overflow issues.

---

### 3. `app/routes/api.widget-config.tsx`

Remove `Cache-Control: "public, max-age=60"`. Changes should reflect immediately on the storefront.

Replace:
```ts
headers: { ...CORS_HEADERS, "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=60" }
```
With:
```ts
headers: { ...CORS_HEADERS, "Cache-Control": "no-cache" }
```

---

### 4. `app/routes/api.zip-check.tsx`

**Line 44 bug**: `const successMsg = widgetConfig?.successMessage ?? "..."` is declared but never used in that scope (only `notFoundMsg` is used). This triggers ESLint `no-unused-vars`.

**Fix**: Remove the `successMsg` declaration at line 44. The `successMsg` at line 82 (in the allowed block) is separate and should be kept.

---

## Verification Steps

1. **Admin UI — unsaved changes**: Edit any field → yellow "You have unsaved changes" banner appears. Click Save → banner disappears, toast shows.
2. **Admin UI — custom CSS preview**: Enter `.zcc-heading { color: red !important; }` in custom CSS → heading in preview turns red immediately.
3. **Admin UI — position preview**: Change position to "Floating" → preview shows a floating button mockup. Change to "Popup" → preview shows a trigger button mockup.
4. **Storefront — inline**: Add block to product page → widget renders in place, zip check works.
5. **Storefront — floating**: Set position to Floating, save, refresh storefront → fixed button bottom-right, clicking opens panel.
6. **Storefront — popup**: Set position to Popup, save, refresh storefront → trigger button visible, clicking opens overlay.
7. **Storefront — instant update**: Change a color, save → refresh storefront immediately → new color shows (no 60s wait).
8. **No lint errors**: Run `npm run lint` → zero errors.
# Production-Grade Size Chart & Recommender — Implementation Plan

## Executive Summary

This plan transforms the existing Shopify Size Chart & Recommender app from a functional prototype into a production-grade, competitive app. The codebase already has substantial functionality (8 admin routes, 4 public APIs, billing, AI recommender, theme extensions). The work focuses on four phases: architecture cleanup, admin UI redesign, backend hardening, and theme extension polish.

**Estimated total effort:** ~80-120 hours across all phases.

---

## Phase 1: Code Architecture Cleanup (Priority: CRITICAL)

### 1.1 Consolidate Duplicate AI Algorithms

**Problem:** `/app/routes/api.recommend.tsx` contains a 260-line inline recommendation algorithm (imperial-based, ANSUR II). `/app/lib/ai.server.ts` has a BETTER algorithm (metric-based, CDC/WHO, more measurement types including shoulder/armLength/inseam, proper Euclidean distance scoring with exponential decay confidence). The lib version is completely UNUSED.

**Solution:**
- **File:** `/app/routes/api.recommend.tsx`
  - Delete the inline `estimateMeasurements()`, `parseMeasurement()`, `scoreRow()`, `recommendSize()`, and `toImperial()` functions (lines 68-257)
  - Import `{ recommendSize, estimateMeasurements }` from `~/lib/ai.server`
  - Map the API's body type "average" to the lib's "regular" (`bodyType === "average" ? "regular" : bodyType`)
  - Convert imperial inputs to metric before calling `estimateMeasurements()` (height: `inches * 2.54`, weight: `lbs * 0.453592`)
  - Map the lib's output format to the existing API response format (`RecommendResponse`)
  - Keep all existing validation, CORS, and error handling intact

- **File:** `/app/lib/ai.server.ts`
  - Add "average" as an alias for "regular" in body type handling
  - Ensure the `chartData` format matches what the API receives from the DB (it already does)
  - No other changes needed — this file is already well-structured

**Risk:** Response format change. Must ensure `measurements` output keys match what the storefront widget expects.

### 1.2 Create Shared Components Directory

**Problem:** No `/app/components/` directory exists. All UI is inline in route files (600-800+ lines each). The CLAUDE.md references components that don't exist yet.

**Solution:** Create `/app/components/` with these extracted components:

| Component File | Extracted From | Reused In |
|---|---|---|
| `components/StatCard.tsx` | `app.settings.tsx` (lines 214-232) | Dashboard, Analytics, Settings |
| `components/ColorPickerField.tsx` | `app.widget.tsx` (lines 123-178) | Widget Customizer |
| `components/TableEditor.tsx` | `app.size-charts.$id.tsx` (lines 310-641) | Chart Editor |
| `components/ChartPreview.tsx` | `app.size-charts.$id.tsx` (lines 647-754) | Chart Editor, Dashboard |
| `components/WidgetPreview.tsx` | `app.widget.tsx` (lines 312-482) | Widget Customizer |
| `components/BarChart.tsx` | `app.analytics.tsx` (lines 198-221) | Analytics |
| `components/FunnelBar.tsx` | `app.analytics.tsx` (lines 227-240) | Analytics |
| `components/PlanBadge.tsx` | New | Dashboard, Pricing, Settings |
| `components/EmptyStateCard.tsx` | New | All list pages |
| `components/DateRangePicker.tsx` | New | Analytics |
| `components/ConfirmModal.tsx` | New | Delete actions everywhere |

**Approach:**
1. Create each component file with proper TypeScript interfaces
2. Export from a barrel `components/index.ts`
3. Replace inline code in route files with imports
4. No functional changes — pure refactor

### 1.3 Create Shared API Middleware Utilities

**Problem:** CORS headers, shop validation regex, and response helpers are duplicated across all 4 API routes.

**Solution:** Create `/app/lib/api.server.ts`:
```typescript
// Shared across api.recommend.tsx, api.size-chart.tsx, api.widget-config.tsx, api.analytics-event.tsx
export const CORS_HEADERS = { ... };
export const SHOP_RE = /^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9-]*\.myshopify\.com$/;
export function corsJson(data: unknown, status?: number): Response { ... }
export function validateShop(shop: string | null): string | Response { ... }
```

### 1.4 Add Rate Limiting for Public APIs

**Problem:** No rate limiting on any public endpoint. A malicious actor could flood `/api/analytics-event` or `/api/recommend` with thousands of requests.

**Solution:** Create `/app/lib/rate-limit.server.ts`:
- Use an in-memory sliding window counter (Map<string, { count: number, resetAt: number }>)
- Key by `shop` domain (not IP, since storefront requests come from Shopify CDN)
- Limits per endpoint:
  - `/api/widget-config`: 60 req/min per shop (GET, cached anyway)
  - `/api/size-chart`: 120 req/min per shop (GET, high traffic)
  - `/api/recommend`: 30 req/min per shop (POST, compute-heavy)
  - `/api/analytics-event`: 300 req/min per shop (POST, fire-and-forget)
- Return `429 Too Many Requests` with `Retry-After` header and CORS headers
- For production at scale: replace with Redis-backed limiter (document as TODO)

**Files to modify:**
- Create: `/app/lib/rate-limit.server.ts`
- Modify: All 4 `api.*.tsx` routes — add `const limited = await checkRateLimit(shop, "recommend"); if (limited) return limited;` at top of handler

### 1.5 Add Feature Flag Enforcement in Public APIs

**Problem:** `/api/recommend` checks if `recommenderConfig.enabled` but does NOT check if the shop's plan includes `aiRecommender`. A shop on the Free plan with a manually-enabled config could bypass billing.

**Solution:**
- **File:** `/app/routes/api.recommend.tsx`
  - After fetching `recommenderConfig`, also fetch the shop's subscription
  - Check `hasFeature(subscription.planId, "aiRecommender")`
  - Return 403 if plan doesn't include it
  
- **File:** `/app/routes/api.analytics-event.tsx`
  - Add a check: if event type is "recommend" or "convert", verify shop has analytics feature
  - Actually, analytics events should always be recorded (even on free plan) for upgrade CTAs. Keep as-is but add a comment explaining the decision.

---

## Phase 2: Admin UI Redesign (Priority: HIGH)

### Design Principles
- Every page uses Polaris components exclusively (no raw HTML for layout)
- Every list has pagination, search, and empty states
- Every action has loading states, success toasts, and error handling
- Consistent card structure: heading, description, content, actions
- Plan-gated features show upgrade CTAs (never hidden)
- Mobile-responsive (Polaris handles most of this)

### 2.1 Dashboard (`app._index.tsx`)

**Current state:** Good foundation — setup guide, stats cards, recent charts, plan info. ~654 lines.

**Improvements:**
1. **Trend indicators on stat cards** — Show percentage change vs previous period (e.g., "+12% from last month"). Requires adding a previous-period query in the loader.
2. **Better stat card design** — Add subtle icons (from `@shopify/polaris-icons`) to each stat card
3. **Recent activity feed** — Show last 5 actions (chart created, chart published, recommendation made) instead of just recent charts. Add a new query to AnalyticsEvent + SizeChart ordered by updatedAt.
4. **Quick actions row** — Prominent CTAs: "Create Chart", "Customize Widget", "View Analytics" as an InlineGrid of action cards below stats
5. **App health status** — Replace the "Enable app" section with a cleaner status indicator showing theme extension status, widget configuration, and active chart count
6. **Extract `StatCard` component** — Used in dashboard and analytics

**Loader changes:**
- Add `previousMonthlyViews` query for trend calculation
- Add recent activity query (last 5 analytics events with type = "recommend" or "convert")

### 2.2 Size Charts List (`app.size-charts.tsx`)

**Current state:** Functional with search, filters, template picker modal, CSV import. ~815 lines.

**Improvements:**
1. **Pagination** — Add cursor-based pagination. Currently loads ALL charts with `findMany` (no `take`/`skip`). For shops with 100+ charts this will be slow.
   - Add `page` and `pageSize` URL params
   - Default `pageSize: 25`
   - Use Polaris `Pagination` component at bottom of list
   - Update `findMany` to use `take` and `skip`

2. **Bulk actions** — Allow selecting multiple charts and performing bulk publish/unpublish/delete
   - Add checkbox state management
   - Add bulk action bar (Polaris `BulkActions` or manual implementation)
   - New action intents: "bulk-publish", "bulk-unpublish", "bulk-delete"

3. **Visual template picker** — Replace the text-based template grid with visual cards showing a mini preview of each template's data. Add category thumbnails/illustrations.

4. **Export functionality** — Add "Export as CSV" button per chart (not just the settings page import/export)

5. **Better empty state** — Use Polaris `EmptyState` with illustration and more compelling copy

6. **Chart count per category** — Show category counts in the filter dropdown

### 2.3 Chart Editor (`app.size-charts.$id.tsx`)

**Current state:** Full table editor with inline editing, column/row management, assignments tab. The `TableEditor` component is ~260 lines inline. ~800+ lines total.

**Improvements:**
1. **Extract TableEditor to component** — Move to `/app/components/TableEditor.tsx`

2. **Row drag-to-reorder** — Add drag handles to rows for reordering (implement with pointer events, no external library needed to stay under bundle size)

3. **Improved unit switching** — When switching between cm/in, offer to auto-convert existing values (with a confirmation dialog). Currently it just changes the label.

4. **Better assignment management** — Replace raw ID input with Shopify Resource Picker:
   - For "product" type: use `shopify.resourcePicker({ type: "product" })` via App Bridge
   - For "collection" type: use `shopify.resourcePicker({ type: "collection" })`
   - Show product/collection names instead of raw IDs
   - This requires fetching product names in the loader via GraphQL

5. **Chart metadata section** — Add fields for:
   - Internal notes/description
   - Display priority (for when multiple charts match a product)

6. **Unsaved changes warning** — Track dirty state and show a `ContextualSaveBar` (Polaris) when there are unsaved changes

7. **Keyboard shortcuts** — Tab through cells, Enter to move down, Escape to cancel edit

### 2.4 Widget Customizer (`app.widget.tsx`)

**Current state:** Good — display mode, trigger settings, colors, typography, text/messages, features, live preview. ~862 lines.

**Improvements:**
1. **Side-by-side layout** — Move preview to a sticky sidebar (Layout.Section + Layout.Section variant="oneThird") so merchants see changes live without scrolling

2. **Device size preview** — Add mobile/tablet/desktop toggle buttons above the preview that change the preview container width

3. **Font family selector** — Add font picker (system fonts: Inter, Roboto, Open Sans, etc.) since competitors offer this

4. **Animation options** — Slide-in, fade-in, none for popup appearance

5. **Better CSS sanitization** — Currently only blocks `@import` and `javascript:` URLs. Add: block `expression()`, `behavior:`, `moz-binding`, `</style>`, and any HTML tags. Move to the shared API middleware.

6. **Preview themes** — Show preview on light/dark product page backgrounds

### 2.5 AI Recommender (`app.recommender.tsx`)

**Current state:** Settings page with body type configuration, test interface. Gated by plan.

**Improvements:**
1. **Body type visualization** — Add simple SVG body silhouettes for each body type option (slim, regular, athletic, plus, petite). These can be inline SVGs to avoid extra network requests.

2. **Test interface improvements** — Show a visual representation of estimated measurements on a body outline. Display which measurement drove the recommendation (e.g., "Based primarily on chest measurement").

3. **Better onboarding** — Step-by-step activation flow:
   - Step 1: Enable recommender
   - Step 2: Select body types
   - Step 3: Choose algorithm (basic vs advanced, plan-gated)
   - Step 4: Test with sample data
   - Step 5: Done! Recommender is live.

4. **Accuracy feedback** — Show how many recommendations had high confidence (>70%) vs low confidence in the analytics period

### 2.6 Analytics (`app.analytics.tsx`)

**Current state:** Stats overview, bar chart (custom), conversion funnel, top charts, insights. ~628 lines. Plan-gated.

**Improvements:**
1. **Better bar chart** — The current BarChart component is functional but basic. Improve with:
   - Y-axis labels
   - Hover state with detailed tooltip
   - Smooth animation on data change
   - Grid lines for readability

2. **Line chart for trends** — Add a line chart alternative (SVG path-based) for daily trend visualization

3. **Export functionality** — "Export CSV" button that generates a CSV of the current view's data

4. **Date range improvements** — Add custom date range picker with calendar UI (or at minimum, start/end date inputs)

5. **Use `analytics.server.ts` helpers** — The loader currently inlines all its aggregation queries. The `lib/analytics.server.ts` file has `getAnalyticsSummary()`, `getConversionFunnel()`, `getTopCharts()`, `getDailyTrend()` helpers that are UNUSED. Refactor the loader to use them.

6. **Product-level analytics** — Add a "By Product" tab showing which products get the most chart views. Use the existing `getProductPerformance()` helper.

### 2.7 Pricing (`app.pricing.tsx`)

**Current state:** Plan cards, comparison table, billing actions. Well-structured.

**Improvements:**
1. **Annual/monthly toggle** — Add a prominent toggle showing savings ("Save 20% with annual")
2. **Current plan highlight** — Make the current plan card visually distinct with a badge
3. **Social proof** — Add "Most Popular" badge on the Growth plan
4. **FAQ section** — Common billing questions (can I downgrade? What happens to my charts?)
5. **Feature tooltips** — Hover on each feature row to see a brief explanation

### 2.8 Settings (`app.settings.tsx`)

**Current state:** App overview stats, export/import, widget reset, analytics reset, delete all charts. Basic structure.

**Improvements:**
1. **Organized sections** — Group settings into tabs or collapsible sections:
   - General (unit system, language preference)
   - Data Management (export, import, reset)
   - Integrations (Google Analytics ID, future: Klaviyo)
   - Danger Zone (delete all, reset analytics)
2. **Better export UX** — Download as file instead of showing JSON in the UI
3. **App status overview** — Show theme extension installation status
4. **Contact/support section** — In-app support link with pre-filled email

---

## Phase 3: Backend Hardening (Priority: HIGH)

### 3.1 Database Migration Path

**Current:** SQLite (`file:dev.sqlite` in schema.prisma). This is fine for development but will not scale.

**Action items:**
1. Add a `DATABASE_URL` environment variable check in `db.server.ts`
2. Create a PostgreSQL-compatible schema (SQLite and PostgreSQL Prisma schemas differ slightly)
3. Document the migration process in README:
   - Change `provider = "sqlite"` to `provider = "postgresql"`
   - Set `DATABASE_URL` env var
   - Run `npx prisma migrate deploy`
4. Add database indexes that are critical for query performance:
   - `AnalyticsEvent`: composite index on `[shop, eventType, createdAt]` (already exists partially)
   - `ChartAssignment`: the existing indexes are good
   - `SizeChart`: add index on `[shop, status, updatedAt]` for the list page sorted query

### 3.2 Pagination for List Endpoints

**Files to modify:**
- `app.size-charts.tsx` loader — Add `take`/`skip` with page params
- `app.analytics.tsx` loader — The rawEvents query fetches ALL view events in the date range. For shops with heavy traffic, this could be thousands of rows. Use `groupBy` with date bucketing instead of fetching all events and bucketing in JS.

### 3.3 Input Validation Improvements

**File:** `/app/routes/api.widget-config.tsx`
- CSS sanitization is incomplete. Currently blocks `@import` and `javascript:` URLs.
- Add: `expression(`, `behavior:`, `-moz-binding`, `</style>`, `<script`, `url(data:text/html`
- Move sanitization to a shared `sanitizeCss()` function in `lib/api.server.ts`

**File:** `/app/routes/api.analytics-event.tsx`
- The `CHART_ID_RE` regex (`/^[a-z0-9]{1,30}$/`) is too restrictive — CUIDs can contain lowercase letters and numbers but the pattern doesn't account for the exact CUID format. Widen to `/^[a-z0-9]{20,30}$/` or use a CUID validation library.

### 3.4 Error Logging Infrastructure

**Create:** `/app/lib/logger.server.ts`
- Structured JSON logging with levels (error, warn, info)
- Include shop, route, and request ID in every log entry
- In production: integrate with a service (Sentry, LogDNA, etc.) via environment variable
- Replace all `console.error()` calls across API routes

### 3.5 Analytics Query Optimization

**Problem:** The analytics loader fetches all raw events and buckets them in JavaScript. This is O(n) in the number of events.

**Solution:** Use Prisma's `groupBy` more aggressively. For daily trends, the current approach of fetching all events and bucketing in JS works for small datasets but will degrade. For PostgreSQL production:
- Use raw SQL with `DATE_TRUNC('day', "createdAt")` for bucketing
- Or keep the current approach but add a `LIMIT` to prevent runaway queries

---

## Phase 4: Theme Extension Polish (Priority: MEDIUM)

### 4.1 Widget Edge Cases

**File:** `/extensions/size-chart-widget/blocks/size-chart.liquid`

Review and ensure:
1. Handle missing product ID gracefully (don't break if placed on non-product pages)
2. Handle API errors with user-friendly messages (already partially done)
3. Handle slow API responses with a loading skeleton
4. Handle the case where no chart is assigned to the current product
5. Handle unit conversion correctly in the displayed table
6. Ensure keyboard navigation works for all interactive elements
7. Test with very large charts (20+ rows, 10+ columns)

### 4.2 Performance Optimization

1. Ensure JS bundle stays under 15KB gzipped
2. Lazy-load the recommendation form (only when user clicks "Find My Size")
3. Cache widget config in localStorage with 5-minute TTL (per CLAUDE.md rule 28)
4. Use `requestAnimationFrame` for any DOM updates
5. Minimize DOM queries — cache element references

### 4.3 Better Error States

1. Network failure: "Unable to load size chart. Please refresh the page."
2. No chart found: Silently hide the widget (don't show an error to the customer)
3. Recommendation failure: "We couldn't determine your size. Please refer to the chart above."
4. Timeout: Set a 5-second timeout on API calls and show appropriate message

---

## Critical Files for Implementation

These are the files that will see the most changes:

1. **`/app/routes/api.recommend.tsx`** — Consolidate AI algorithm, add rate limiting, add plan enforcement
2. **`/app/routes/app.size-charts.$id.tsx`** — Extract TableEditor/ChartPreview components, add resource picker, unsaved changes
3. **`/app/routes/app.analytics.tsx`** — Refactor to use analytics.server.ts helpers, improve charts, add export
4. **`/app/routes/app._index.tsx`** — Add trends, quick actions, activity feed
5. **`/app/routes/app.widget.tsx`** — Side-by-side layout, device preview, CSS sanitization

## New Files to Create

1. `/app/lib/api.server.ts` — Shared API utilities (CORS, validation, rate limiting)
2. `/app/lib/rate-limit.server.ts` — Rate limiting middleware
3. `/app/lib/logger.server.ts` — Structured logging
4. `/app/components/TableEditor.tsx` — Extracted table editor
5. `/app/components/ChartPreview.tsx` — Extracted chart preview
6. `/app/components/WidgetPreview.tsx` — Extracted widget preview
7. `/app/components/BarChart.tsx` — Extracted analytics chart
8. `/app/components/FunnelBar.tsx` — Extracted funnel visualization
9. `/app/components/StatCard.tsx` — Reusable stat card
10. `/app/components/ColorPickerField.tsx` — Extracted color picker
11. `/app/components/index.ts` — Barrel export

## Key Decision Points

1. **Rate limiting backend:** In-memory for now, document Redis upgrade path for production
2. **Chart library:** Stay with custom SVG-based charts (no external library) to keep bundle small and avoid dependency
3. **Resource Picker for assignments:** Requires App Bridge `shopify.resourcePicker()` — already available since app uses `useAppBridge()`
4. **PostgreSQL migration:** Document only — don't change the actual schema provider (that's a deployment decision)
5. **Component library approach:** Extract to separate files but keep in-app (no separate package)
# Size Chart & Recommender — Full Production Redesign Plan

## Context

The app has solid foundations (auth, schema, billing, APIs, theme extension) but needs a comprehensive polish pass to match top competitors like Kiwi Sizing, ESC Size Chart, and AVADA Size Chart. Key gaps: duplicate AI algorithms, no rate limiting, inline 600-800 line route files with no component extraction, analytics without visual charts, basic settings page, and missing production hardening. The goal is to make every page feel like a premium, production-grade Shopify app that a merchant would pay for — no second passes needed.

---

## Phase 1: Architecture Cleanup (Do First — Everything Depends on This)

### 1.1 Consolidate AI Recommendation Engine
- **Problem**: `app/routes/api.recommend.tsx` has a 200-line inline algorithm. `app/lib/ai.server.ts` has a BETTER unused algorithm (Euclidean distance, exponential confidence, 7 measurements vs 4).
- **Fix**: Refactor `api.recommend.tsx` to import and use `estimateMeasurements()` + `recommendSize()` from `lib/ai.server.ts`. Delete the inline algorithm. Map "average" body type to "regular".
- **Files**: `app/routes/api.recommend.tsx`, `app/lib/ai.server.ts`

### 1.2 Create Shared API Utilities
- **Problem**: All 4 API routes duplicate CORS headers, shop regex, JSON response helpers.
- **Fix**: Create `app/lib/api.server.ts` with:
  - `CORS_HEADERS` constant
  - `SHOP_RE` regex
  - `corsJson(data, status)` helper
  - `validateShop(shop)` helper
  - `corsOptions()` for preflight responses
- **Files**: New `app/lib/api.server.ts`, update all `api.*.tsx` routes

### 1.3 Add Rate Limiting
- **Fix**: Create `app/lib/rate-limit.server.ts` with in-memory sliding window (Map<string, {count, resetAt}>). Apply to all public API routes.
- Limits: `/api/recommend` 30/min, `/api/analytics-event` 300/min, `/api/size-chart` 120/min, `/api/widget-config` 60/min
- **Files**: New `app/lib/rate-limit.server.ts`, update all `api.*.tsx`

### 1.4 Enforce Plan Feature Flags in Public APIs
- **Problem**: `/api/recommend` doesn't check if shop has Growth/Pro plan — free plan shops can bypass billing.
- **Fix**: Fetch Subscription, call `hasFeature(planId, "aiRecommender")`, return 403 if not entitled.
- **Files**: `app/routes/api.recommend.tsx`

---

## Phase 2: Component Extraction

Extract reusable components from bloated route files BEFORE redesigning UI.

| Component | Source File | Purpose |
|-----------|-------------|---------|
| `components/StatCard.tsx` | Dashboard, Analytics | Metric card with label, value, optional trend |
| `components/ColorPickerField.tsx` | Widget customizer | Color input with hex display |
| `components/BarChart.tsx` | Analytics | Simple SVG bar chart |
| `components/FunnelBar.tsx` | Analytics | Conversion funnel visualization |
| `components/EmptyStateCard.tsx` | Multiple pages | Consistent empty state with CTA |
| `components/PlanGate.tsx` | Multiple pages | "Upgrade to unlock" banner |
| `components/TableEditor.tsx` | Chart editor | Inline-editable table with add/remove row/col |
| `components/WidgetPreview.tsx` | Widget customizer | Live widget preview iframe/mock |
| `components/index.ts` | — | Barrel exports |

**Files**: New `app/components/` directory with above files

## Phase 3: Admin UI Redesign (Page by Page)

### 3.1 Dashboard (`app/routes/app._index.tsx`)
**Current**: 655 lines, functional but basic stat cards and setup guide.
**Redesign**:
- Improve setup guide: progress bar with step circles, cleaner card layout, auto-dismiss completed steps
- Stat cards with trend indicators (compare to previous period — query previous 30 days in loader)
- Quick actions row: 3 visual action cards (Create Chart, Customize Widget, View Analytics) with icons
- Recent charts section with ResourceList pattern (status badge, assignment count, last updated)
- Plan status card in sidebar with usage bar and upgrade CTA
- App health section: widget active indicator, recommender status, chart count vs limit

### 3.2 Size Charts List (`app/routes/app.size-charts.tsx`)
**Current**: 816 lines, has search/filter/templates but loads ALL charts (no pagination).
**Redesign**:
- Add pagination (`take`/`skip` in loader, Polaris Pagination component)
- Bulk actions: select multiple charts, bulk publish/unpublish/delete
- Better template picker: visual cards with category badges instead of flat list
- Resource list with thumbnails showing chart category icon
- Sort options (name, date, status)
- Better plan limit display (usage bar with "X of Y charts used")
- Improved CSV import modal with drag-drop feel and better error display

### 3.3 Chart Editor (`app/routes/app.size-charts.$id.tsx`)
**Current**: 826+ lines, functional table editor inline.
**Redesign**:
- Extract `TableEditor` component with:
  - Inline cell editing (click to edit)
  - Add/remove rows and columns with buttons
  - Drag handle for row reordering (nice-to-have)
  - Unit system toggle (cm/inches) with auto-conversion
  - Column header editing
- Sidebar with chart metadata (name, category, status toggle)
- Tabs: "Chart Data" | "Assignments" | "Display Settings"
- Shopify Resource Picker for product/collection assignments (`shopify.resourcePicker()`)
- Contextual save bar for unsaved changes
- Live preview panel showing how the chart renders

### 3.4 Widget Customizer (`app/routes/app.widget.tsx`)
**Current**: 815+ lines, all settings stacked vertically.
**Redesign**:
- Side-by-side layout: settings left (2/3), live preview right (1/3) sticky
- Organized sections with Polaris Cards:
  - Display Mode (popup/inline/both) with visual selector cards
  - Trigger Style (button/link/tab) with icons
  - Colors section with organized picker grid
  - Typography & Spacing
  - Text Content (headings, messages)
  - Advanced (custom CSS with code editor feel)
- Device size toggle for preview (mobile/desktop)
- "Reset to defaults" button
- Preview updates in real-time as settings change

### 3.5 AI Recommender (`app/routes/app.recommender.tsx`)
**Current**: 815+ lines, functional but could be more intuitive.
**Redesign**:
- Activation card with big toggle and clear status
- Body type selector with visual silhouette icons (SVG)
- Step-by-step setup flow for first-time activation
- Improved test interface: show which measurements were compared, confidence breakdown
- "How it works" collapsible section explaining the algorithm
- Plan gate with clear upgrade path for Free/Starter users

### 3.6 Analytics Dashboard (`app/routes/app.analytics.tsx`)
**Current**: Has data aggregation but basic number display, no visual charts.
**Redesign**:
- Use `lib/analytics.server.ts` helpers (currently UNUSED — loader reimplements everything inline)
- Visual SVG bar chart for daily trend (BarChart component)
- Conversion funnel visualization (view > click > recommend > convert)
- Top charts leaderboard with horizontal bar indicators
- Date range selector: preset buttons (7d/30d/90d) + custom range
- Stats cards row with trend comparison
- Export button (CSV download)
- Plan gate for Free/Starter users with sample data preview

### 3.7 Pricing Page (`app/routes/app.pricing.tsx`)
**Current**: 815+ lines, has plan comparison but could be more compelling.
**Redesign**:
- Monthly/Annual toggle with savings badge ("Save 17%")
- "Most Popular" badge on Growth plan
- Feature comparison table with checkmarks/crosses
- Current plan indicator with "Your Plan" badge
- Trust indicators section (security, support hours)
- FAQ accordion at bottom

### 3.8 Settings Page (`app/routes/app.settings.tsx`)
**Current**: Basic with export and a few toggles.
**Redesign**:
- Organized sections with cards:
  - **General**: App status overview, theme extension instructions
  - **Data Management**: Export all data (JSON), import data, reset data (with confirmation)
  - **Default Settings**: Default measurement unit (cm/in), default chart category
  - **Notifications**: Email preferences for analytics reports (future)
  - **Danger Zone**: Delete all charts, disconnect app (with red styling and confirmations)
- Breadcrumb back to dashboard

## Phase 4: Backend Hardening

### 4.1 CSS Sanitization Improvement
- **Problem**: Widget config API only blocks `@import` and `javascript:` URLs.
- **Fix**: Also block `expression()`, `behavior:`, `-moz-binding`, `</style>`, `<script`, data URIs with scripts.
- **File**: `app/routes/api.widget-config.tsx`

### 4.2 Pagination for Lists
- Add `take`/`skip` params to size charts loader
- Add pagination to analytics top charts
- **Files**: `app/routes/app.size-charts.tsx`, `app/routes/app.analytics.tsx`

### 4.3 Database Index Additions
- Add index on `AnalyticsEvent.productId` for product performance queries
- **File**: `prisma/schema.prisma` + migration

### 4.4 Analytics Loader Refactor
- Replace inline analytics queries with `lib/analytics.server.ts` helpers
- **Files**: `app/routes/app.analytics.tsx`

---

## Phase 5: Theme Extension Polish

### 5.1 Edge Case Handling
- Handle missing product ID gracefully (non-product pages)
- Add loading skeleton in widget while chart loads
- Better error states with retry button
- **Files**: `extensions/size-chart-widget/blocks/size-chart.liquid`

### 5.2 Performance Verification
- Verify each block stays under 15KB gzipped individually
- Test with large charts (20+ rows)
- **Files**: `extensions/size-chart-widget/blocks/*.liquid`

## Execution Order (Optimized)

| Step | What | Files | Why This Order |
|------|------|-------|----------------|
| 1 | Shared API utilities | New `lib/api.server.ts` | Foundation for all API fixes |
| 2 | Rate limiting | New `lib/rate-limit.server.ts` | Security before any other changes |
| 3 | Consolidate AI algorithm | `api.recommend.tsx`, `lib/ai.server.ts` | Critical bug fix |
| 4 | Plan enforcement in APIs | `api.recommend.tsx` | Billing integrity |
| 5 | Component extraction | New `components/*.tsx` | Must exist before UI redesign |
| 6 | Dashboard redesign | `app._index.tsx` | First thing merchants see |
| 7 | Size Charts list redesign | `app.size-charts.tsx` | Core feature page |
| 8 | Chart Editor redesign | `app.size-charts.$id.tsx` | Core feature page |
| 9 | Widget Customizer redesign | `app.widget.tsx` | High-value page |
| 10 | Analytics redesign | `app.analytics.tsx` | Uses extracted BarChart + lib helpers |
| 11 | AI Recommender redesign | `app.recommender.tsx` | Premium feature page |
| 12 | Pricing redesign | `app.pricing.tsx` | Conversion page |
| 13 | Settings redesign | `app.settings.tsx` | Support page |
| 14 | CSS sanitization + DB index | `api.widget-config.tsx`, `schema.prisma` | Hardening |
| 15 | Theme extension polish | `blocks/*.liquid` | Final polish |
| 16 | Run `npm run typecheck` | — | Zero errors gate |

---

## Verification Plan

1. **TypeScript**: `npm run typecheck` — zero errors
2. **Lint**: `npm run lint` — zero warnings
3. **Build**: `npm run build` — successful production build
4. **Dev Test**: `npm run dev` — navigate every route, test:
   - Dashboard loads with stats and setup guide
   - Create chart from template, edit cells, add assignments
   - Widget customizer preview updates live
   - Analytics shows visual charts with date range filter
   - Recommender test interface returns sensible results
   - Pricing page shows correct plans and handles subscription
   - Settings page export works
   - API endpoints return proper CORS headers and rate limit
5. **Theme Extension**: Install on dev store, verify size chart popup and recommender form work on product page
6. **Security**: Verify rate limiting blocks excessive requests, plan gating blocks free users from premium APIs

---

## Key Constraints

- **Stack**: React Router 7 (NOT Remix), Polaris React components + `<s-*>` web components for nav, TypeScript strict
- **Auth**: `authenticate.admin(request)` at top of every `app.*` loader/action
- **Data isolation**: `where: { shop: session.shop }` on every query
- **No `any` types**, no `console.log`, no `// TODO`, no `// @ts-ignore`
- **Widget JS**: < 15KB gzipped per block
- **All existing functionality must be preserved** — this is enhancement, not rewrite
